 cāral nāṭa.  (kuṟun. 141)
paru - peru. cattāṉ - cettāṉ. alli - elli (iravu) mutaliya tiripukaḷil akaram ekaramākat tirivatāl, ataṉ nīḷa vaṭivamāṉa ākāram ēkāramākat tirivatum iyalpām.
e-ṭu:	cāṟu - cēṟu (tiruviḻā)
		cār - cēr
		cāral - cēral
		cāṇ - cēṇ (kaṉṉaṭam)
cāral nāṭaṉic cāral eṉṟatu ōr iṭavāku peyar. atu muṉṉarē vaḻakkiṟantu, piṉpu ataṉ tiripāṉa cēral eṉṉum vaṭivamē vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu, eṉak koḷka.
lakaramey yīṟu cila coṟkaḷil ṉakaramey yīṟākat tirital iyalpu.
e-ṭu:	mēla - mēṉa
		vel - veṉ (veṟṟi)
		kal - kaṉ (tōṇṭu)
		āl - āṉ (3ām vēṟṟumiyurupu).
im muṟippaṭi, cēral eṉpatu cēraṉ eṉat tirintatu. cēral eṉpatu aṉṉīṟu peṟiṉ cēralaṉ eṉṟākum. atu vaṭamoḻi yiṟ kērala(ṉ), kēraḷa(ṉ) eṉat tiriyum. kēralaṉ eṉpatu kēraṉ, kēlaṉ eṉat tokum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kēraṉ eṉpatik kālṭuvel aṟiñarum kēlaṉ eṉpati rā. irākaviyaṅ kārum cēraṉ eṉṉum peyarkku mūlamākak koṇṭaṉar. itu tali kālākak koṇṭa taṭumāṟṟam.
iṉi, cārtal eṉpataṟkuc cāytal eṉṟum, cār eṉpataṟkut tāḻvāram eṉṟum poruḷiruttalāl. maliccārali allatu aṭivārattik kuṟikkum cār eṉṉum collē cēr eṉat tirintu, vaḷḷal eṉpatu pōl allīṟu peṟṟuc cēral eṉṟāyatu eṉak koḷḷalum oṉṟu.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, varalāṟṟaṭippaṭiyilē moḻi kaḷi āytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum oruvaḻippōkkāṉa kakara cakara māṟṟamaṉṟi iruvaḻip pōkkāṉa kakara cakarap parimāṟṟamē uṇmiyākak koḷḷutaṟkuriya teṉṟum, cēraṉ eṉṉum kuṭavēntaṉ kuṭip peyar tūya teṉ collēyeṉṟum, kēraṉ, kēlaṉ, kēraḷaṉ eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil varupaviyellām cēral eṉpataṉ tiripēyeṉṟum, aiyantiripaṟa aṟintukoḷka.
cēraṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ mūlam allatu varalāṟu vēṟu vakiyākavum iruntirukkalām. iṟṟi aṟivu niliyil nām kāṭṭakkūṭiya mūlam cāral eṉpatē. uṇmi eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum. mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum tūya tamiḻk kuṭikaḷā yiruntatu pōṉṟē. ak kuṭippeyarkaḷum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷākavē iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpati maṭṭum uḷattil uṟutiyāy irut(tu)tal vēṇṭum. mēliyāriya moḻikaḷil kakaram cakaramāvatē iyalpu.
e-ṭu: birk - birch, Kirk - church, whilk - which - sik - such, bik bitch, caster - chester, particle - parcel, kenotopion - cenotaph.
iv vāriya moḻiyiyalpi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē, tamiḻilum avvāṟu kakaramē cakaramākumeṉak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. mēli moḻi nūlār, tamiḻ vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu vantateṉa aṭip paṭiyil avar tavaṟiṉatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. tamiḻ āriyat tiṟku muntiyatum mūlamumātaliṉ moḻinūl tiṟavukōl tamiḻi lēyē uḷḷatu. iti avar aṟiyār. aṟiyuṅkālam aṇuki varukiṉṟatu. (irā.pi. cētuppiḷḷi maṇiviḻā malar 1961)
kaccakkāyc cilli viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar: 
kaccakkāyic cilliyāl aṭittu āṭum āṭṭu kaccakkāyc cilli.
āṭuvār toki: 
palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi: 
āḷukkup pattiṟkuk kuṟiyāta pala kaccakkāykaḷum, akaṉṟa cilliyum, ōraṭi viṭṭamuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamum, iti āṭu karuvi yām. cillikku vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaṭṭattil cappātti eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam: 
poṭṭalil itu āṭap peṟum.
āṭumuṟi: 
āṭakar nāṟkacat toliviluḷḷa uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ tattam cilliyi eṟivar. yāruṭiyatu vaṭṭat tiṟku allatu vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviṟku mikaneruṅki yirukkiṉ ṟatō, avar muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. ēṉiyō rellām tattam aṇmi muṟippaṭi muṉ piṉṉāka āṭuvar.
āṭakarellārum tattam kaccakkāykaḷi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ iṭṭa piṉ, ovvoruvarum uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu, vaṭṭat tiṟkuḷ parappi allatu kuvittu vikkappaṭṭirukkum kaccak kāy kaḷit tattam cilliyāl aṭittal vēṇṭum. vaṭṭattiṟku veḷic ceṉṟa kāykaḷiyellām āṭuvōr eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.
cilli vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ cellāviṭiṉum, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ceṉṟum kāykaḷi aṭittu veḷiyēṟṟāviṭiṉum, kāykaḷi veḷiyēṟṟiyakkālum cilliyum uṭaṉ veḷiyēṟāviṭiṉum, tavaṟām. tavaṟiviṭiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum; tavaṟāviṭiṉ toṭarntu āṭalām.
iṟutiyil, mikutiyāṉa kāykaḷi vittiruppavar kelittavarāvar.
cēlam vaṭṭārattil kaccakkāykkup patilākac ciṟu curuṭṭup peṭṭiyiyum vittu āṭuvatuṇṭu. atu cikareṭpākuc cilli eṉappaṭum.  (ta.nā.vi.)

oṉṟik kaṇṭippāyc ceytu tīra vēṇṭuvatik kaṭṭāyam eṉpar. kaṭṭa vēṇṭiya āyam, kaṭṭāyam. kaṭṭutal celuttutal

kaṭṭuttāli
oru muṟi maṇakkappaṭṭavaḷi maṟumaṇañ ceytal ciṟappil lātatākak karutappaṭuvatāl, kaṭṭuppaṭṭavaḷ maṇattil maṇamuḻā (kaliyāṇa mēḷam) iṉṟiyē tāli kaṭṭappaṭuvatu muṇṭu. atu kaṭṭut tāliyeṉṟum maṇa muḻavu uḷḷatu koṭṭut tirumaṇam eṉṟum peyar peṟum kimpeṇ maṇam perumpālum kaṭṭut tāliyākavē yirukkum. kaṭavuḷ 
māntaṉ iyaṟṟa muṭiyāta iyaṟkiyi iyaṟṟiya oru talivaṉ iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟum avaṉ ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantavaṉ eṉṟum kaṇṭu allatu koṇṭu avaṉik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟaṉar mutaṟṟamiḻar.
(kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyarkku ellāvaṟṟiyum iyakkupavaṉ allatu celuttupavaṉ eṉṟum poruḷurikkalām. kaṭavutal - celuttutal) col. 33
kaṭavuḷ
ciṟu teyvam - immip payaṉiyē aḷippavaṉākak karutap peṟum iṭatteyvaṅkaḷum, pūtat teyvaṅkaḷum, naṭukal teyvaṅkaḷum.
peruntēvaṉ - immiyil ivvulaka iṉpattōṭu maṟumiyil vīṭṭiyum aruḷum iṟivaṉ. ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantu niṟkum kaṭavuḷ. (ti.ma.14.)
kaṭavuḷ uṇṭā?
kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpārum illi yeṉpārum, toṉṟu toṭṭu ulakil iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. uṇṭeṉpārē iṉṟum perumpālarēṉum, illi yeṉpār toki varavara vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu.
kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ
(1)	katiravak kuṭumpattic cērnta kōḷkaḷ ellām, iṭiyaṟātu ōr oḻuṅkāka iyaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
	ōr ūril ūrkāvalō araciyalāṭciyō ciṟitu nēram illāviṭiṉum, kalakamuṅ koḷḷiyum koliyum nērkiṉṟaṉa. uyiraṟṟa nāḷum kōḷum pāviyāṭṭup pōl oḻuṅkāka āṭivariṉ, avaṟṟi āṭṭum ōr āṟṟal iruttal vēṇṭum. av vāṟṟal aṟivaṟṟa tāyirukka muṭiyātu. av vaṟivē iṟivaṉ.
(2)	iv vulakam muḻuvataṟkum, katiravaṉ pakal viḷakkākavum tiṅkaḷ irā viḷakkākavum eṇṇiṟkum eṭṭāta kālattiliruntu viḷaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
	oru vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟi vippatu atiṟ kuṭiyirukkum makkaṭkē. makkaḷillā vīṭṭil viḷakkut tāṉākat tōṉṟi eriyātu. pala vulakaṅkaṭkum iru cuṭariyum viḷakkāka ēṟpaṭuttiyavaṉ oruvaṉ iruttal vēṇṭum.
	vēli ceyyāta tūkka vēḷiyākiya irākkālattiṟku, veppamāṉa neruppoḷi viḷakkākātu kuḷirnta niḻaloḷi viḷakkāka viruppatum, kavaṉikkattakkatu.
(3)	piṟa kōḷkaḷip pōṟ cuṟṟātu orē yiṭattilirukkum katiravaṉ, pattut ticiyum oḷi camamākap paravumāṟu uruṇṭiyā yiruppatum, aḷaviṭap paṭāta nīḷ peruṅ kālam erintu variṉum ataṉ eriyāvi kuṉṟi yaṇi yātiruppatum, iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa iṟumpūtuc ceyti yātalāl, ati yiyakki yāḷum oru param poruḷ iruttal vēṇṭum.
(4)	kōḷkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭātu taṉ taṉ pāti vaṭṭattil iyaṅkumāṟum, ivi cuḻaluṅkāl avaṟṟiṉ mēluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ nīṅkāvāṟum, ovvoṉṟiyuñ cūḻa oru kavarcci maṇṭalam amintiruppatum, iyaṟkikku appāṟ paṭṭa ōr āṟṟaliṉ amippē.
(5)	kālamum iṭamum toṭakkamum īṟum illātavi yātalāl, iṟṟi makkaḷulakan tōṉṟumuṉ, eṇṇikki yaṟṟa uyirulakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi yaḻintiruttal vēṇṭum. itit tāṉ,
	paṭittu viḷiyāṭum paṇpi ṉōṉum
	tuṭittut tuyartīr tōṟṟat tōṉum
	taṉṉil vēṟu tāṉoṉ ṟilōṉum
	aṉṉōṉ iṟiva ṉākumeṉ ṟurittaṉaṉ
	- maṇimēkalikku aṟivuṟuttiya civaṉiyat tarukki  (civavāti).
(6)	māntaṉ tōṉṟi aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭāyiṟṟeṉa vittuk koḷḷiṉum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tali muṟikaḷ kaḻintiruttal vēṇṭum. pattukkaṇakkākat toṭaṅkiya makkaṭ toki iṉṟu nūṟu kōṭik kaṇakkākap perukiyuḷḷatu. ovvoru tali muṟi yilum ettaṉiyarāyiṉum, attaṉiyarum aṭiyāḷaṅ kāṇumāṟu vevvēṟu mukavaṭiviluḷḷaṉar. ki variyum vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu aṟivu nirampiya oru pērāṟṟaliṉ ceyalē yākum.
(7) 	kaṭavuḷi nampiṉōr kiviṭap paṭār. eṉpatu, iṉṟum cilar vāḻkkiyil meyppikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(8) 	uṭalnalam, maṉanalam, matinalam mutaliya nalaṅkaḷ uḷḷārum illārum paṭikkappaṭṭiruttal.
(9)	pañcam, koḷḷinōy, peru veḷḷam, nila naṭukkam mutaliya iyaṟki aḻivu nikaḻccikaḷ nērtal.
kaṭavuḷ illi yeṉpataṟkuk kāṭṭappaṭum cāṉṟukaḷ
(1) 	kaṭavuḷ puṟak kaṇṇiṟkup pulaṉāvatilli.
(2) 	oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muraṇpaṭṭa palvēṟu mataṅkaḷ ulakil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
(3) 	nallōr palar, vaṟumi, nōy, piṟarāl tuṉpam mutaliya vaṟṟāl varuntik kuṟuvāḻkkiyarāyccāka, tīyōr palar ellā vakiyilum iṉpuṟṟu nīṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.
(4)	pala aḵṟiṇi uyiriṉaṅkaḷ piṟavaṟṟik koṉṟu tiṉpaṉavākavē paṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(5)	cilar ettuṇi urukkamāy iṟivaṉi vēṇṭiṉum, tām virumpiyatip peṟuvatilli.
iṅṅaṉam, kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpataṟkum illi yeṉpataṟkum kāṭṭappaṭum cāṉṟukaḷuḷ uṇṭeṉpataṟ kuriyaviyē mikuntum valimiyuḷḷaṉavākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. kāṭci yaḷavi pōṉṟē karuttaḷaviyum uṇmiyaṟiyum vaḻiyākum.
kaṭavuḷ eṅkum niṟintu āvi vaṭiviliruppatāl, avari oruvaṉum puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇa muṭiyātu. muraṇpaṭṭa mataṅkaḷ māntar paṭippu. nallōrkku maṟumiyil nal vāḻvirukkalām. pal piṟavi nampikkiyāḷar nallōr tuṉpattip paḻaviṉip payaṉ eṉpar.
māntaṉ matiyāṟṟal maṭṭiṭṭatātaliṉ, iṟivaṉ āṭci yiluḷḷa ellāvaṟṟiyum aṟiya muṭiyātu.
āḻa vamukki mukakkiṉum āḻkaṭalil
nāḻi mukavātu nāṉāḻi.  (mūturi. 19).
nallōrkku nērum tīṅkukaṭku, avar paḻampiṟappiṟceyta tīviṉikaḷik karaṇiyamākak kāṭṭuvar koṇ muṭipāḷar.
eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, iru cārārum tat tam koḷkiyi etirk koḷkiyār nampumāṟu nāṭṭamuṭiyā tiruppatāl, karuttu vēṟupāṭṭiṟ kiṭan tantu, oru cārāri yoru cārār paḻikkātum pakikkātumiruppatē uṇmiyāṉa pakut taṟivām.
mēlum, kaṭavuḷ uṇmiyāka illiyeṉiṉ, nampāta vaṉukkuk kēṭilli. orukāl, uḷḷāreṉiṉ avaṉukkuk kēṭuṇṭām. ātalāl, nampā matattiṉarum nampu matattik kaṭippiṭippatē cālac ciṟantatām.
kaṭākam
kaṭākam - kaṭāha
kaṭakam - kaṭākam = vaṭṭamāṉa koppari, peruveḷik kōḷaki. (va.va: 103)
kaṭi
kaṭi-kāt (khad) - i.vē. 
kaṭittal = pallāl veṭṭutal, kaṭittut tiṉṉutal. (va.va: 103)
kaṭiki1
kaṭiki1 - kaṭa, kaṭī, kaṭikā (gh, t)
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = kuḻi, āḻam. kuṇṭu - kuṇṭāṉ = kuḻinta allatu kuṇṭāṉa kalam. kuṇṭāṉ - kuṇṭā.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭiki - kuṭiki - kuṭukki - kuṭuvi.
kuṭiki = nīrkkalam (kamaṇṭalam).
arumpuṉaṟ kuṭiki mītu  (kantapu. kāviri. 49)
kuṭiki - kaṭiki = nīrkkalam, nāḻiki vaṭṭil, nāḻiki, maṅkala nāḻiki kuṟikkum kaṇiyaṉ, maṅkalap pāṭakaṉ.
kaṭiki - kaṭiñi. vaṭavar kāṭṭum kaṭa (gh) eṉṉum mūlam kuṭam eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripē.
kaṭiki + āram = kaṭikiyāram - kaṭikāram.
āram eṉpatu ōr īṟu.
o.nō: kūṭāram, koṭṭāram, vaṭṭāram.
kaṭīyantira, kaṭikā yantira eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ puṇarppiṉiṉṟu kaṭikāram eṉṉum teṉcol vantataṉṟu.
kaṭiki2 - 
kaṭa, kaṭā (gh, t) kuṭi = kulam, kūṭṭam. kuṭi - kuṭiki - kaṭiki = ciṟu kūṭṭam, ūravi.
iṅkuk ki eṉpatu ciṟumipporuṭ piṉṉoṭṭu (dim.)
vaṭavar kāṭṭum kaṭ (gh) eṉṉum mūlam toṭarpaṟṟa palvēṟu poruḷkoṇṭa col. kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil atu kuṭa eṉṉum teṉcoṟṟiripākum.
kul - kuḷ - kuḻu - kuḻa - kuṭa - kuṭam = tiraṭci. kuṭattal = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal. (va.va: 103-104)
kaṭicol
kaṭico lilli kālattup paṭiṉē  (ecca. 56)
eṉpatu oru tolkāppiyac cūttiram.
itaṟkuc cēṉāvariyar uri varumāṟu
itaṉ poruḷ: ivi toṉṟutoṭṭaṉa vallaṉaveṉṟu kaṭiyappaṭuñ collilli; avvak kālattut tōṉṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭu māyiṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: campu, caḷḷi, caṭṭi, camaḻppu eṉa varum. ivi toṉṟutoṭṭu vantaṉavāyiṉ. mutalākātaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ
cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai auveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē  (tol. eḻuttu. 62)
eṉa vilakkār āciriyar: ataṉāṉ avi piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya collēyāmeṉpatu.
iḵtu eḻuvaki vaḻuvamitiyuḷ oṉṟākātu ōr pātukāva lātaliṟ kiḷaviyākkat tiyipiṉmiyāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉā reṉpatu.
iṉi oru cārāruri: iṉṉa anuvatikkuṅ kālamā makkālattu, avi vaḻuvaṉmi ellā āciriyarkkum uṭampāṭākaliṉ, ataṉit taḻuvik koṇṭavāṟeṉka. ivi yiraṇṭum ic cūttirattukkup poruḷākak koḷka.
iṉi oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṟ putiyaṉa tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōlap paḻiyaṉa keṭuvaṉavum uḷaveṉak koḷka. avi aḻāṉ puḻāṉ mutaliyaṉavum, eḻuttiṟ puṇarnta coṟkaḷ ikkālattu vaḻaṅkātaṉavumām.
taṉṉuriyum maṉṉuriyumākac cēṉāvariyar kūṟiyīruri kaḷuḷ, muṉṉuriyē cūttirattiṟkup poruntiya uṇmi yuriyām.
campu caḷḷi caṭṭi camaḻppu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, tol kāppiyarāṟ koḷḷappaṭāviṭiṉum allatu tolkāppiyar kālattu vaḻaṅkavēyilli yeṉṟu (orucārār koḷkippaṭi) koḷḷiṉum. avi tūya teṉcoṟkaḷ eṉpati evarum maṟukkamuṭiyātu.
campu eṉpatu oruvakik kōri. camputal eṉpatu erital. campu - cāmpu - cāmpal. cāmpaṉ - cāmpāṉ = piṇattic cuṭupavaṉ.
avvak kālattil tōṉṟum putuc coṟkaḷit taḻuvik koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṉum vitiyik kūṟum vēṟoru tolkāppiyac cūttiram.
kuraṅkiṉ ēṟṟiṉik kaṭuvaṉ eṉṟalum
marampayil kūkiyik kōṭṭāṉ eṉṟalum
cevvāyk kiḷiyit tatti eṉṟalum
vevvāy verukiṉip pūci eṉṟalum
kutiriyuḷ āṇiṉic cēval eṉṟalum
 iruḷniṟap paṉṟiyi ēṉam eṉṟalum
 erumiyuḷ āṇiṉik kaṇṭi eṉṟalum
 muṭiya vanta avvaḻak kuṇmiyiṉ
 kaṭiya lākā kaṭaṉaṟin tōrkkē  (marapu. 69)
eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ veḷippaṭi itaṉ uriyil. pērāciriyar,
 kaṭaṉaṟintōr eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaḻakkiṉuñ ceyyuḷiṉum avi vantamiyiṟ kaṭappāṭaṟivōrkkuk kaṭiyalākāteṉṟa vāṟu. iṉṉum ipparikārattālē kōḻiyi vāraṇameṉṟa lum, verukiṉi viṭiyeṉṟalum pōlvaṉa palavuṅ koḷka. avi,
kāṉa vāraṇa mīṉuṅ
kāṭāki viḷiyu nāṭuṭi yōrē  (puṟam. 52)
vāraṇak koṭiyoṭu vayiṟpaṭa niṟīi  (tirumuruku. 20)
verukku viṭiyaṉṉa veruṇōkkuk kayantali  (puṟam. 324)
 eṉa varum, eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ic cūttirattilum itaṉuriyilum kāṭṭappeṟṟa coṟkaḷ yāvum tūya tamiḻ eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
kaṭuvaṉ = kaṭumiyāṉatu. kōṭṭāṉ = kōṭṭil vacippatu.
kōṭu = marakkiḷi. tatti = ili kāy kaṉiyoṭu tottik koṇṭu kiṭappatu.
tottutal = oṭṭutal. uṭumīṉ tottap polikaṉa kakkiri (kamparā. piramāt. 117)
tottutal = toṅkutal. ceccik kaṇṭat tottūṉ pōla (ñāṉā. pāyi. 5, 12)
 iv viru poruḷum tattip peyarkkup poruntum. tottu - totti - tatti.
pūci eṉpatu vīṭṭuppūṉi. pūcu - pūci - pūṉi - pūñi. nilattiṟ pūciṉāṟpōla metteṉṟu naṭappatu pūci. pūṉiyip pūcu pūcu eṉṟaḻippatu nelli vaḻakku.
cēval eṉpatu potuvāy āṇpaṟaviyik kuṟippiṉum, āṇ vilaṅkiyum kuṟippataṟkuc ciṟitum vilakkilli. cēval eṉpataṉ mūlamāṉa cē eṉṉuñ col vilaṅkiṉ āṇik kuṟittal kāṇka.
cē = kāḷi. cēṅkaṉṟu = kāḷaṅkaṉṟu. āṇkaṉṟu. cē - cēvu - cēval.
ēṉam = kariya vilaṅku. paṉṟi. irumi = karumi. iru - eru - eru mi - kariya māṭu. iru - iṉu - eṉu - ēṉu - ēṉam. ēṉu - ēṉi yāṉi. teluṅkil erumiyi eṉumu eṉṟum, yāṉiyi ēṉuka eṉṟum kūṟutal kāṇka. yāṉiyi ēṉiyeṉṟu iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭuk kuṭiyāṉavar kūṟuvar.
kaṇṭi = kaṭumiyāṉatu. kaṇṭam = kaṭumi, koṭumi. kaṇṭaṉ = koṭiyavaṉ. āṇerumi mikak koṭiyatu. ataṉ koṭu miyi iyaṟkiniliyiltāṉ kāṇamuṭiyum. puliyiṉum maṭaṅkaliṉum kāṭṭerumi koṭitā yiruttalpaṟṟiyē. atu kūṟṟuvaṉukku ūrtiyākak kūṟappaṭṭateṉka. erumimaṟam eṉṟu puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyum (7: 13).
oruva ṉoruvaṉi yuṭipaṭi pukkuk
kūḻi tāṅkiya erumiyum  (poruḷ. 72)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamum kūṟum puṟattuṟiyi nōkkuka.
vāraṇam = nilattiyum kuppiyiyum vārik kiḷippatu. kōḻi eṉṉum peyarum ip poruḷuṭiyatē.
viṭi = paruttatu. parutta āṇvilaṅku. viṭittal = paruttal. vilaṅkilum paṟaviyilum āṇ paruttiruttalik kāṇka.
iccoṟkaḷellām tūya teṉcoṟkaḷāyiruttalālēyē kaṭiya lākā kaṭaṉaṟin tōrkkē eṉṟār tolkāppiyar. itaṉāl, avvak kālattut tōṉṟum putupputu coṟkaḷel lām tūya teṉ coṟkaḷāyiruntāl, avaṟṟi vilakkutal kūṭātu eṉpatē. kaṭico lilli kālattup paṭiṉē eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ poruḷākum.
paḻiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum
vaḻuvala kāla vakiyi ṉāṉē  (naṉ. 452)
eṉṉum poruḷum ituvē.
ikkālattut tōṉṟik kaṭiyalākāc coṟkaḷ. maḷiki (palacarakkuk kaṭi). ceyalāḷaṉ, coṟpoḻivu, mānāṭu, tērvu (parīṭci) mutaliyaṉa.
māntar eṉṉum colliṉ orumi vaṭivam vaḻaṅkiya paṇṭi nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭamiyiṉ, cila pulavar māntaṉ eṉṉum colli ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟilar. palarpāṟ colliṉiṉṟu orumic colli vakuttuk koḷvataṟkup pulami vēṇṭuva tilli; pakuttaṟivē pōtiyatākum.
cilar pulavar. allatu pulavar cilar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya paṇṭi vaḻakku vīḻntu. iṉṟu cila pulavar eṉṉum vaḻakku eḻun tuḷḷatu. cila eṉṉum col aḵṟiṇi vaṭiviṉa teṉṉuṅ kāraṇattiṉāl, aṟiñar cilar ataṉi ēṟkiṉṟilar. āyiṉum, nalla, periya, vanta, pōṉa mutaliya akaravīṟṟuk kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷum terinilip peyareccaṅkaḷum pōla, cila pala mutaliya akaravīṟṟuk kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷum koḷḷap paṭuvataṟkut taṭi yilli yeṉka.
iviyum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavumē kaṭico lilli kālattup paṭiṉē eṉṉum cūttirattāl taḻuvappaṭumēyaṉṟi, `capāṣ, `calām eṉṟa coṟkaḷum ivipōṉṟa piṟavumalla eṉpatu, tamiḻarum tamiḻ māṇavarum, tamiḻp pulavarum kavaṉittaṟi vārāka.
capāṣ calām eṉṟa urutuc coṟkaḷ iṟṟikku 300, 400 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē ciṟanta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cila pulavar karutukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻil coṟkaḷ taḻuvap peṟutaṟku avaṟṟiṉ takutiyum tūymiyum kāraṇamākumēyaṉṟi, avi vaḻaṅkum 300, 400 āṇṭukkālam kāraṇamākātu. aṅṅaṉam kālam kāraṇamāyiṉ. 23 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil taḻuvappaṭumāṟu iṉṟu evarum ettuṇi ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyum tamiḻiṟ pukuttalāmē!
iṉi, capāṣ calām eṉṟa urutuc coṟkaḷit tamiḻiṟ pukuttiyavar pulavar perumakkaḷ (aruṇakirinātarum, kumara kurupararum) eṉṟu oru kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟit taḻuva virumpum tamiḻppulavar.
ittakiya iṭarppāṭṭu nilikaḷil, tamiḻ ciṟantatā, tamiḻil vīṇāka ayaṟcoṟkaḷip pukutti ataṉ tūymiyik kulikkum pulavar ciṟantavarā eṉṟu nōkkutal vēṇṭum. tamiḻppulavariṉum tamiḻ ciṟantatu eṉpati evarum maṟukkār.
mēlum, aruṇakirinātarum kumarakurupararum irunta kālam collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum illāta kālam. vaṭa moḻi uṇmiyil tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum, tamiḻ ataṉ kiḷi moḻiyeṉṟum, vaṭacoṟkaḷ kalappatu tamiḻukkup perumi eṉṟum karutappaṭṭa kālam; pala tuṟikaḷil pakuttaṟivic caṟṟum payaṉpaṭuttātu tamiḻaṉ taṉmāṉa maṟṟirunta kālam. it takiya niliyil vēṇṭātu pukuttap peṟṟa ayaṟcoṟkaḷi aḷaviyākak koḷvatu, tamiḻaṟiñarkkuc caṟṟum poruntātu.
emmoḻiyilāyiṉum oruporuṭku allatu karuttiṟkuc collirukkumpōtu, ati nekiḻaviṭṭu ataṟkīṭāka ayaṟ colli mēṟ koḷvatu aṟivuṭimiyākātu. tamiḻukkō iv varampu mikak kaṇṭippāṉatu.
ayaṟcoṟkaḷāl maṭṭumaṉṟi, ayaṉmoḻikaḷālum aḷa viṟantu nerukkuṇṭu. iruppatā iṟappatā eṉṉum niliyil tamiḻ tattaḷippatu ikkālam. ittakiya nerukkaṭiyāṉa niliyil, poṟuppu vāynta pulavar tamiḻiṉ tūymiyiyum niliyi yum kākkavēṇṭiyatu kaṭaṉ.
tamiḻukkuk kēṭāṉa pala ayalnāṭṭuk karuttukaḷ tamiḻariṉ uḷḷattiṟ kuṭikoḷḷat toṭaṅkuṅkāl, avaṟṟip peyart teṟivatu tamiḻp pulavar kaṭamiyāyirukka, aṅṅaṉam ceyyātu avaṟṟi mēṉmēlum vērūṉṟac ceyvatu avarkkut takumā? etu tamiḻ eṉṟum, eṅṅaṉam tamiḻi vaḷarttal vēṇ ṭum eṉṟum, aṟiyātu māṇavar iṭarppaṭum ikkālattil, tamiḻp pulavarē tamiḻit taḷarttaṟku aṭikōlvatu ettakiya kēṭāṉa ceyal.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmi muṉmi tāymi talimi mutaliya paṇpu kaḷi aṟitaṟkup piṟkālat tamiḻākiya purāṇat tamiḻum kal veṭṭut tamiḻum uṟutuṇi ceyyā; mēṟkaṇakku nūlkaḷi yum uḻavar tamiḻiyum nōkkutal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam nōk kātu maṉam pōṉavaḻi pēcuvatum eḻutuvatum. oruvaṉukkup pakivar avaṉ vīṭṭārē eṉṉum maṟi moḻiyiyē meyppikkiṉṟatu.
ētēṉum oru putukkaruttut tōṉṟiṉāl allatu koḷḷap paṭṭāl. ataṟku ēṟkeṉavē uḷḷa paḻañcolliṉiṉṟē putuccol tirittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ituvē tiruntiya moḻiyiṟ kiyāḷa vēṇṭiya neṟimuṟiyākum.
Radius eṉṉum paḻañ colliṉiṉṟu Radio eṉṉum putuccol, āṅkilattil tirikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
putiya mēlik kalikkaruttukaḷi yuṇarttattakka putuc coṟkaḷi ākkikkōṭaṟku vēṇṭiya karuvic coṟkaḷ, tamiḻil nirampavuḷa. putukkaruttukaḷi yuṇarttum putuccoṟ kaḷip puṉitaṟkē tamiḻ vaḷampeṟṟirukkumpōtu. paḻaṅ karuttukaḷi yuṇarttum paḻañcoṟkaḷit taḷḷiviṭṭu avaṟṟukkup patilāka ayaṟcoṟkaḷip pukuttit tamiḻiṉ tūymiyik kulittaṟkut tamiḻ eṅṅaṉam iṭantarum!
ākavē, capāṣ calām eṉṟa ayaṟcoṟkaḷāl tamiḻukkup peruntīṅkēyaṉṟic ciṟu naṉmiyumilli yeṉṟukoḷka. (centamiḻc celvi mē 1949)
kaṭimaram
 paṇṭit tamiḻaracar ovvoru marattik kaṭimaram allatu kāval maramākak koṇṭu atip pakivar aṇṭātapaṭi karuttāyk kāttu vantataṉālum, kā eṉṉum cōlip peyarkkuk kāttaṟporuḷum iruppatālum, ovvoru tiruppatiyilum kaṭavuḷ uruviṟku niḻal tantu niṟkum maram talamaram eṉap pōṟṟappaṭutaliṉālum, muṟkālattil makkaḷ aṭimutal muṭivari kiḻaṅkum pālum kīriyum pūvum kāyum kaṉiyum niḻalum uṟiyuḷumākap palavakip payaṉutavik kātta maraṅkaḷiyum cōlikaḷiyum teyvaṅkaḷāka vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉa ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu. (col. 19.)
kaṭu
kaṭu 1 - kaṭ (dd)
kaṭu = vaṉmi (kaṭiṉam), kaṭumi. kaṭṭeṉal = vaṉmiyā yiruttal. kaṭukkeṉal = vaṉmiyāyiruttal.
kaṭu2 - kaṭu (t)
 kaḷ = muḷ. kaḷḷi = muṭceṭi. kaḷ - kaṭu = muḷ (tivā).
 kārppu, kippu, mikuti, vemmi, koṭumi.
 kaṭu - kaṭi = kūrmi, mikuti, kārppu.
vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamilli. krut (veṭṭu) eṉṉum vaṭa col kattu (veṭṭu) eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟiripātaliṉ, mūlamākātu. (t.t.: 104)
kaṭuku
 kaṭuku - kaṭuka (t) - i.vē.
 kaṭu - kaṭuku = kāramuḷḷa poruḷ.
 kaṭuku ciṟuttālum kāram pōkātu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. kaṭuku - kaṭukam.
 ammīṟu peṟṟa vaṭivē īṟukeṭṭu vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷatu. (t.t.: 104)
kaṭikkaḻakam
kaṭikkaḻakak kālak kuṟunila maṉṉar
akuti, atti, atiyamāṉ takaṭūr porutu vīḻnta eḻiṉi, atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉañci, atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉañci makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉi, antuvañ cāttaṉ, antuvaṅkīraṉ, ampar kiḻāṉ aruvanti, aviyaṉ, ātaṉaḻici, ātaṉuṅkaṉ, ānti, āmūr mallaṉ, āy aṇṭiraṉ, iyakkaṉ, iruṅkōvēḷ, iḷaṅkaṇṭīrakkō, iḷaṅkumaṇaṉ, iḷaviccikkō, iḷa veḷimāṉ, īrntūr kiḻāṉ tōyaṉ māṟaṉ, eyiṉaṉ, eḻiṉi, ēṟṟi, ēṟikkōṉ, ēṉāti tirukkiḷḷi, olliyūr kiḻāṉ makaṉ peruñcāttaṉ, ōymāṉ nalliyak kōṭaṉ, ōymāṉ villiyātaṉ, kaṅkaṉ, kaṭṭi, kaṭiya neṭuvēṭṭuvaṉ, kaṇṭīrak kōpperu naḷḷi, kantaṉ (nāñciṟ porunaṉ), karumpaṉūr kiḻāṉ, kumaṇaṉ, koṇkāṉaṅ kiḻāṉ, ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, cōḻanāṭṭup piṭavūr kiḻār makaṉ peruñ cāttaṉ, cōḻiyavēṉāti tirukkuṭṭuvaṉ, tantumāṟaṉ, taruma puttiraṉ, taḻumpaṉ, tāmāṉ tōṉṟik kōṉ, teṉ paratavar, tērvaṇ maliyaṉ, toṇṭimāṉ, nampi neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ, naṉṉaṉ, naṉṉaṉ cēy naṉṉaṉ, nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉ, nālikiḻavaṉ 
nākaṉ, neṭuvēḷātaṉ, paḻiyaṉ, piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ, pulli, pūntuṟi (puṉṟuṟi), poṟiyāṟu kiḻāṉ, malli kiḻāṉ kāriyāti, maliyamāṉ cōḻiyavēṉāti tirukkaṇṇaṉ, maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri, mukkāval nāṭṭu āmūr mallaṉ, mutiyaṉ, mūvaṉ, miyaṟkōmāṉ māvaṉ, mōkūrp paḻiyaṉ, vallār kiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, valvil ōri, vāṭṭāṟṟeḻiṉi yātaṉ, viccikkōṉ, veḷimāṉ, vēṅki mārpaṉ, vēḷ evvi, vēḷ pāri, viyāvikkōp perum pēkaṉ mutaliyōr.
ivaruḷ, toṇṭimāṉ naṉṉaṉ mutaliya ciṟṟaracarum, vaḷḷal kaḷum muṭiyaṇintavarāvar. kaṭik kaḻakak kālattiṟpotuvāka mūvēntar kikaḷum tāḻntuviṭṭataṉāl, kuṟu nila maṉṉar taliyeṭuttu muṭiyaṇintu koṇṭaṉar. cēraṉ ceṅkaṭṭuvaṉ ēḻaracari veṉṟu, avar muṭikaḷi māliyāka aṇintirun tāṉ. avveḻuvaruḷ aivar kuṟunila maṉṉar.
vilkeḻu tāṉi vicciyar perumakaṉ
vēntaroṭu poruta ñāṉṟi  (kuṟun. 328)
eṉpatu, kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ vēntari etirttatik kūṟutal kāṇka .
kuṟunila maṉṉaruṭ peru vaḷḷalkaḷ
atiyamāṉ, āy, ōri, kāri, naḷḷi, pāri, pēkaṉ, kumaṇaṉ, nalliyakkōṭaṉ eṉpavar peruvaḷḷalkaḷ. ivaruḷ mutaleḻuvar ilakkiyap pukaḻ peṟṟavar. avaruḷḷum pāri perum pukaḻ peṟṟavaṉ. avveḻuvar koṭip poṟiyi yum avarukkuppiṉ nalliyakkōṭaṉ oruvaṉē tāṅkiṉā ṉeṉṟu, nallūr nattattaṉār pāṭuvar (ciṟupāṇ: 113 - 15). tam tampiyāl nāṭukoḷḷappaṭṭu kāṭu pōntirunta kumaṇaṉ, peruntaliccāttaṉārkkuk koṭukkat taṉṉiṭam poruḷiṉ miyāl, taṉ taliyi veṭṭikkoṇṭu pōyt taṉ tampi yiṭaṅ koṭuttup perum poruḷ peṟumāṟu taṉ vāḷik koṭuttu,
cātali ṉiṉṉāta tilli yiṉita tūum
īta liyiyāk kaṭi.  (230)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭāṉāṉ.
iṉi, evvi, naṉṉaṉ, paṇṇaṉ mutaliya vēṟupala vaḷḷalkaḷu miruntaṉar.
ōmpā vīki māvē ḷevvi  (puṟam. 24.)
icinal līkik kaḷiṟuvīcu vaṇmakiḻ
pārattut talivaṉ āra naṉṉaṉ  (akam. 152).
taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuri yāḷaṉ
paṇṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi  (akam. 54)
ivvaḷḷalkaḷ iṉṟēl, kaṭik kaḻakak kālattiṟ pulavar pāṇar kūttar porunar mutaliya iravalar vāḻntirukkavum, tamiḻum iciyum kūttum vaḷarntirukkavum, muṭiyāteṉṟē kūṟalām. māperuñ celvarāṉa mūvēntarum, pirāmaṇarkkut toṇṭun tāṉamuñ ceyvatilum, avar ēviya vēḷvikaḷi yellām iyaṟṟuvatilumē, kālattiyum kuṭikaḷ paṇatti yum celaviṭṭu, ēḻip pulavark kellām eṭṭāk kiyarākavē yiruntu vantaṉar.
āriyat tol katiñar, tamiḻ vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ talittiṟak koṭic ciṟappiyum poṉṟāp pukaḻiyum maṟittaṟ poruṭṭu, variyātu koṭuppavaṉ talivaḷḷal eṉṟum, kēṭkak koṭuppavaṉ iṭivaḷḷal eṉṟum, pukaḻak koṭup pavaṉ kaṭivaḷḷal eṉṟum, vaḷḷalkaḷi mūvakippaṭutti, cempiyaṉ mutaliya eḻuvar taliyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷum, akkuraṉ mutaliya eḻuvar iṭiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷum, āy mutaliya eḻuvar kaṭiyeḻuvaḷḷalkaḷum, āvar eṉṟu poruttamiṉṟi uṇmikku māṟāka vakuttuḷḷaṉar. irunta vūrkaḷiyel lām iravalarukkuk koṭutta āyum pāriyum, pulavaṉukkut taliyiyuṅ koṭutta kumaṇaṉiyum, otta koṭiyāḷikaḷ varalāṟṟiṟkut terintavari oruvarumilli.
cempiyaṉi āriyaṉeṉṟu karutik koṇṭu, avaṉit tali yeḻuvaḷḷalkaḷuḷ oruvaṉākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. avaṉ tamiḻa kattuc cōḻaruḷ oruvaṉ iṭiyeḻuvaḷḷalkaḷuḷ oruva ṉākak koṇṭa kaṉṉaṉip perumippaṭutta, koṭikkuk kaṉṉaṉ eṉṟum, kārttikik kappāl maḻiyu milli, karuṇaṉuk kappāl koṭiyumilli eṉṟum, kūṟuvar. `koṭukkaṉuk kappāl maḻiyumilli, kumaṇaṉuk kappāl koṭiyumilli. eṉṟē paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅkal vēṇṭum. koṭukkaṉ = tēḷ, naḷi (kārttiki) mātam. kaṭikkaḻakak kāla vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ tokiyi aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭē, ēṉikkālattār tokiyiyum ēḻēḻāka vakuttiruttal kāṇka.
aṇṭiraṉ eṉpatu kaṇṭīraṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟa ōr iyaṟpeyar. tamiḻakattiṉ teṉkōṭiyiluḷḷa potiyamali nāṭṭi āṇṭa āy aṇṭiraṉ teluṅkaṉallaṉ. aṇṭiraṉ eṉṉuñ coṟku āntiraṉ eṉṉuñ colloṭu toṭarpilli.
vaṭapāṟ kuṟunila maṉṉar
erumiyūraṉ
iṟṟi micūr nāṭṭiṉ paḻampeyar erumi nāṭu eṉpatu. erumikaḷ mikkiruntataṉāl annāṭu appeyar peṟṟatu. ataṉ talinakar erumiyūr. atilirun tāṇṭavaṉ erumiyūraṉ. taliyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻi yaṉāl vellappaṭṭa eḻuvaruḷ erumiyūraṉum oruvaṉ.
koycuvaṟ puravik koṭittērc ceḻiyaṉ
ālaṅ kāṉat takaṉṟali civappac
cēral cempiyaṉ ciṉaṅkeḻu titiyaṉ
pōrval yāṉip polampūṇ eḻiṉi
nārari naṟaviṉ erumiyūraṉ
tēṅkamaḻ akalattup pularnta cāntiṉ
iruṅkō vēṇmāṉ iyaltērp porunaṉ eṉ
ṟeḻuvar nalvalam aṭaṅka orupakal
muracoṭu veṇkuṭi akappaṭut turicelak
koṉṟukaḷam vēṭṭa  (akam. 36)
eṉṟu nakkīrar pāṭutal kāṇka.
nērā vaṉtōḷ vaṭukar perumakaṉ
pērici erumi naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷati  (akam. 253)
eṉpatil, erumi eṉpatu vaṭukar talivaṉ oruvaṉiṉ peyarik kuṟittatēyaṉṟi, avaṉi erumi nāṭṭoṭu toṭarpu paṭuttiya taṉṟu.
erumikku vaṭamoḻiyil makicam (mahiṣa) eṉṟu peyar. ataṉāl erumiyūr eṉpatip pirāmaṇar makicapuri eṉa moḻi peyarttaṉar. accol ataṉ āṅkila vaṭivi yoṭṭi iṉṟu micūr eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
māvā rātē (273) eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭip pāṭiya erumi veḷiyaṉār eṉṉum pulavar, erumiyūriṉarāvar. iruḷ kiḻip patupōl eṉṉum 72-ām akap pāṭṭip pāṭiya kaṭalaṉār ivar makaṉār āvar.
naṉṉaṉ
erumi nāṭṭiṟkum kuṭamalikkum mēṟkiluḷḷa teṉ kaṉṉaṭappakuti, koṇkāṉam eṉṉum peyarkoṇṭa tākum. koṇku tuṟimukam allatu kaṭaṟkari. koṇkaṉ neytal nilattalivaṉ. koṇkāṉam kaṭaṟkari nilamātalāl ap peyar peṟṟatu pōlum!
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil koṇkāṉatti āṇṭavaṉ naṉṉaṉ. avaṉ talinakar kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil eṉpar. ēḻiṟ kuṉṟam eṉṉum perumaliyiyum; pāḻi, pāram, viyalūr, pirampu eṉṉum pērūrkaḷiyum, uṭiyatu koṇkāṉam. avvūr kaḷuḷ pāḻi oru vallaraṇ nakar. atu ēḻil maliyiṉ eḻu kuvaṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa pāḻiyi araṇākak koṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. naṉṉaṉ oru koṭiyāḷiyākavum iruntāṉ.
poṉpaṭu koṇkāṉa naṉṉaṉ naṉṉāṭ
ṭēḻiṟ kuṉṟam peṟiṉum  (naṟ. 391).
icinal līkik kaḷiṟuvīcu vaṇmakiḻ
pārattut talivaṉ āra naṉṉaṉ
ēḻil neṭuvarip pāḻic cilampil   (akam. 152).
cūḻi yāṉic cuṭarppūṇ naṉṉaṉ
pāḻi aṉṉa kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉakar  (akam. 15).
naṟavumakiḻ irukki naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ
vayali vēli viyalū raṉṉa 	 (akam. 97).
naṉṉaṉ vēḷir marapic cērntavaṉ eṉpatu, vēṇmāṉ eṉṉum peyarālum,
naṉṉaṉ utiyaṉ aruṅkaṭip pāḻit
toṉmutir vēḷir ōmpiṉar vitta
poṉ 	(akam. 258)
eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. pāḻip poṉ kavara aṭikkaṭi pōr nikaḻntataṉāl annakar ceruppāḻi eṉap peyar peṟṟatu pōlum!
ēḻilmali eṉpatip pirāmaṇar catta cilam (sapta cila) eṉa moḻipeyarttaṉar. piṉṉar ēḻilmali eṉpatu elimali eṉat tirintapōtu, ēḻilmali nāṭṭi avar mūcikanāṭu eṉṟaṉar. mūcikam (mūṣika) eṉpatu eliyik kuṟikkum vaṭacol. pāḻiccilampu eṉpatu pāḻikkal eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. atu iṉṟu pāṭkal eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. viyalūr eṉpatu, iṉṟu kaṉṉaṭamoḻi yiyalpiṟkēṟpap peyilūr (Bailur) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
koṇkāṉattiṉ kaṭalōra vaṭapakuti tuḷunāṭu eṉappaṭum. aṅkut tōkikkā eṉṟa ūruḷḷatu. mayilkaḷ niṟinta cōliyiṉāl avvūr appeyar peṟṟatu.
..................... paṟikkaṇ pīlit
tōkik kāviṉ tuḷu nāṭ ṭaṉṉa  (akam. 15).
pāḻic cilampum mayilukkup peyar peṟṟateṉpatu,
ēḻil neṭuvarip pāḻic cilampiṟ
kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa  (akam. 152)
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. tōkikkā eṉpatu tōkkā eṉa maruvi, iṉṟu cōkku (jōk) eṉat tirintuḷḷatākap pērā. auvi. cu. turiccāmip piḷḷiyār kūṟuvār.
koṇkāṉam eṉpatu kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē koṅ kaṇam eṉat tirintu viṭṭatu. kokkeṉṟu niṉittaṉiyō koṅkaṇavā? eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar maṉiviyār kūṟiyatākak katiyuṇmiyum, koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅ karunāṭar eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟuvatiyum (25: 156), kāṇka.
veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āy eyiṉaṉ
koṇkāṉattiṉ teṉkīḻp pakuti puṉṉāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. ati āṇṭa veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉar marapil vantavaṉ veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āy eyiṉaṉ. avaṉ talinakar vāki.
 vaṇki eyiṉaṉ vāki yaṉṉa   (puṟam. 351).
 yāma viraviṉ neṭuṅkaṭi niṉṟu
 tēmutir cimiyak kuṉṟam pāṭum
 nuṇkō lakavunar vēṇṭiṉ veṇkōṭ
 ṭaṇṇal yāṉi yīyum vaṇmakiḻ
 veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āay eyiṉaṉ  (akam. 208)
eṉpataṉāl, avaṉ koṭittiṟam viḷaṅkum.
patiṟṟuppattiṉ 2-ām patikam, imayavarampaṉ neṭuñ cēralātaṉi, utiyañ cēraṟku veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāḷ nalliṉi īṉṟa makaṉ eṉkiṉṟatu. avvēṇmāḷ veḷiyaṉ marapiṉaḷ pōlum.
piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ
koṇkāṉattiṟkuk kiḻakkil, kuṭamalit toṭariluḷḷa kuvaṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟu kutiri mali. ammalināṭṭi yāṇṭavaṉ piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ.
aruvi yārkkuṅ kaḻipayil naṉantalik
kaṟivaḷa raṭukkattu malarnta kāntaṭ
koḻuṅkiḻaṅku miḷirak kiṇṭik kiḷiyoṭu
kaṭuṅkaṭ kēḻa luḻuta pūmi
naṉṉāḷ varupata nōkkik kuṟavar
uḻāatu vittiya parūukkuraṟ ciṟutiṉi
muntuviḷi yāṇar nāṭputi tuṇmār
mariyāṉ kaṟanta nurikoḷ tīmpāl
māṉṟaṭi puḻukkiya pulavunāṟu kuḻici
vāṉkē ḻirumpuṭi kaḻāa tēṟṟic
cānta viṟakiṉ uvitta puṉkam
kūtaḷaṅ kaviṉiya kuḷavi muṉṟiṟ
ceḻuṅkōḷ vāḻi yakalilip pakukkum
ūrāk kutirik kiḻava kūrvēl
naṟinārt toṭutta vēṅkiyaṅ kaṇṇi
vaṭinavil ampiṉ villōr peruma
kivaḷ ḷīkik kaṭumāṉ koṟṟa
viyaka varippil tamiḻakaṅ kēṭpap
poyyāc cennā neḷiya ēttip
pāṭupa eṉpa paricilar nāḷum
īyā maṉṉar nāṇa
vīyātu parantaniṉ vaciyilvāṉ pukaḻē.  (puṟam. 168),
eṉṉum karuvūrk kantap piḷḷi cāttaṉār pāṭṭum
oḷitikaḻ muttam viṟaliyark kīttu
nārpiḻik koṇṭa veṅkaṭ ṭēṟal
paṇṇami nalyāḻp pāṇkaṭum parutti  (puṟam. 170)
 eṉṉum uṟiyūr maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār pāṭṭum,
iṉṟu celiṉun tarumē ciṟuvari

piṟarkkum aṉṉa aṟattaki yaṉṉē   (mēṟpaṭi 170)
eṉṉum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār pāṭṭum, kutirimalik kuṟavar vāḻkkiyiyum koṟṟaṉ koṭit tiṟattiyum teḷiyak kāṭṭum.
kutirimalip pakutiyiluḷḷa vaṭakari mēṟkari eṉṉum iṭappeyarkaḷ, iṉṟu paṭakarā markkarā eṉṟu urumāṟi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kutirimali nāṭṭut talinakarp peyarāṉa koṟṟaṉ karuvūr eṉpatu, iṉṟum kotta kaṉavūr eṉṟu vaḻaṅki varuvatākap pērā. auvi. cu.tu. kūṟuvar.
iruṅkōvēḷ
koṇkāṉattiṉ vaṭapāliruntatu kaṭampariṉ paṅkaḷanāṭu. avviru nāṭukaṭkum kiḻakkil kuṭamalit toṭariṉ kīḻpāl iruntatu vēḷiratu vēṇāṭu. annāṭṭut tuvari nakarit talinakarākak koṇṭu iruṅkōvēḷ āṇṭu vantāṉ.
uvarā vīkit tuvari yāṇṭu
nāṟpat toṉpatu vaḻimuṟi vanta
vēḷiruḷ vēḷē  (puṟam 201)
 eṉṟu kapilar pāṭutal kāṇka.
tuvari (JthurK¤âu«) iṉṟu erumiyūr nāṭṭic cērntatu.
vēmpāyp (Bombay) pitiram (piratēcam) muḻutum muṉ kālattil vēṇāṭā yiruntatu. muṉṉar nicām ariyattic cērntirunta ellōrā (Ellora), paḻiya paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil (cāsaṉaṅkaḷil) vēḷūr eṉṟum vēḷūrakam eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēmpāyp pitirattuc cōḻāpuram (Sholapur) māvaṭṭattil oru nakar vēḷāpuram (Velapur) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭuḷḷatu. akamatu nakar vaṭṭattil vēḷāpuram eṉṟum, pūṉā māvaṭṭattil vēḷakam eṉṟum, pala nakaraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. appitirattic cērnta vēḷakam (vēḷ kirāmam (?)) eṉṉum māvaṭṭat talinakar pelkām (Belgum) eṉṟum, vēḷpaṭṭi eṉṉum ūr pēlhūṭṭi (Bel hutti) eṉṟum, vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. oycaḷa maṉṉariṉ paṭṭappeyarāṉa pellāḷa eṉpatu vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripākavē karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
toṭakkak kālattil, vēṇāṭu marāṭṭiya nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi kūrccarattil paraviyiruntatu. kattiyavār kaccup (Cutch) pitiraṅkaḷil, iṉṟum palavūrkaḷ vēḷā eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa pēlā (Bela) eṉṉum peyar koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. akattiyar tuvārapatip pōntu patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷiruḷ ḷiṭṭārik koṇṭuvantār eṉṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟum, vētakkālattil marāṭamum kuccaramum pañca tiraviṭattuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭatum, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.
piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya caḷukkiyar vēṇāṭṭi āṇṭata ṉālēyē, vēḷpula varacar caḷukku vēntar. eṉṟu tivākaramum piṅkalamum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.

eṉṟu kūṟatal kāṇka.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭut talimipūṇṭa cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukku, aḻumpilvēḷ, villavaṉ kōti eṉṉum iru tamiḻar muṟiyē, amiccaṉum, paṭit talivaṉumā yiruntaṉar. uḻavu, kittoḻil, vāṇikam, aracu, kalvi mutaliya pala toḻilum, tamiḻarālēyē (tamiḻil) naṭattappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ākiyāl, pārppaṉar tuṇi aṟṟit tamiḻarkkuc ciṟitum vēṇṭiyatāyilli.
āvum āṉiyaṟ pārppaṉa mākkaḷum
peṇṭirum piṇiyuṭi yīrum pēṇit

eṉṉum aṭikaḷ, pārppaṉar maṟamum (vīramum) valimiyum aṟṟavar eṉṟum, kaḷaṅkamaṟṟavareṉṟum iraṅkattakka vareṉṟum, cilarāl karutappaṭṭatik kuṟikkumēyaṉṟit tūyareṉṟu ellārālum karutappaṭṭatākak kuṟiyātu. pārppaṉar tūyareṉṟu ellārālum karutappaṭṭiruppiṉ. māṇikkavācakari arimarttaṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ ciṟiyiṭṭuk kaṭuntaṇṭam ceytirukkamāṭṭāṉ. 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, vaṭamaliyappap piḷḷiyiṉ kīḻatikāriyāl nārāyaṇa tīṭci tarum ciṟi ceyyappaṭṭirukkamāṭṭār.
āṉmuli yaṟutta aṟaṉi lōrkkum
māṇiḻi makaḷir karuccitit tōrkkum
pārppārt tappiya koṭumi yōrkkum
vaḻuvāy maruṅkiṟ kaḻuvāyu muḷa   (puṟam. 34: 1-4)
eṉavarum aṭikaḷum mēṟkūṟiya karuttiyē terivikkum. pārppārt tappiṉavarkkum kaḻuvāyuḷatu eṉṟu kūṟiyirut talik kavaṉikka. muraṭṭuk kuṇamiṉṟi amitiyāyirukkum ellā uyirp poruḷkaḷiyum tamiḻar orutiṟamā yeṇṇik kāppar. pūṉiyiyum kaḻutiyiyum kolvatu peruntīviṉi eṉṟu karutappaṭuvati nōkkuka. pārppaṉar akkālattil mikac ciṟiya tokiyiṉarāyum, eḷiyavarāyum, āṭciyil iṭam peṟātavarāyum iruntatiṉāl, tammik kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa cāṉṟōrākak kāṭṭik koṇṭaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ palvēḷvi(yāka)c cāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti yiṉ ceyalālum, palyāṉic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ ceyalā lum, āriya vēḷvikaḷi vēṭkum vaḻakkam kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē tamiḻnāṭṭil vērūṉṟiviṭṭati yaṟiyalām.
pālik kutamaṉār eṉṉum pārppaṉar, palyāṉic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ utaviyāl, tāmum tam maṉiviyum tuṟakkam (cuvarkkam) pukavēṇṭumeṉṟu, oṉpatu peruvēḷvi vēṭṭup pattām vēḷviyil tam maṉiviyuṭaṉ maṟintu pōṉār eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattuc ceyti ārāyattakkatu.

villuṭi vippiṉ viyaṉkāṭ ṭiyaviṉ  (perumpāṇ: 67 - 82).

kaṭāram (parmā) maliyā mutaliya kīḻināṭukaṭkum cumaturi cāli (cāvakam) mutaliya kīḻittīvukaṭkum, nīrvāṇikar ceṉṟu vaṇikam ceytu vantaṉar. avar ikkālattu nāṭṭuk kōṭṭic ceṭṭimār pōl taṅkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷi iṅkēyē viṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉar. ivvaḻakkam ki.mu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉarē iruntateṉpatu,
munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūuvō ṭilli.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (980) aṟiyappaṭum.
kīḻkariyilirunta koṟki toṇṭi pukār mutaliya tuṟi nakaraṅkaḷuḷ, pukār mikap peritākavum ulakilēyē taliciṟanta tākavum iruntatu. atu kāvirik kayavāyil aminta aḻakiya tuṟi nakaramātalāl kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉṟum, avvāṟu kaṭaliṟpukum iṭattiliruntataṉāl pukār eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟatu. tuṟi nakarip paṭṭiṉam eṉpatu paṇṭi vaḻakku. pati - pataṉam - pattaṉam - paṭṭaṉam (paṭṭaṇam) -paṭṭiṉam.

vaḷaṅkeḻu muciṟi   (akam. 149).
kaṭikkaḻakat toṭakkam
(ki.mu. 5 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
maturi mānakar amikkappaṭṭavuṭaṉ, mūṉṟām pulavar kaḻaka mum tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu. nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar uṟuppiṉa rāyiṉar. mukkaḻakattilum uṟuppiṉar tokikku variyaṟi illi. avvakkālattil taliyāya pulavar allatu ciṟanta pulavar ettaṉivarō attaṉivarum iṭam peṟṟaṉar. putitāka yārēṉum ciṟanta pulavar variṉum cērkkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉiyē, takunta pulavar variṉ kaḻakappalaki tāṉē oru muḻam nīṇṭu iṭantarum, eṉṉum marapuri kuṟikkum. nāṟpat toṉpatu eṉṉun toki ēḻē ḻeṉṟu vakukkappaṭuvatāl, moḻip paṟṟum mūppum pulamittiṟamum nōkkātu, matam paṟṟiyō kulam paṟṟiyō nilam paṟṟiyō ēḻeḻuvar tokukkap paṭṭaṉar eṉak karutaṟka. ettaṉivar variṉum ēṟṟup pōṟṟaṟ kēṟṟa celvam pāṇṭiyaṉiṭattiruntatu. mukkaḻakattilum pulavar tokiyi oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇil muṭittatu, ōriṭatti iṟivaṉukku otukkavēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ koḷki paṟṟiyē.
 ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cinti yuḷḷum
 uṟivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭali lāyntavoṇṭīntamiḻ
eṉṟu māṇikka vācakar pāṭiyatu ikkaruttup paṟṟiyē. akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum kaṭik kaḻaka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷāyiruntaṉa.
iṭik kaḻakattiṟkum kaṭikkaḻakattiṟkum īrāyirattin nūṟāṇṭiṟku mēl iṭiyīṭupaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, iṭik kaḻakat tiṟutip pāṇṭiyaṉāṉa muṭattiru māṟaṉē kaṭikkaḻaka mutaṟ pāṇṭiyaṉāṉāṉ eṉpatu poruntātu. kaḻaka irukkiyi iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri uttara maturi yeṉṟu kūṟiyatu, teṉmaturi yoṭu oppunōkkiyeṉ ṟaṟika.
kaḻakattiṟkut toki yeṉṟum kūṭal eṉṟum peyarkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ataṉpiṉ, iṭavaṉāku peyarāka, maturiyum kūṭaleṉap paṭṭu, māṭac ciṟappāl māṭakkūṭal, nāṉmāṭak kūṭal eṉṉum vaḻakkeḻuntatu. māṭaṅkaḷ mukilpaṭiyumāṟu vāṉaḷāva vuyarntiruntataṉāl, nāṉ mukiṉ māṭak kūṭal eṉṟum pulavar pukaḻntu pāṭiṉar. attoṭarit tolkati yāḷar payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭu, maturimēṟ kaṭumaḻi poḻinta nāṉmukilkaḷi nāṉmāṭaṅkaḷ kūṭittaṭuttaṉa veṉṟu kati puṉintuviṭṭaṉar.
maturik kōṭṭi vāyil oṉṟaṉ muṉ, oru mūtāla maram paṭarntōṅkiyiruntataṉāl, avviṭam ālavāy eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntirukkalām. appeyariyum poruḷ tirittu, pāmpiṉāl elli kāṭṭappaṭṭaviṭameṉṟu kati kaṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. ālavāy eṉpatu mutalil nāṉmāṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ peyarākavē yiruntatu. nāṉmāṭap peyarkaḷi, avi tiruvālavāy tirunaḷḷāṟu tirumuṭaṅki tirunaṭuvūr. iṉik kaṉṉikariyamāl kāḷi ālavāy eṉṟumām eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka. (kali. 92 uri).
kaṭikkaḻaka muṭivu
ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ukkirap peruvaḻuti kālattil, pāṇṭiya varacum kaṭik kaḻakamum kulintaṉa.
10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa paṭṭiṉattu aṭikaḷ kaḻakattik kuṟikkum caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭacollum periya caṅkik kuṟikkum caṅkam eṉṉum teṉcollum vaṭivottirup patāl, caṅkip payaṉpaṭuttum kālam nōkki ati mūṉṟākki,
mutaṟcaṅ kamutūṭṭum moykuḻalā rāci
naṭuccaṅkam nalvilaṅku pūṭṭum  kaṭiccaṅkam
āmpō tatuvūtum ammaṭṭō immaṭṭō
nāmpūmi vāḻnta nalam.  (potu)
eṉṉum veṇpāvil, paṇṭit tamiḻk kaḻakam mūṉṟeṉ patik kuṟippāka vuṇarttutal kāṇka.
nalvilaṅku pūṭṭum eṉṟatu tirumaṇattaṉṟu ūtappaṭṭati. akkālattil maṅkala viṉikkum caṅkūtappaṭṭatu. āmpōtu eṉṟatu iṟantapiṉ ūtappaṭum vēḷiyi. mūviṭattum caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭivē amiyumāṟu, mutaṟcaṅkam pālūṭṭum eṉṟu pāṭiyirukkalām. āyiṉ, pālūṭṭuvatu ciṟu caṅkātalāl, atic caṅku eṉṟu kuṟittār.

kāṇ eṉṉuñ col āṅkilattilum ataṉ iṉa moḻi kaḷilum kaṉ (cun), kāṉ (con), keṉ (ken) ke aṉ (can) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. kīṉ (keen) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatākavum kīṟṟu (Skeat) kūṟṟiṉiṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
kāṇutal eṉṉuñ col, tamiḻil, puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇutali yum akak kaṇṇāṟa kāṇutaliyum, kuṟikkum. muṉṉatu pārttal; piṉṉatu aṟital, tiyūttāṉiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇ eṉpataṉ tiricoṟkaḷellām piṉṉatiyē uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
Cun: AS. cunnan, to know, ME. cunnen, to know, ON. cunna, to know, Ice. kunna, to know, Sw. kunna, to know, Dan. kunde, to know, M Goth. kunnan, to know.
ceyvāṉ (ceyya) eṉṉum etirkāla viṉiyeccattiṉ (muṟṟeccattiṉ) vāṉ īṟu maliyāḷattil āṉ īṟākat tiriyum. atuvē aṉ eṉṟu kuṟukiyum piṉṉar eṉ eṉṟu tirintum paḻiya āṅkilattil nikaḻkāla viṉiyecca (Infinite) īṟāka vaḻaṅkiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. āṅkila iṉa moḻikaḷil avviraṇṭum a, e eṉak kaṭikkuṟintuḷḷaṉa.
E cunning, 1. Knowing, skilful, ME. cunning, conning, Northern form cunnand, Ice. kunnandi.
2. Knowledge. knowledge, skill. ME. conninge, Ice. cunnandi, AS cunnung, temptation, trial.
E. con, A secondary verb, formed from AS cunnan, to know; it signifies accordingly ‘to try to know’ and may be regarded as the desiderative of ‘to know’ - Skeal’s. Etymological Dictionary of the English Language, p. 126. con, 1. to learn, learn to know, study. 2. to commit to memory. - The Random House Dictionary, p. 303. To con (the passage) (Shakespeare) = to learn by heart.
E. ken, to know. Every man kens (knows) best where his own shoe pinches, (prov). ME kennen, to make known, see, know, discern. OE cennen to make known, declare, Ice. kenna, to know, Sw. kanna, Dan. kjende, Du. kennen, G. kennen.
E. can, to know, to know how to, to be able.
G., Ice., Goth. kann.
Cf. AS cunnan, to know, to know how to do, to be able.
Ice. kunna, to know, to be able.
Sw. kunna, to know, to be able.
Dan. kunde to know, to be able.
mutaṟkaṇ, kāṉ (con, kon) eṉṉum tiyūttāṉiyac col mutaleḻuttuyir muṉ piṉṉāka muṟimāṟi kṉā (cna, kna) eṉṟum kṉō eṉṟum tirintatu.
AS cnawan, Ice. kna, ON. kna, ME. knowen, E. Know, OHG. chnaan, cnahan.
kakaram cila moḻikaḷil ‘ga’ eṉṉum eṭuppoliyāka (voiced sound) māṟukiṉṟatu.
L. gna: gnarus, knowing, having knowledge of, acquainted with; O. Irish gnath, known, accustomed, W. gnawd, a custom.
kirēkkattil iv veṭuppolik kakaram (ga) iraṭṭikkiṉṟatu.
Gk. gignnosko.
camaṟkirutattil eṭuppolik kakaram (ga) jakaramākat tirikiṉṟatu. Skt. jna (jñā). g - j, na - ña, pōlittiripu.
va. jñāṉa - in. jāṉ.
jakaram cila moḻikaḷil sakara eṭuppoliyāka (za) melintu viṭukiṉṟatu.
Russ, znate, to know; Pers. far-zan, knowledge.
iv vellāt tiripukaṭkum mūla vēr GEN (keṉ) eṉak kuṟikkiṉṟār kīṟṟu (Skeat).
mēliyar iṉṉum moḻikaḷic cariyāy ārāyavilli. iṟṟi aṟiviyalkaḷellām mēliyar kaṇṭaviyē. āyiṉ, moḻikaḷ varalāṟṟiṟku muntiya paṇṭik kālattaṉa vātalāl, avarkaḷiṉ ārāyccikkuṭpaṭṭataṉṟu. poṟik karuvikaḷ et tuṇic ciṟantaṉavāyiṉum māntaṉ kiyāḷāviṭiṉ iyaṅkā; iyakkiya piṉpum iṟutivari māntaṉ uṭaṉiruttal vēṇṭum. tuliyuṅ kaṇippāṉum keṭṭiruppiṉ tavaṟāṉa niṟiyuṅ kaṇakkuṅ kāṭṭum. tavaṟāṉa vaḻiyik kiyāḷiṉ ev viṉi yum veṟṟiyāy muṭiyātu.
pala aṭippaṭit tamiḻc coṟkaḷ tiyūttāṉiyattil tamiḻukku neruṅkiyum, ilattīṉil caṟṟut tirintum, kirēkkattil atiṉum mikat tirintum, vētamoḻiyilum camaṟkirutattilum mikamikat tirintum, uḷḷaṉa.

valliṉa meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ, eṭuppilā (voiceless) volikaḷē eṭuppolikaḷiṉum muntiyavi. tamiḻil taṉi allatu iyaṟki yeṭuppolikaḷ illi. melliṉa mey yoṭu kūṭiya ceyaṟki yeṭuppolikaḷē yuḷa. tamiḻ eṭup pilā volikaḷ tiraviṭattilēyē eṭuppolikaḷākat tirintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

 ātalāl, āriyat tiripip paṟṟic colla vēṇṭiyatilli.
kāṇ eṉpataṉ tiri coṟkaḷ, ilattīṉa kirēkkattil eṭup poliyoṭum tiyūttāṉiyattil eṭuppilā voliyoṭum iruttal, muṉṉavaṟṟiṉum piṉṉavi tamiḻukku nerukka māyiruttali yuṇarttutal kāṇka.
kālṭuvelār, tolkāppiyamum pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭut tokikaḷum tamiḻarkkum teriyātu maṟintu kiṭanta kālattil, vaḻi kāṭṭiyumiṉṟit taṉṉan taṉimiyāy ārāynta pōtum, tamiḻiṉ toṉmi muṉmikaḷiyum mūlat taṉmi yiyum uṇarntu, tam tiraviṭa oppiyalilakkaṇa nūliṉ iṟuti yil, colliyal uṟavukaḷ (Glossarial Affinities) eṉṉum pakuti yil, kīḻ varumāṟu varintuḷḷār.
“kan, the eye; kan (in the preterite kandu), to see; also secondarily, to mark, to consider, to think. In the latter sense it comes kannu in Tamil, but the base remins unchanged. In (kanu, kannu) Telugu, the ordinary n, he nasal of the dental row, is used instead of n, the cerebral nasal. Comp. the Welsh ceniaw, to see; English ken, view, power of reach of vision, to ken, to know by sight. In Webster’s “English - Dictionary” kanna was sid to be `an eye’ in Sanskrit; whereas it is exclusively a Dravidian word. This error  may be compared with Klaproth’s representing kuruta, blind, as a Sanskrit word, instead of referring it to the Dravidian languages, to which alone it belongs. There is a curious word in Sanskrit kana, one-eyed, which seems to have some Dravidian relationship. It becomes in Bengali kana, blind which, in form at least, is identical with the Dravidian negative kana, that sees not. Possibly the Dravidian kan, to see, kannu, to consider, may have some ulterior connection with the Gothic kunn-an, to know: Greek gno-ni; Sans. na; Latin gna (gnarus); Old High German chann. The different shades of meaning which are attributed to Greek to gno-ni and eide-ni; seem to corroborate this supposition; for the latter is represented as meaning to know by reflection, to know absolutely, whereas the former means to perceive, to mark, and may therefore have an ulterior connection with the Dravidian root.”  (Caldwell’s Dravidian Comparative Grammar, University Edition, p. 591.)
tamiḻ vaḷarcci taṭukkappaṭṭirunta kaṭanta mūvāyiramāṇṭuk kālattil, āriyap pūcārikaḷāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. jñā eṉṉun tiri colliṉiṉṟu piṉvarumāṟu ērāḷamāṉa coṟ kaḷi ākkik koṇṭaṉar.

ivi oru parukki allatu ōraricip patam. ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟu ēṉic coṟperukkattiyum uyttuṇarntu koḷka. iṅṅaṉam vaṭamoḻiyi vaḷarttuk koḷḷutaṟku ūṇuṭi mutaliya vāḻkkip poruḷkaḷellām variyātu vaḻaṅkiya vaḷḷalkaḷ, mēliyāriyat tiripākiya vētamoḻiyiyum ataṉoṭu tamiḻic cērttu vaḷartta camaṟkirutattiyum tēva moḻiyeṉṟum, ataṉip pōṟṟuvatāl maṇṇulakamē vīṭṭulakamāy māṟi viṭumeṉṟum, kuruṭṭuttaṉamāy nampiya pēti mūvēntarē.
tamiḻakattiṟku vētamoḻiyō camaṟkirutamō eḷḷaḷavun tēviyilli. āyiṉum camaṟkirutatti vaḷarttatu. tamiḻi vīḻttaṟkum nāḷaṭivil ataṉi oḻittaṟkumē eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. tamiḻakam civa matamum tirumāl matamum tōṉṟiya nāṭāyiruntum, iṉṉum tirukkōyil vaḻipāṭu vaṭamoḻiyilēyē naṭipeṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟiruppatu, tamiḻariṉ maṭamiyiyum aṭimit taṉattiyumē valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭum.
karaṇam allatu kāṭci yeṉṉum mūlaccol ciṟavātu, ñāṉam eṉṉum ataṉ tiricollē ciṟantu tōṉṟuvatu, uṇmiyāṉa oḷikkalliṉum pōlikkallē polintu tōṉṟuvatu pōlum! eṉiṉum, camaṟkirutattiṟku ñāṉattit tantatu tamiḻē eṉṉum pukaḻ nilittu niṟkum.  (ce.ce.)

kaṇṇāmpotti viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar: oruvar oru piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottik koṇṭirukkumpōtu, piṟa piḷḷikaḷ ōṭi oḷiyum viḷiyāṭṭu kaṇṇāmpotti eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi: mutiyār oruvar, āṭa virumpum piḷḷikaḷi yellām oruṅkē iruttikkoṇṭu, ovvoruvariyum cuṭṭi oru maraput toṭaric colli, at toṭariṉ iṟutic collāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottuvar.  iṉi, mutiyār oruvar mītu ellāp piḷḷikaḷum paṭapaṭaveṉṟu kiyālaṭikkumpōtu, ammutiyavariṉ kiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottuvatu muṇṭu. pottum pōtu, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷellām ōṭi oḷintu koḷvar.
mutiyār akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottik koṇṭirukkumpōtu, avviruvarukkum piṉ varumāṟu viḷiyāṭṭu nikaḻum.
mutiyavar: kaṇṇām pottiyārē, kaṇṇām pottiyārē1
piḷḷi: eṉṉa?
mu: ettaṉi muṭṭiyiṭṭāy?
pi: mūṉṟu muṭṭiyiṭṭēṉ
mu: avaṟṟuḷ oru muṭṭiyip porittut tiṉṟuviṭṭu, oru muṭṭiyip puḷitta taṇṇīrukkuḷ pōṭṭuviṭṭu, oru muṭṭiyip piṭittuk koṇṭuvā.
pulāluṇṇātārāyiṉ, ivvuriyāṭṭil viḷikkup piṟpaṭṭa viṉā, viṭi, ēval piṉvarumāṟirukkum.
mu: ettaṉi paḻam paṟittāy?
pi: mūṉṟu paḻam paṟittēṉ.
mu:avaṟṟuḷ oru paḻattip piḷḷiyārukkup paṭittu viṭṭu, oru paḻatti aṟuttut tiṉṟuviṭṭu, oru paḻattip piṭittuk koṇṭuvā.

nalla muṭṭi koṇṭuvā
eṉṟu ēvuṅ koṅkunāṭṭu vaḻakkum uḷatu.
iṅṅaṉam ēvappaṭṭa piḷḷi, uṭaṉē ōṭippōy aṅku miṅkum pārttu, oḷintu koṇṭirukkum piḷḷikaḷuḷ oruvarit toṭamuyalum. mutalāvatu toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷi aṭuttamuṟi kaṇ pottappaṭum. toṭappaṭumuṉ ōṭivantu mutiyārit toṭṭuviṭṭāl, piṉpu toṭutal kūṭātu. oru varum toṭappaṭāviṭiṉ, muṉpu kaṇpottiya piḷḷiyē maṟumuṟiyuṅ kaṇpottappaṭum.

kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar iyalpu

kantaṉ

kantu = tūṇ, paṟṟukkōṭu. kantu - kantaṉ = tūṇiṟ poṟik kappaṭṭavaṉ allatu paṟṟukkōṭāṉavaṉ, murukaṉ. (va.va: 108)
kantu
kantu: kummutal - tiraḷutal, kum - kam - kantu = tiraṇṭa tūṇ.
o. neh.: um - untu. kantu - skanta (va.). (ti.ma: 739)
kantukam
kantukam - kantuka
kaḷ - kaṇṭu = nūṟpantu. kaṇṭu - kantu - kantukam = pantu. iṉi, kum - kam - kantu - kantukam eṉṟumām. (va.va: 168)
kanti
kanti - kantā (nt)
kattu - kantu - kantal, kanti. kontu - kontal - kantal (?) (va.va: 108)
kappari
kappari - karppara
koppari - kappari. (va.va: 108)
kam
kam - ka (nīr)
am - kam = nīr. (va.va: 108)
kampalam
kampalam - kampala
kampalam - kampaḷam - kampaḷi (va.va: 108)
kampa viḷiyāṭṭu
nālvar, oru maṇṭapattiṉ allatu catura iṭattiṉ nāṉku mūlik kampattilum, kampattiṟkoruvarāka niṉṟukoṇṭu, etirum valamum allatu etirum iṭamumāka, māṟi māṟi allatu cuṟṟiccuṟṟi vēṟuvēṟu kampattiṟku iyaṅkik koṇṭirukka, iṉṉoruvar avarit toṭalvēṇṭum. muṉṉarē ētēṉu moruvakiyil tōṟṟavar allatu tavaṟiyavar toṭa vēṇṭiyavarāvar.
oruvar kampattiviṭṭu vilakiyirukkumpōtum, ēṟkaṉavē āḷuḷḷa iṉṉoru kampattic cērntirukkumpōtum, avarit toṭalām. toṭappaṭṭavar toṭṭavar viṉiyi yum, toṭṭavar toṭappaṭṭavar viṉiyiyum, mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
toṭukiṟavar, pāl mōr tayir viṟpavarpōl, pālō pāl! allatu mōrō mōr! allatu tayirō tayir! eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu tirivatu, cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakkam, uccantaliyik kiyāl taṭṭikkoṇṭu toṇṭāṉ toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟu collit tirivatu, pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakkam ākum. pāṇṭināṭṭil iv viḷiyāṭṭiṟkut toṇṭāṉ toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟē peyar.
tīṭṭuḷḷa allatu tīṇṭappaṭāta oruvar tammiṉiṉṟu vilaki yōṭiya piṟari, viḷiyāṭṭiṟkō kuṟumpiṟkō toṭṭa ceyali, naṭittuk kāṭṭuvatākavuḷḷatu iv viḷiyāṭṭu.
karaṇam
tirumaṇattiṟkuriya oppanta allatu tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅku karaṇam eṉappaṭum. karaṇam ceyki. atu āṭci paṟṟi caṭaṅki uṇarttiṟṟu. karaṇattōṭu kūṭiya tirumaṇatti vatuvi maṇam eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakku. (ta.ti. muṉ V)
maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷit taṉ niliyāṉa vāḻkkit tuṇi yākak koḷvatāka, palaraṟiyak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ cūḷ (āṇi) iṭuvatē karaṇamām. (ta.ti. muṉ V)

karuppum kaṟuppum
karumi kuṟitta colliṉ karuppu, kaṟuppu eṉṉum iruvaṭivu kaḷuḷ. muṉṉatē muṉṉatām. im muṭiviṟku ētukkaḷ mūṉṟu, aviyāvaṉa:
 1.	kaḷ eṉum vērccolliṉiṉṟu karu eṉṉum vaṭivē muntit tōṉṟal.
 kaḷ - kar - karu - kaṟu.
 o.nō: kuḷ - kur - kuru - kuṟu:
 muḷ - mur - muru - muṟu (vaḷi)
teḷ - teṟu. veḷ - veṟu, eṉpavaṟṟil ṟakaravaṭivu nēraṭiyākat tōṉṟiyiruppiṉum, avi rakara vaṭivākiya iṭinili yillātaṉa. kaṟu eṉpatō av iṭiniliyi uṭiyatu. rakarattiṉ vaṉmiyē ṟakaramātalāl, rakaramē muntiyatām. neṭuṅkaṇakkilum rakaram muṉṉum ṟakaram piṉṉum vikkap paṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
oḷir - oḷiṟu, muri - muṟi (vaḷi) eṉpaṉavum ṟakarattiṉ piṉmiyik kāṭṭum.

 3.	ṟakaraṅ koṇṭa vaṭivu vaḻipporuṭkē ēṟṟami. iṟṟit tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ mutalatāṉa tolkāppiyattil, kaṟuppum civappum vekuḷip poruḷa (855) eṉṟu, kaṟuppu eṉṉum col ciṉapporuḷik kuṟippatākavē kūṟuppaṭṭuḷḷatu. kaṟuttavarum civantavarumāka iruvaki niṟattār tamiḻakattut toṉṟutoṭṭuḷar. civappu eṉṟatu poṉṉiṟattiyum cempoṉṉiṟattiyum, ciṉattiṉāl, kariyar mukam mikak karukkum: civappar mukam mikaccivakkum: kaṇ iruniṟattārk kum civakkum.
 ceṟuvar nōkkiya kaṇtaṉ
 ciṟuvaṉi nōkkiyuñ civappā ṉāvē  (puṟam: 100)
 eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭiyik kāṇka.
poruḷ māṟumpōtu colvaṭivam māṟavēṇṭumeṉpatu collākka neṟimuṟiyātalāl. karuppu eṉṉum col ciṉattik kuṟikkum pōtu kaṟuppu eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
vaciyunark kaṟutta pakivar  (patiṟṟup. 32: 15)
kaṟuttōr = pakivar.
kaṟuttō ruṟumuraṇ tāṅkiya.  (patiṟṟup. 66: 9)
kaṟu - kaṟuvu = nīḷciṉam, maṉavayiram.
kaṟuvi vekuṇṭurippāṉ (tirikaṭu. 46)
kaṟuttal = maṅkic ciṟitu karuttal. e.ṭu: kaṟukkaṇ veḷḷi.
kaṟuppu = kaṟi, kuṟṟam, taḻumpu, karumēkanōy.
kaṟu - kaṟi = karutta kaḷaṅkam. kaṟimiṭaṟu (puṟam. 1:5)
kaṟi = kaḷaṅkam, mācu, kuṟṟam, uṟintu karutta arattam.
kaṟuppuk kaṭṭutal = payirmutirntu iruṇṭa pacciniṟaṅ koḷḷutal.
kaṟuttal = muṟṟutal. keḷavi kaṟuppa (maturik. 371)
kaṟuppuveṟṟili = iruṇṭa pacci veṟṟili.
 pacci, nīlam, karuppu mūṉṟum ōriṉa niṟaṅkaḷātalāl, payir muṟṟi iruṇṭapacumi yaṭital kaṟuttal eṉappaṭṭatu.
 iṅṅaṉam vaḻipporuḷkaṭkē kaṟuppu eṉṉum vaṭivam uriyatām. āyiṉum, 10ām allatu 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa piṅkalantiyil kaṟuppu eṉṉuñ coṟkuk karumip poruḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyattil atu am mutaṟporuḷilum vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
kamparāmāyaṇam, nakar nīṅku paṭalam 58ām ceyyuḷil, kaṟuttāy eṉṉum vaṭivam, poṟuttāy, iṟuttāy, veṟut tāy eṉṉum ēṉiyaṭi mutaṟcīrkaṭku etukiyāka vantatākak karutalām. atē vaṉappil, ilaṅki kāṇpaṭalam. 47ām ceyyuḷil, nāḷ eṉṉum col, yāḻ, vāḻ, pāḻ eṉpaṉavaṟ ṟiṟku etukiyāka nāḻ eṉṟu vantiruttal kāṇka. ōr kaṟuppu millāta eṉṉum paṭikkācup pulavar pāṭṭilum. ār kaṟuppaṉ pērkaṟuppaṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiluḷḷa ṟakaram etuki paṟṟiyatē.
potuvāka, piṟkālattilakkiyattil coṟkaḷ ciṟappup poruḷ karutātu mōṉiyetukit toṭi yoṉṟēpaṟṟi āḷap paṭṭuḷa, ūṭal, pulavi, tuṉi eṉṉum mūṉṟum mūvēṟu niliyik kuṟippaṉavāyiṉum, orē poruḷil āḷappaṭṭirut talik kāṇka.
ātalāl, kaṟukaṟuttal (mikunta karuniṟamaṭital, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam, 325. uri), kaṟutta kākkaṭṭāṉ (karuṅkākkaṇam, yāḻppāṇa akarāti), kaṟuttakār (kuṟuvi nelvaki), kaṟutta vaṉ (karuniṟamuṭiyavaṉ) kaṟuttavuppu, kaṟuppaṉ (kariyavaṉ), kaṟuppuk kaṭṭutal, kaṟuppuk kaṉṉimār, kaṟuppuk kāñcoṟi, kaṟuppuk kuṅkiliyam, kaṟuppuk koḷ, kaṟupput tāmar, (oru vaki maram). kaṟupputtēyili, kaṟuppuppayaṟu, kaṟuppuppuḷḷi, kaṟuppuppūlā, kaṟuppu maṭṭivāy, kaṟuppu maṇittakkāḷi, kaṟuppuvarāl, kaṟuppu vavvāl, kaṟuppu vīram (viḷakkukkari) mutaliya piṟkāla ilakkiya vaḻakkum akara mutali vaḻakkum koḷḷattakkaṉavalla.
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyil, varaccūli, varaṭci, varaṭcuṇṭi, varaṭcūli, varaṭcoṟi, varaṭṭaṭip pāṉ, varaṭṭi, varaṭṭutal, varaṭṭu, varaṭṭuc cōki, varaṭṭup pacu, varaḷ, varaḷvāyu eṉa ṟakaram varavēṇṭiya coṟkaḷiyel lām oḻuṅkāy rakaramiṭṭuk kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya akaramutalikaḷip piṉpaṟṟātu. tūya karuniṟattik kuṟikkum collellām rakarattik koṇṭaviyeṉṟum, ciṉam, kaṟi, mutircci mutaliya vaḻipporuḷik kuṟippaṉavellām ṟakarattik ko ṇṭaviyeṉṟum, terintukoḷka. mara vayiram karuttum civantum irukkumātalāl, karuttatik karuppu eṉṟum civantatic cēku eṉṟum collal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, karuppu eṉṉum col pañcam eṉṉum poruṭkēyuriya tākac cilar karutuvar. karuppu iruḷiyum, iruḷ tuṉpattiyum nāṭṭut tuṉpaṅkaḷuṭ koṭiya pañcattiyum kuṟikkum. ataṉālēyē. attamikkum pōtil eṉṉum kāḷamēkam pāṭṭuc coṟṟoṭarkku. pañcakālattil eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam aṇivakip poruḷkaḷ ellā niṟap peyarkaṭkumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu:
karuppu = pēy, vayiram, cārāyam.
veḷḷi = veḷutta āṭi, cuṇṇāmpu, veḷḷāṭu, veṇpā, kaḷḷamiṉmi, teḷivu.
pacci = iḻavu vīṭṭiṟ koṭukkum payaṟu. iṭakkar, pacci miyiṟ kuttiya toyyil, moḻiyiṉ iyalpu nili.
civappu = māṇikkam (civappukkal), ciṉam.
mañcaḷ = kāmāli.
iṉi, potumakkaḷ eṉṉum col putiyateṉṟum, poruntāta teṉṟum, cila pulavar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

pāmpaṟiyum pāmpiṉa kāl
eṉṉum 7ām ceyyuḷil potumakkaḷ eṉṉum col vantiruttal kāṇka.
iṉi, ikkāla akaramutalikaḷil (akarātikaḷil) cila coṟ kaṭkut tavaṟāṉa poruḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatāl avaṟṟi āyntu uṇmiyik kaṭippiṭittal vēṇṭum. ōri eṉṉum colliṟkum hōrā eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṟkum yātoru toṭarpumilli. aṅṅaṉamē ataṟkum avar (hour) eṉṉum āṅkilac colliṟkum, kaṭakavōri, kaṉṉiyōri eṉak kaṇiya nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatu cariyē, ōri eṉpatu kūṭṭattik kuṟikkum col: atu ciṟappāka makaḷir ōriyi yum uṭukkaḷiṉ ōriyiyum kuṟikkum. makaḷirik kuṟikkum col, avar viḷiyāṭṭiyum viḷiyāṭum iṭattiyum ākupeyarāka uṇarttum.
ollutal = poruntutal, kūṭutal. ol - or - ōr - ōri.
ōriyik kuṟikkum irāci eṉṉum vaṭacol, kūṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷatē. atu piṉṉar iṉatti uṇarttum ip poruḷil atu race eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟku iṉamākum. Constellation eṉṉum āṅkilac collum uṭukkūṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟiyē ōriyik kuṟikkum. Con = together stella = star. ōri eṉṉum tūya tamiḻc colli āriyac col lākak kāṭṭaṟku. atik kirēkkac collōṭum āṅkilac collōṭum valintu toṭarpupaṭuttiṉar. ātalāl, iṉik kaṭakavōri, kaṉṉiyōri eṉṟē kuṟippiṭuka.  (tamiḻppāvi eḻuttāḷar maṉṟac ciṟappu malar 1964 - 65)
karumpuvaki
karumpu kariyatu; vēḻam veḷiyatu; irāmak karumpu veṇmi yum cemmiyuṅ kalantatu. (col 71)
karumam
karumam - karmaṉ (i.vē.)
karumam, karmaṉ eṉṉum irucollum ceyki, viṉi, toḻil eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum orē colliṉ iruvēṟu vaṭivu kaḷē. karumam eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṭiya karuvi eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyililli. kāraṇam kāriyam eṉṉum vaṭa colliṇi yottatē karuvi karumam eṉṉum teṉcol liṇiyum.
mūṉṟā kuvatē
oṭuveṉap peyariya vēṟṟumik kiḷavi
viṉimutal karuvi aṉimutaṟ ṟatuvē.  (tol. 557).
karumam allāc cārpeṉ kiḷavikku
urimiyum uṭittē kaṇeṉ vēṟṟumi  (mēṟpaṭi 568). (va.va.)
iṅṅaṉam karuvi karumam eṉṉum iru collum, toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varuvatuṭaṉ, ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭṭu-ṟuppukkaḷākavum amikiṉṟaṉa. ivviraṇṭum karu eṉṉum orē mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu piṟantavi. ātalāl, karuvi eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē karumam eṉṉum collum teṉcollātal teḷivu.
karu eṉṉum mutaṉili iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu. karuttal ceytal. karuṅkaḷamarum veṇkaḷamarum pōlap palvakippaṭṭa kariyarum palvakippaṭṭa poṉṉarumeṉa, iruvēṟu niṟavaki yiṉarākat tamiḻar toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. varuntiyuḻippataṉ viḷivāka, kariyar ki mikak karuppatum poṉṉar ki civappatum iyalpu. (va.va.)
karuṅki viṉiñaruṅ kaḷamaruṅ kūṭi  (cilap. 10: 125).
ceytaki cēvēṟum, ceyyāta ki nōvēṟum.  (paḻamoḻi)
ki karuttal paṟṟik karu eṉṉum viṉic collum civattal paṟṟic cey eṉṉum viṉic collum, tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṟkup piṟaviṉi vaṭivam vēṇṭiyatilli.
o.nō: veḷuttal = veḷḷiyātal (ta.vi.) tuṇiyi veḷḷi yākkutal (pi.vi.)
karumam - kammam - kam.
kammam = mutaṟṟoḻilākiya payirttoḻil.
kammavar - kammavāru = payirttoḻil ceyyum teluṅkar.
kam = palvēṟu kaṉiya (ulōka)t toḻil. īmuṅ kammum  (tol. 328) (va.va.)
kammāḷaṉ = poṟkollaṉ, aiṅkollaruḷ oruvaṉ.
kammiyaṉ = kaṟṟaccaṉ (ciṟpi).
karu + vi = karuvi. karu + aṇam = karaṇam = ceyki, tirumaṇac caṭaṅku, karuvi, akakkaruvi.
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇara  (tol. 1088).
itiṟ karaṇam eṉpatu tirumaṇaviṉiyākiya caṭaṅkik kuṟittatu.
vaṭavar karu eṉṉum mutaṉiliyik kru eṉat tirittuḷḷaṉar. iṅṅaṉam coṉmutal uyirmeyyil uyiri nīkkuvatu āriya marapu. (va.va.)
 o.nō: poṟu - pru (bh), tiru - crī, vari - vrīhi.
 kari - E cry, turuvu - E through, puruvam - E brow.
vaṭavar karaṇam eṉṉum collik kāraṇa eṉa nīṭṭi, ataṟkēṟpak kārya eṉṉum colliyun tirittuḷḷaṉar. kāraṇam eṉṉum nīṭṭam tamiḻukkēṟkum. āyiṉ, kārya eṉṉum tirippu ēṟkātu.
ēṟkeṉavē karaṇam eṉpatiṉiṉṟu karaṇiyam eṉṉum col tirintuḷatu. ataṟkēṟpak karumam eṉpatiṉiṉṟu karumiyam (kāriyam) eṉa oru collit tirittuk koḷḷalām.
cey, paṇ(ṇu), puri mutaliya pala piṟa oruporuṭcoṟkaḷ tamiḻil iruppataṉālum, karu eṉpatu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉati ṉālum, piṉṉatu vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkaṟ kiṭantarukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku muntiyateṉṟum peruñ colvaḷa moḻiyeṉṟum aṟiyiṉ, immayakkan teḷintuviṭum. tamiḻ tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkuriya il maṉi vīṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟi, āriya moḻikaṭkuriya kuṭi eṉṉuñ colliyun taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟu, kālṭuvelār kūṟiyirup patik kūrntu nōkkuka. (va.va.)
karuvi vaki
karuvi parupporuḷ āytappotu (Instrument) āyutam. oru toḻiṟkuriya karuvi (Tool); paṭi pōrkkaruvi (weapon); karaṇam aṟivuk karuvi allatu uṟuppuk karuvi; kāraṇam kāriyatti viḷivippatu (cuse); ētu vātakkāraṇam (Reason); mutal vaṇika mutal pōṉṟa mutaṉili; aṭimarattiṉ aṭipōṉṟa mutaṉili; marattiṉ vērpōṉṟa mutaṉili; vittu maram muḷitta viti pōṉṟa mutaṉili; talikkīṭu pōli ētu (pretext) (col: 45)

kariyēṟutal
ivvulaka vāḻkki ōr āṟṟi allatu kaṭalip pōṉṟatu. ivvulaka iṉpamākiya ciṟṟiṉpattil paṟṟu vittu vāḻvatu annīr niliyil mūḻki iṟappatiyum, paṟṟaṟṟu vāḻvatu ati nīntik kariyēṟi uyvatiyum nikarkkum. itaṉāl ulakap paṟṟoḻittup pēriṉpa vīṭṭi aṭivataṟkuk kariyēṟutal eṉṟum, vīṭṭulaka vācikaḷukku akkariyar eṉṟum peyar.
piṟavik kaṭaliṉiṉṟu āṉmākkaḷik kariyēṟṟupavar eṉṉum karutti yuṭkoṇṭē camaya kuravarit tīrttaṅkarar eṉpar camaṇar. tīrttam eṉpatu nīrniliyik kuṟikkum vaṭacol. (col . 5)

kar - kara. karattal = 1. maṟital. karantuṟi kaṇakkum (maṇi. 2: 26), 2. maṟittal. taṉṉuḷaṭakkik karakkiṉuṅkarakkum (puṟanā. 1: 8). karappāṉ = pakalil maṟintu tiriyum pūcci. karavu = maṟivu. Gk. Kruptos (hidden, secret.)
9. koṟṟoḻil.
irumpu karuniṟamāyiruppatāl, akkaṉiyattiṉ peyarum, aivakik kolluḷ oṉṟāṉa irumpaṭipput toḻiliṉ peyarum, attoḻilic ceyvōṉ peyarum, karu eṉṉuñ colli aṭimoḻiyākap peṟṟaṉa.
karumpoṉ = irumpu. karumpoṉiyal paṉṟi (cīvaka. 104.) karuntātu = irumpu. karuntātu koṭku miruñcili (ñāṉā. 57: 29). karuṅkol = irumpu (cūṭā). karuṅkollaṉ = irum paṭippōṉ, karumakaṉ - karumāṉ - karumaṉ (piṅ.) = iruppuk kollaṉ, karumakak kammiyaṉ (kamparā. pampā. 37) ma. karumāṉ. E. Blacksmith.
10. karumam
viṉiceytu kāyppēṟumpōtu, cenniṟattār allatu poṉṉiṟattār ki mikac civantum, karuniṟattār ki mikak kaṟuttum tōṉṟuvatu iyalpu. ceytaki cēvēṟum, ceyyāta ki nōvēṟum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. itu cenniṟattār kiyik kuṟittatu. karuniṟattār ki karuṅkiyeṉappaṭum.

1. kuṟṟak karuttiṉ kiḷi
kaḷakkam - kaḷakkar = 1. kīḻōr, 2. oru vēṭṭuvak kulattār.
kīḻmakkaḷum āṟalikkum vēṭṭuvarum kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ eṉṉum vakiyil oru vakuppārāvar.
2. maṟivuk karuttiṉ kiḷikaḷ
1. kaḷavu
kaḷaveṉpatu oruvaṉ poruḷi iṉṉoruvaṉ maṟivākak kavartal. kaḷ - kaḷḷam = tiruṭṭu. kaḷḷam = kaḷḷaṉ.
kaḷ + tal = kaṭṭal (tiruṭutal), kaṭpō ruḷareṉiṉ (cilap. 5: 115). kaḷvu - kaḷvam = tiruṭṭuc ceyal. kaḷvaṉ = tiruṭaṉ. kaḷvu - kaḷavu - kaḷavaṉ. kaḷavu - kaḷavāḷi - kaḷavāṇi. kaḷavu - kayavu (piṅ.).
kaḷḷam - ma. kaḷḷam, te. kalla.
kaḷvaṉ - ma. kaḷvaṉ, tu. kaḷvē, va. fyk, fsî - k., f., tu. kaḷavu. Gk. Klepto (to Steal), L. clam (Secretly).
karattal = kavartal. karavu = kaḷavu (tivā). karavar = kaḷvar (piṅ.) karavaṭam = kaḷavu (tivā.) karavaṭar = tiruṭar (tivā.) karavaṭanūl = kaḷavunūl. (cilap. 16: 180, arum.)
2. vañcaṉi
vañcaṉi yeṉpatu oṉṟi uḷḷattil maṟittu vittal.
kaḷḷam = vañcaṉi. kaḷḷam piṟavō pacappu (kuṟaḷ. 1184). kaḷavu = vañcaṉi, naṅkaḷava ṟuttuniṉ ṟāṇṭami (tiruvāca. 5: 35). karavu = vañcaṉi. kaḷavaṟintār neñciṟkaravu (kuṟaḷ. 288). karavaṭam = vañcakam. maṉattilē karavaṭamām vēṭam (taṇṭali. cata. 29.)
3. poy
poyyāvatu collāl oṉṟi maṟittal. kaḷḷam = poy. kaḷḷamē pēci (tēvā. 1115: 6). karavu = poy. karaveṉu muṉṟa ṉūlil (tiruvāta. pu. putta. 55).
4. īyāmi
īyāmiyāvatu oruvaṉ taṉ poruḷ nukarcciyip piṟarukku maṟittal.
karattal = koṭātiruttal (piṅ.) karu - karum - karumu. karumutal = piciṉittaṉam paṇṇutal. karumi = kañcaṉ.
5. karital
karitalāvatu oru poruḷ ciṟitu ciṟitāy maṟital.
kara - kari. karital = ciṟitu ciṟitāyk karattal, urukutal. kara - ma. fu¡F, bj., ka.tu. karagu.
 6. nīkkutal

 7. kaḷ

kaṭkaruttiṉ kiḷi.

 kalvi (Culture)
makkaḷ nākarikamillāta māṇmutu paḻimiyiṟ kuṟuntoki yarāyk kuṟiñci nilattu vāḻntapōtu kāykaṉi kiḻaṅku mutaliya iyaṟki viḷiporuḷkaḷiyē uṇṭu vantaṉar. uṇpatum uṟaṅkuvatumē avark kiruperuntoḻil. makkaṭṭoki mika mika, iyaṟki viḷivu pōtātāyiṟṟu. vitikaḷtāvara varkkat tiṉiṉṟum kīḻē viḻuntu nilattil muḷippati muṉṉamē uṟṟu nōkki ūkittiruntaṉar. aḵtaṉṟi vaḷḷik kiḻaṅkip paṉṟikaḷ uḻutaviṭattu viḻunta vitikaḷ virivil muḷittu, aṭarntōṅki, viḻumiya palaṉṟantamiyuṅ kaṇṭiruntaṉar. ātalāl avarē attaki yiṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceyaṟkiyiṟ payir paccikaḷi viḷikkat toṭaṅkiṉar.
 paṉṟiyuḻutaviṭattup payirviḷippati.
aruvi yārkkuṅ kaḻipayi ṉaṉantalik

ruḻā atu vittiya parūukkuraṟ ciṟutiṉi  (puṟam. 168)
eṉpatāṟ kāṇka.
ākavē, mutaṉmutal makkaḷ kaṟṟa kalvi uḻavuttoḻil eṉpatē pulaṉākiṉṟatu. uyir vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavu ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭutaliṉ, kalvi eṉṉum collum uḻavut toḻiliyē mutaṉ mutaṟ kuṟittatu. kal, pakuti; vi, vikuti. kallal tōṇṭal. kalleṉṉuñ collē illeṉṟu tiriyum. payirttoḻil viṉikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷum uḻavē mutalatum mutaṉmiyatu mātaliṉ, av vuḻaveṉ peyarē oruvaki ākupeyarāyp payirttoḻil muḻumikkumāyiṟṟu. aṉṟiyum vāṉāvārip payirkaṭku uḻavoṉṟē yamiyum. uḻavāvatu nilatti akaḻtalum nilipeyarttalum pūḻiyākka lum, ērumerutuṅkoṇṭuḻavaṟiyāta paḻaṅkālattuk kalluṅ kaḻiyuṅ karuviyāka nilatti akaḻtalē uḻavāyiṟṟu. akkālat tum kuṟiñci nilattum maḻikkuk kuṟiyiṉmiyiṉ nīrpāycca vum vēṇṭātāyiṟṟu.
kallal, tōṇṭal, uḻutal eṉpaṉa oruviṉi kuṟittaliṉ oru poruṭ kiḷavi. ākavē, kalvi eṉpatu mutaṉmutal uḻavu kuṟitta kiḷaviyē yeṉpatu peṟṟām. uḻaviṟkup piṉ kaṟkappaṭṭatu kittoḻil. kittoḻiṟkup piṉṉatu kaliyaṟivu allatu nūṟkalvi. ākavē, toḻiṟkalvi, nūṟkalvi yeṉak kalvi iru vakittāyiṟṟu. uḻavum kittoḻilum toḻiṟkalvi, kali yaṟivu nūṟkalvi. ivviraṇṭaṉuṭ ṭalimiyum iṉṟiyami yāmiyum paṟṟi nūlkalviyē kalviyeṉa vitantōtappaṭṭatu.
vaṭanūṉ muṟiyil vaḻaṅkum aṟupāṉāṟ ṟoḻilkaḷum toḻiṟ kalviyākum; ēṉi nāṉmaṟi yaṟukalikaḷ nūṟkalvi yākum.
iruvakik kalviyilum ennūliyum maṟi yeṉpatu paṇṭi vaḻakku. icinūli narampiṉ maṟi eṉṟār tolkāppiyar. maruttu nūli āyurvētameṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. kaṇkaruvi yākak kikaṟkum toḻiṟkalviyum ciṟupāṉmi nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulatti nūlvāyilāyuṇarttu meṉka.
ovvoru nūlum oru cār maṟiporuḷi yuṭimiyiṉ maṟi yeṉappaṭṭatu. iṉi, ellā nūlkaṭkum muṭivu vīṭupēṟu kūṟum kaṭavuṇmaṟiyātaliṉ, am maṟiyuṇarccikku orucār karuviyākum piṟa nūlkaḷum av viyipāṉē maṟiyeṉap paṭṭaṉa veṉṉalu moṉṟu.
nūṟkalviyum ulakanūl, aṟivunūl eṉa iruvakittām. aṟivu nūl vīṭṭunūleṉavum paṭum. kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭum vīṭupēṟum kūṟum maṟiyākamak katikaḷum, ilakkaṇa tarukka tattuva nūl kaḷum vīṭṭu nūlkaḷākum. ulaka vāḻkkikkuriya vellām ulaka nūlkaḷākum. āṉmavuṇarcci ciṟantakālattu vīṭṭunūlē nūleṉappaṭṭatu. tamiḻilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅ kaḷellām vīṭupēṟṟiṟkē vaḻikāṭṭuvaṉa.

alakucāl kaṟpiṉ aṟivunūl kallā
tulakanū lōtuva tellāṅ - kalakala
kūun tuṇiyallāṟ koṇṭu taṭumāṟṟam
pōon tuṇiyaṟivā ril
 eṉṟār periyōr.
ikkālattuk kaṟkappaṭum āṅkilak kalvi tamiḻmuṟippaṭi ulaka nūliṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē.
eḻuttaṟiyumpōtē ari ōm nama: eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatum, eḻuttukaḷē mantiravaṭivāyiruppatum, eḻuttukkūṭṭi vācikkap puku māttiriyē aṟañceya virumpu, ālayan toḻuvatu cālavu naṉṟu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa aṟivurikaḷi aṟivippatum, ilakkaṇa muḻumiyum meyyuṇarcci yilakuvatum, ilakkiyamellām vīṭupēṟṟi yilakkāk koḷvatum tamiḻukkē ciṟanta takimi kaḷām.
ākavē, kalviyeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭu tamiḻil mutaṉmutal uḻaviṟ kiyaṟpeyarā yiruntu, piṉṉark kittoḻiṟkum nūṟkalvikkum iṉa vilakkaṇamāy iṟutiyil.... nūlukkē ciṟappāka variyaṟuk kappaṭṭateṉka.
nilattik kalluvatiṉāl, āḻattiṉ maṟintirukkum kaṉi (Mine)íª tāvaramumākiya iruvaki viḷiporuḷkaḷu mārāyappaṭum: vitikaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ kiḷarnteḻum payirpacci kaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭiyum: kāykatirkaḷummuṟṟi makkaṭku nukarcciyuṇṭākum. atupēlak kalviyiṉālum aimpūtap pakuti yāṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷu mārāyappaṭum ; maṉattiṉ āṟṟal kaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭiyum. (cf. educate - to draw out the powers of mind); āṉma nukarcciyākiya vīṭupēṟum cittikkum.
kalviyeṉṉun tamiḻccoṟkuc camamāṉa āṅkilaccol Culture eṉpatākum. atu kal eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyāyp piṟanta viṉip peyarē. kalvi eṉpatil vi vikuti: Culture eṉpatil taram (ture) vikuti. kalviyeṉṉuñ coṟkuṟikkum poruḷkaḷiyē Culture eṉpatuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟatu. aviyāvaṉa:
1. Culture - n. Cultivation
L. colo - to till. kal = tōṇṭu, uḻu. payiriṭu.
iḵtaṉṟi āṅkilattil uḻavu kuṟikkum ēṉic coṟkaḷil iṉṟiyamiyāta cilavum tamiḻēyāta laṟiyappaṭum.
toḷ - till; toḷ - toṭu - tōṇṭu. toḷḷal = tōṇṭal allatu uḻutal.
ēr - n. E. ear, L. aro, GK. aroo. adj. E. arable.
akaram - n. E. acre, Gk. agros, L. ager, a field.
akaram marutanilattūr - cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu. marutam = vayal. kāṟu (koḻu) - share.
Culture eṉṉuñ col kalviyālāṉa oḻukkatti allatu tiruttatti uṇarttiya piṉṉar, uḻavu āṅkilattil Agriculture eṉa aṭikoṭukkappaṭṭatu.
2. Culture-n. refinement
kalviyālaṟivum aṟivā loḻukkamum payaṉātaliṉ kalvi yeṉpatu kāraṇavākupeyarāyt tiruttattiyuṅ kuṟikku meṉka.
kalvi toṭarpāṉa coṟkaḷ
kaḻakam - college, n. (orig.) any collection or community of men with certin privileges or a common pursuit as a college of heralds or the college of cardinals: a seminary of learning; a literary, political, or religious institution. - Chambers Etymological Dictionary
N.B. orig. ‘g’ was pronounced as ‘g’ in god and the final ‘e’ was not silent.
 kal +akam = kallakam - kaḻakam.
 kallakam viṉittoki
 Pedagogue - n. a teacher.
L. - Gk. pidos (pital), a boy; ago - (uki) to lead.
pedantry - n. vin display of learning (same)
Cyclopeadia - n. the circle of human knowledge.
Gk. kyklos (cakkaram), a circle; pideia (from the root - go) learning.
3. Cult - n. worship
L. colo - to worship.
kaṟṟataṉā lāya payaṉeṉkol vālaṟivaṉ
naṟṟā ṭoḻāa reṉiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 21)
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl uḻavē ulaka mutaṟkalvi yeṉṟum kalvi yeṉṉuñ col mutaṉmutal uḻavu kuṟitta collē yeṉṟum, piṟkālattu nūṟkalviyi variyaṟuttamiyiṉ uḻavupporuḷil vaḻakkaṟṟateṉṟum, ulakil mutaṉmutal nākarikameyti uḻavuṅ kalviyu maṟintār tamiḻmakkaḷēyeṉ ṟum avarāṉēyē av virutoḻilum mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ paraviya teṉṟum. iv virutoḻiliyuṅ kuṟikkum mēṉāṭṭuc coṟ kaḷil iṉṟiyamiyāta vellām centamiḻt tiripē yeṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka. (centamiḻc celvi cuṟavam 1933)

kalavam - kalāpa

kalavam viritta maññi (poruna. 212). 2. mayil (ciṉiyāku peyar).
kalavañcēr kaḻikkāṉal  (tēvā. 532: 4)
kalavu - kalāvu. kalāvutal = kalattal. kalavam - kalāvam = mayil tōki.
kalimayiṟ kalāvam  (puṟam. 146: 8)
mayil teṉṉāṭṭuk kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya paṟavi; kuṟiñcit teyvamākiya murukaṉ ūrti.
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalā (ciṟupakuti) + āp (koḷ, to obtin) eṉṟu pirittu, palapakutikaḷi oṉṟucērppatu kaṟṟi (“that which holds single parts together, abundle” eṉṟu kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatu, vaṭṭañcuṟṟi valintum nalintum poruḷ koḷḷuvatā yiruttal kāṇka. (va.va: 109 - 110.)

pirā. (pirākuvī). kaṉ (khan), to give birth to; KGad, karr, to yean: Kur. (Kurukh), kharj, to be produced, to bear fruit.
LATIN WORDS
te. kaṉu - L. geno, to beget.
genero, to beget, produce, create, cuse to exist, bring to life, generate.
gener, a son-in-law.
generosus, of nobel birth.
genesis, the constellation which presides over one’s birth.
genetivus, genativus, inborn, innate.
genetrix, one who brings forth or bears, a mother.

genitalis, belonging to birth.
genitura, a begetting, genitor, a begetter.
gens, a clan, a number of families connected together by a common discent and the use of the same gentile name.
genus, birth, descent, origin.

kaliccollākka neṟimuṟikaḷ
taṟkālak kalvik kalikaḷiṟ pala. mēṉāṭṭiṉiṉṟum namakku van tamiyāl, avaṟṟippaṟṟiya perunūlkaḷum, kuṟiyīṭukaḷum iṉṟum perumpālum mēli moḻikaḷilēyē uḷḷaṉa. taṟpōtu kuṭiyaracukkāṉa vaḻituṟikaḷi vakukkuṅkāl, tāymoḻiyiṟ kalvi kaṟpikka vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ tamiḻnāṭṭuk kalvikkut tamiḻiṟ kaliccoṟkaḷiyum, kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum moḻi peyarttuk koḷḷutalum ākkik koḷḷutalum iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. itaṟkākap pala cārpil pala muṟikaḷ pala kuḻukkaḷ palaviṭattiṟ kūṭiyārāyntum ōraḷavu vēli ceytum vantirukkiṉṟaṉa; varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, kalic coṟ kaḷi ākkikkoḷḷum neṟimuṟikaḷi iṉṉum ak kuḻukkaḷ cariyāy uṇarātiruppatu peritum varuntattakkatu.
tamiḻiṟ kaliccoṟkaḷi ākkuvār āṅkilam tamiḻ iraṇ ṭiyum naṉṟāyaṟintavarāyum collārāycciyuṭiyavarāyum tamiḻppaṟṟu nirampiyavarāyu miruttal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻaṟiyāta perumāḷar kaliccollākkuvatu kuruṭar vaḻikāṭṭuvatiyum, collārāycciyillātār ākkum kaliccol iḷañciṟār ōviyattiyumē okkum. tamiḻppaṟ ṟillātavariṭam kaliccollākkatti oppuvippatō peṟṟa tāyip paṟṟalariṭam oppuvippatēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. mēṟkūṟiya mūviyalpukaḷ uḷḷavar kuḻumiya piṉpum āttirappaṭalākātu. palanūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāy mēli moḻikaḷil tōṉṟiya kuṟiyīṭu kaḷukku ōriru nāḷil allatu mātattil nērcol kāṇa muṭiyātu. pataṟiya kāriyam citaṟik keṭum; pataṟāta kāriyam citaṟātu. virintu ceyvatu maṟintupōm. nīṭittuc ceyvatu nilittu niṟkum.
aitarāpāttiluḷḷa ucumāṉiyāp palkalikkaḻakattār. paṭṭat tērvukaṭkuriya kallūrik kalviyi urutumoḻiyiṟ pukuttu muṉ, nālāṇṭākap poṟumiyuṭaṉ uḻittuk kalic coṟkaḷiyellām am moḻiyil ākkik koṇṭaṉar. nāmum avarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum.
tamiḻiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām iṉṉum tokukkappaṭa villi. nūlvaḻakkiluḷḷa coṟkaḷē palkalikkaḻaka akarāti 
yil (University Lexicon) muṟṟum iṭampeṟavilliyāyiṉ, ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷip paṟṟic collavēṇṭuvatē yilli. uḻavu, kittoḻil, ulakiyal mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya pala ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ kaliccollākkattiṟkup peritum payaṉ paṭuvaṉa. ākiyāl, mutalāvatu nūlvaḻakku, ulaka vaḻakku, kalveṭṭu im mūṉṟiṉiṉṟum itukāṟum akarātiyiṟ pukāta coṟkaḷi yellām tokuttuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum. itu ceyyāmaṟ kaliccollākkuvatu karuviyillāmaṟ karumañ ceyvatē pōlum.
araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭāṭi yaṟṟē nirampiya
nūliṉṟik kōṭṭi koḷal.   (kuṟaḷ. 401)
iraṇṭāvatu, centamiḻc colliyal neṟimuṟikaḷil, kalic collākkattiṟku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi yellām terintukoḷ ḷal vēṇṭum. atāvatu, muṉṉoṭṭukaḷ, piṉṉoṭṭukaḷ, iṭiyoṭṭukaḷ, īṟukaḷ eṉṟa nāṟṟiṟappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉ allatu kuṟiccoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ payaṉkaḷellā vaṟṟiyum terintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
mūṉṟāvatu, avvak kaliyil tērccipeṟṟa āṅkila aṟiñarik koṇṭu moḻipeyarkkavēṇṭum. āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷi yellām kalivāriyāka eḻutuvittuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
nālāvatu, muṟkūṟiya mūvakit tiṟaṉum oruṅkaminta tiṟavōr, oruṅkē yamarntu tīra āyntu, kalivāriyāka moḻi peyark kavō coṟpuṉiyavō vēṇṭum.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ acinili, puṇarnili, pakucoṉṉili, tokinili, paltokinili, pirinili eṉa aṟu nilikaḷiyaṭiyumēṉum, aviyāvum taṉinili, puṇarnili eṉa iraṇṭā yaṭaṅkum. taṉiniliyāvatu coṟka ḷellām taṉittaṉiyāka amintu kiṭakkum nili: puṇar niliyāvatu ac coṟkaḷ iraṇṭum palavum kūṭip pakucoṟ kaḷāyum toṭar moḻikaḷāyum puṇarntu niṟkum nili: taṉiniliyākkam perumpālum paṇṭik kālattilēyē muṟṟup peṟṟatu. puṇarniliyākkam ekkālattiṟkum uriyatu. ātalāl, coṟkaḷip pakuttāyntu collākka muṟikaḷik kaṇṭu piṭittu avaṟṟik kiyāḷiṉ ekkālattum iyaṟki muṟippaṭiyē vēṇṭumaḷavu putuccoṟ puṉintu koḷḷa lām.
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷatti aṟiyāta cilar, tamiḻiṟ pōtiya col illiyeṉṟum, atil moḻipeyarttaṟku āṅkilam allatu vaṭamoḻi pōṉṟa ayaṉmoḻit tuṇi iṉṟi yamiyātateṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivar, kirēkku ilattīṉ mutaliya āriya moḻikaḷil centamiḻ vērkaḷ ceṟintu kiṭappatiyum, āṅkilak kaliccoṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷ eḷimiyiyum, collamippu neṟimuṟikaḷiyum, tamiḻ nikaṇṭukaḷiyum aṟintiruntāl iṅṅaṉam kūṟār.
āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ amippeḷimiyi viḷakkutaṟku, cēmparār (Chambers) āṅkilac colliyal akarāti yiṉiṉṟum cila col viḷakkaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟēṉ.
	(1)	Alum: a mineral salt (cf. aḷam = c¥ò)
		L. alumen
 		Alumina: one of the earth. L. alumen, alum.
 		Aluminium: the metalic base of alumina.
	(2) 	Candidate: so called becuse at Rome, the applicant used to dress in white.
 		L. candidus, white-candeo (cf. fhªJ), to shine.
	 (3)	Chancellor: Low L. cancellarius, orig. an officer that had charge of records, and stood near the cancelli (L.) the crossbars that surrounded the judgement-seat.
	(4)	Electric: having the property of attracting and repelling light bodies when rubbed.
		L. electram-Gk. electron, amber (cf. m«g®) in which the above property was first observed.
	(5)	Protein: the common radical of the most essential articles of food.
 		Gk. protos, first, and suffix-in.
kaliccoṟkaḷit taṉittamiḻilēyē yāttal vēṇṭum. av yāppukkūṭāta orō vaḻi maṭṭum ayaṉmoḻit tuṇi vēṇṭappaṭum. itu ellā moḻikkum potu.
moḻipeyarpput tiṟaṉil tamiḻ emmoḻikkum iḷitta taṉṟu. taṉittamiḻil kuṟiyīṭṭukkākap puṉitaṟku, vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷi vaḻakkiṟkuk koṇartal vēṇṭum. vaḻaṅkāmalē coṟkaḷ vaḻakkaṟukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattil vaḻaṅkāta tuppuravu (=cuttam) eṉṉum teṉcol teṉpākat tilum, teṉpākattil vaḻaṅkāta nūkku (=taḷ) eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭapākattilum, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkāta neyttōr eṉṉum teṉcol teluṅkil netturu eṉṟum vaḻaṅku vati nōkkuka. aṉṟiyum tamiḻccollirukka ayaṟcol nāṭal. kiyil veṇṇeyirukka neyk kali vatum, ētilār ārat tamar pacikka viṭutalumākum.
