 e-ṭu: colemn - piḻampu.
 colonel, (the leader of a column of soldiers) piḻampar.
 phenamenon (an appearance) - tōṟṟaravu.
am īṟupeṟṟa tōṟṟam eṉṉum col potuvāṉa tōṟṟattik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkataliṉ, aravu īṟupeṟṟa tōṟ ṟaravu eṉṉum col oru vitapput tōṟṟattik kuṟittaṟ kēṟṟa tātal kāṇka.
13. 	cila āṅkilac coṟkaḷukkuc cemmoḻipeyarppuc (literal) ceytukoḷḷalām.
 	e-ṭu: inspect - uṇṇōkku
 	inspection: uṇṇōkkam.
 	inspector: uṇṇōkkāḷar.
14. cila coṟkaḷ āṅkilattukkum tamiḻukkum potuvā yiruppatāl, avaṟṟi aṅṅaṉamē vittukkoḷḷalām.
 	e-ṭu: note - nōṭṭu.
 	nōṭu = pār, kavaṉi, nōṭuvatu nōṭṭu, nōṭṭam, nōṭṭam eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar tamiḻilum, nōṭu eṉṉum pakuti allatu ēval kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
15. ōr āṅkilac coṟku nērāṉa tamiḻccolli av vāṅkilac col kuṟikkum pala poruḷilum vaḻaṅkalām.
 	e-ṭu: article = uruppaṭi = 
	1. poruḷ (cāmāṉ)
 	2. paṇṭam
 	3. ceytip pakuti.
16.	oru poruḷiṉ pala kūṟukaḷik kuṟittaṟku oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷi āḷalām.
 e-ṭu: pork - paṉṟikkaṟi. L. porcus, a pig.
 tumpikki = yāṉikki, tumpi = yāṉi.
17.	cila āṭpeyarkaḷiyum, av vāḷkaḷip paṟṟiya vitappuc ceytikaḷik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷāka vaḻaṅkalām.
 	e-ṭu: Boycott, from Captin Boycott who was excommunicated by his neighbours in Ireland in 1881.
18.	coṟkaḷi iyaṉṟavari poruḷ mayakkamiṉṟi amikka vēṇṭum.
19.	ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷukku, ataṉ ayaṉ moḻip peyarē amiyavēṇṭumeṉpatu yāppuṟaviṉṟu.
 	e-ṭu: ostrich = tīkkōḻi.
 	cycle = mitivaṇṭi.
20.	tamiḻukkillātu piṟamoḻikaṭkuḷḷa collākka muṟi kaḷil, centamiḻ iyalpiṟ kēṟṟavaṟṟit tamiḻilum taḻuvik koḷḷalām.
āṅkilattil peyarccoṟkaḷi viṉiccoṟkaḷākap payaṉ paṭuttuvatu perumpāṉmi, tamiḻilum, karumpu eṉṉum col, karumpi allatu atu pōṉṟatit tiṉpatik kuṟikkum viṉic collākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
 toṭarpiṉāl, vaḻivaḻi vevvēṟu poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkum ākupeyarpōṉṟē, vevvēṟu viṉikaḷi allatu karuttu kaḷik kuṟikkum ākuviṉikaḷumuṇṭu. āṅkilattiluḷḷa focus ṉṉum col ilattīṉil aṭuppu eṉṉum poruḷatu. atu āṅkilattil neruppic celuttal, oru poruḷit tiruppal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil viṉiyākavum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. im muṟiyil tamiḻilum coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkik koḷḷalām.  (ce.ce. 1941)
kaliyuṅ kalviyum
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil kalvi ikkālattiṟpōl araciyal tiṇikkaḷaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iruntatilli. āciriyariṉ muyaṟciyiṉālum potumakkaḷiṉ pōṟṟaraviṉālumē, potuk kalvi nāṭṭiṟ parappappeṟṟu vantatu. kaṟṟuvalla pulavariyē aracar ōraḷavu pōṟṟivantaṉar.
potukkalvi: eḻuttum eṇṇum kaṟpitta tuvakkakkalvi yāciriyarkku iḷampālāciriyar eṉṟum, ciṟṟilakkaṇamum nikaṇṭum kaṇakkuṅ kaṟpitta naṭuttarak kalviyāciriyarkkuk kaṇakkāyar eṉṟum, aintilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṟkuriya ilakki yamuṅ kaṟpitta mēṟṟarak kalviyāciriyarkku āciriyar eṉṟum, peyar. āciriyar, nūlāciriyar nuvalāciriyar (pōtakāciriyar) uriyāciriyar eṉa mūvakiyar.
tuvakkakkalvi māṇavarkkum naṭuttarak kalvi māṇavarkkum, paḷḷippiḷḷikaḷ eṉṟum, māṇiyar eṉṟum māṇavar (māṇavakar, māṇākkar) eṉṟum, caṭṭar eṉṟum, peyar. avaruḷ talimiyāṉavaṉ caṭṭanampi allatu caṭṭa nampip piḷḷi (caṭṭām piḷḷi) eṉappaṭṭāṉ. mēṟṟarak kalvi māṇavarkku māṇavar eṉṟum maḻapulavar eṉṟum, kaṟṟuc colliyar eṉṟum, peyar.
tuvakkakkalvi niliyilum naṭuttarakkalvi niliyilum māṇa variṉ peṟṟōrum, mēṟṟarakkalvi niliyil māṇavarum, āciriyarkkuc campaḷam iṟuttu avarippōṟṟi vantaṉar. ēḻiyāyiruntu āciriyaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyyum māṇa vaṉukku muttarak kalviyum ilavacamāyk kaṟpikkappaṭṭatu.
ciṟappuk kalvi: māṇavar potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, ici nāṭakam kaṇiyam maruttuvam mutaliya ciṟappuk kalikaḷi, avvakkaliyāciriyar vāyilāyk kaṟṟaṉar. ak kālattu nūlkaḷellām ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntamiyiṉ, evvaki nūṟkalvikkum ciṟanta ilakkaṇakkalvi iṉṟiyamiyātatā yiruntatu.
poruḷ koṭuppōrkkum paṇiviṭi ceyvōrkkum kalippayiṟci yaḷikkappaṭṭatu.
meynnūṟkalvi: potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, allatu potuk kalviyuñ ciṟappuk kalviyuṅ kaṟṟapiṉ, meyp poruḷaṟivu peṟa virumpiṉavar, kāṭṭakattuḷḷa muṉivariṭam allatu yōkiyariṭam ceṉṟu mūvāṇṭu meynnūṟkalvi kaṟṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
vēṇṭiya kalviyāṇṭumūṉ ṟiṟavātu 
 eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (1134).
ceyyuḷiyaṟṟum tiṟam: eṇṇum eḻuttum tavira evvakik kalviyum ceyyuḷ vāyilākavē kaṟṟamiyālum, iḷamiyi liruntu ceyyuḷ ceytum ceyyuḷiṟ pēciyum payiṉṟami yālum, paṇṭip pulavarellām ceyyuḷiyaṟṟun tiṟam ciṟakkappeṟṟiruntaṉar.
aracar tamiḻi vaḷarttalum pulavarip pōṟṟalum: pāṇṭiyar toṉṟu toṭṭuk ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari kaḻakam (caṅkam) irutti, nūl niliyam pōṟṟi, (iṭiccaṅka nūl niliyattil maṭṭum eṇṇāyiratteccam nūliruntatāka vaḻimuṟic ceyti kūṟum.) mutunūlārāycciyum putu nūlākkamum nikaḻvittu, muttamiḻi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kaḻakaṅ kulintapiṉ, muttamiḻaracarum tattam aviyil ōriru ciṟanta pulavari yamarttip pōṟṟivantaṉar. avikkaḷap pulavarkkum, nūlaraṅkēṟiyavarkkum, aracarip pukaḻntu pāṭiyavarkkum, muṟṟūṭṭum ciṟantaparicum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. āṭal pāṭal vallārum araṅkēṟi āyiratteṇ kaḻañcupoṉ peṟṟaṉar. araṅkēṟṟam aracaṉ muṉṉiliyilum avvattamiḻiṟ ciṟanta atikāriyiṉ talimiyilum naṭipeṟṟatu. araṅkēṟiya nūlāciriyari veṇpaṭṭaṇivittu yāṉimēlēṟṟi nakarvalam varuvippatu vaḻakkam.
ilakkiyap pulavarum camayanūṟ pulavarum coṟpōr nikaḻtti uṇmi nāṭṭaṟkut, talinakartoṟum paṭṭimaṇṭapam eṉṉum tarukkamaṇṭapam iruntatu. pulavariṭit teyvap pulamiyiyum piṟaruḷḷak karuttaṟiyum āṟṟaliyum vaḷarttaṟ poruṭṭu, aracar avvappōtu tam uḷḷattiluḷḷa kiḷarcci mikka karuttip paṟṟi avarip pāṭaccolvatuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam pāṭappaṭuvatu kaṇṭacitti eṉappaṭṭatu. araca ruṭiya uḷḷakkarutti aṟivippatum aiyuṟavit teḷivippatumāṉa pāṭaṟkup poṟkiḻiyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. ittakiya camiyaṅ kaḷil, iṅṅaṉam pāṭiṉārkkituveṉṟu viḷampi muṉṉarē poṟ kiḻiyic caṅkamaṇṭapattil tūkkiviṭuvatu pāṇṭiyar vaḻakkam.
kuṟikki (camaciyi) pāṭalum, arikaṇṭam emakaṇṭam pōṉṟa kaṭumpā (ācukavi) pāṭalum, aracar avvappōtu nallicip pulavarkkum kaṭum pāvalarkkum naṭattivanta ceyyuṭ pōṭṭi vakikaḷākum.
takka pulavar talimi yamiccarākavum, tūtarākavum amarttap peṟṟaṉar. avikkaḷat talimip pulavarkkum, araṅkēṟiya pulavarkkum, paṭṭimaṇṭapattil veṉṟa pulavarkkum, civiki kuṭi koṭi mutaliya koṟṟac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. pulavariṉ parinturikkum vēṇṭukōṭkum iṇaṅkuvatum, avaratu aṟivuriyiyum aṟavuriyiyum iṭitturiyiyum ēṟṟukkoḷvatum, avaratu pulanturiyiyum paḻippuriyi yum poṟuttuk koḷvatum, cevviyaṟi yātum viṭi peṟātum eccamayattilum ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuḷ avarip pukaviṭuvatum, avarukkup paṇiviṭi ceyvatum, avari vaḻiviṭukkumpōtu ēḻaṭi piṉpōy mīḷvatum, akkālat taracar palar ceyalkaḷākum.
paricaḷiyāviṭattum tammi avamatittapōtum pulavar aracaric cāvippatumuṇṭu.
kalvit taravuyarvu: akkālap pulavar kaṭuttup pāṭum āṟṟaluṭiyarāyiruntu ceyyuḷilēyē tammuḷ uriyāṭi vantaṉar. nikaṇṭu eṉṉum akarātiyuṭpaṭa aṉittilakkiya mum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē yiruntaṉa. maturic caṅkattilum piṟa pulavaraviyilum araṅkēṟiya piṉṉarē nūlkaḷ nāṭṭil ulavalāyiṉa.
 kalvi vaḷarcci: taliccaṅkattil irunta pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟapattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avarip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar eḻuva reṉṟum; iṭiccaṅkattil irunta pulavar aimpattoṉpatiṉ mar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar mūvāyiratteḻu nūṟṟu var eṉṟum, avarip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiya var aivar eṉṟum; kaṭic caṅkattil irunta pulavar nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avarip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭi yaruḷ kaviyaraṅ kēṟiyavar mūvar eṉṟum, muccaṅka varalāṟu kūṟum.
itupōtuḷḷa kaṭiccaṅka ilakkiyattiliruntu aṟiyappaṭum pulavar ēṟattāḻa eḻunūṟṟuvar āvar. avaruḷ makaḷir irupattiruvarum aracar paṉṉiruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarum āvar.
aṟuvivāṇikaṉ, ōlikkaṭikkāraṉ, kaṇiyaṉ, kūttaṉ, kūlavāṇikaṉ, pāṇaṉ, porunaṉ, maruttuvaṉ, vaṇṇakkaṉ mutaliya palvakit toḻilāḷarum; kāvaṟpeṇṭu, araci, kuṟa makaḷ, pēymakaḷ mutuliya palatara makaḷirum; pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
kaṭic caṅkattiṟkup piṟkālattilum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉārum kulacēkarāḻvārum kaṇṭarātittarum ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaṉum pōlum aracarum, tiruveṇkāṭarpōlum vaṇikarum, 2 ām auviyārum kārikkālammiyārum pōlum makaḷirum, palar pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta pulavar, caṅkakālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum ettuṇiyarō aṟiyōm.
ācukavikaḷ kācu eṉṟu piṟkālattil vāṅkappaṭṭa vari, araca voppam peṟṟu avar vāḻkkic celaviṟku vāṅkappaṭṭatu pōlum!
aracar ilakkiyat toṇṭu: aracar pulavari nūliyaṟṟumāṟu ūkkiyum tāmum nūliyaṟṟiyum vantatuṭaṉ, pala taṉippāṭal kaḷiyum pāvum poruḷum aḷavum paṟṟit tokuttum tokuppittum uḷḷaṉar. naṟṟiṇi tokuppittōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ paṉṉāṭu tanta māṟaṉvaḻuti. kuṟuntoki tokuttōṉ pūrikkō. aiṅkuṟunūṟu tokuppittōṉ yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañ cēralirum poṟi, akanāṉūṟu tokuppittōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirapperuvaḻuti.
kavvi
kavi - kavvi = piḷappu, pirivu, malarvu alar.
kavvi - kavvitu. (ti.ma. 1144)
kavari
kavari - kavaram = ciṉam. kavaram - kavari = ciṉam mikka kāṭṭerumi, erumi.
 paṭittu cēṭeṟiyuñ ceṅkaṭ kavariyum  (kallāṭam, 59: 90)
kavari = nīṇṭa mayiraṭarnta tipēt terumi,
kavari mayirpōl aṭarntu vaḷarum campā neṟpayirvaki
kavari - cavari - cavaram - camaram - cāmaram.
cavari - camarī (va,). cāmaram - cāmara (va.). (ti.ma: 739-740.)
kavaḷam
kavaḷam - kavala
kavvu - kavaḷ - kavaḷam = kavvum aḷavāṉa uṇavu.
vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamilli. (va.va: 110)
kavāṉ
kavāṉ: kavīṉī, kavīṉikā (g) - a. vē.
kavi - kavāṉ = kavitta toṭiccantu, atupōṉṟa maliccantu.
 vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamilli. (va.va: 111)
kavuḷ
kavuḷ - kapōla
kavvu - kavuḷ = kuṟaṭupōṟ kavvum alaku, kaṉṉam.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum krap - kap (iraṅku) eṉṉum mūlam immiyum poruntātu. (va.va: 110)
kaḻakam
kaḻakam eṉpatu ciṟappākavum perumpāṉmiyākavum kaṟṟōr kūṭṭattiyē kuṟikkumēṉum, ciṟupāṉmi cūtāṭu miṭattiyum kuṟikkumāṟu ilakkiyattil āḷappaṭṭuḷatu.
kaḻakam eṉṉum colliṉ vērum varalāṟum teriyiṉ. (poruḷ teḷivām)
kul - kula - kulavu. kulavutal = kūṭutal.
kula - kala.
kala - kalavi.
kala - kalavi, kalappu - kalampu - kalampum - katampam.
kalampu - kalampakam
kala - kalaṅku - kalakku - kalakkam
kala - kalakam, kalām, kalāpam, kalāpaṉi.
iruvar allatu iru kaṭciyār kalantē poruvatāl, kalattaṟ karuttiṟ pōrkkaruttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. ki kalattal eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum, poru, camar eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷiyum nōkkuka.
mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟṟirivup paṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu, kalattaṟcoṟku, irupāṟ kūṭṭam, kalappu, kalakkam, kalakam eṉṉum nāṟ poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷami kāṇka. nīrum maṇṇum pōlap pala poruḷkaḷ allatu karuttukaḷ kalappatē kalakkam.
kul - kuḷ - kaḷ. kaḷḷutal = kūṭutal. poruntutal, ottal.
kaḷ - kaḷam = ērkkaḷamum pōrkkaḷamumpōlap palar kūṭumiṭam.
kaḷam - kaḷaṉ = avikkaḷam
kaḷaṉ - kaḻaṉi = ērkkaḷam uḷḷa vayal.
kaḷam - kaḷar = avi.
kaḷar - kaḷari = avi, kalvi nāṭakam mal vil payilum araṅku, vaḻakkumaṉṟam, toḻil ceyyum iṭam.
kaḷḷa = pōla.
kaḷḷa matippa vella vīḻa  (tol. 1235)
kuḷ - kuḻu - kuḻampu - kuḻappu - kuḻappam.
kuḻavi = tiraṇṭa ari kal.
kūṭutaṟ karuttil tiraṭcik karuttut tōṉṟum.
cērē tiraṭci   (tol. 846)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka.
kuḻu - kuḻūu
kuḻu - kuḻām.
kuḻu - kuḻampu.
kuḻu - kuḻumu - kuḻuvu. kuḻumu - kuḻumal, kuḻumam.
kuḻuvu - kuḻuval, kuḻumutal = kūṭutal, kuḻuvutal = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal.
kuḻumu - kaḻumu - kaḻumal = mayakkam, niṟivu, mikuti.
kuḻu - keḻu - keḻumu - keḻuvu.
keḻumutal = poruntutal, keḻuvutal = poruntutal, mayaṅkutal.
keḻuvu = naṭpu, keḻuva ōr uvamavurupu
keḻu - keḻi = naṭpu
kuḻu - kuḻi = tiraṇṭa kātaṇi.
kuḻu - (kuḻaku) - kaḻaku - kaḻakam = ōlakkam (Durbar), pulavaravi, kali payiliṭam, paṭikkalam, mal payiliṭam, cūtāṭumiṭam.
kuḷ - (kūḷ) - kūṭu.
kaḻakam eṉṉum col. kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷkoṇṭa kuḻu eṉṉum vēraṭiyākap piṟantiruttalāl, potuvākak kūṭṭam eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvatākum. atu, mutaṟkaṇ, ciṟanta kūṭṭa mākiya pulavaravikkup peyarāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntami ḻēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō.
eṉṉum parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇac ceyyuḷil , kaḻakam pulavaraviyik kuṟittatu. caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟku nēr teṉcol kaḻakam eṉpatē.
paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muttamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷum tamakku muntiya tamiḻilakkiyatti ārāyntu vantatiṉāl, kaṟṟōr payilum iṭattikkuṟitta kaḻakam eṉṉumcol, nāḷaṭivil, kaṟpōr payilum iṭattiyum kuṟikkat talippaṭṭatu. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa maturi yeṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapurat teṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu uttara maturi yeṉpa. eṉṟu iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri muṟiyē muttamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷiyum paṟṟik kūṟutal kāṇka. itaṉāl, iḷiñar kalvi payilumiṭatti.
kantaṉi yaṉiyavar kaliteri kaḻakam  (kamparā. nāṭṭup. 48)
eṉṟār kampar.
iṉi, īṇṭik kaḻakam eṉṉum coṟku, viṟkali maṟkali mutali ya pōrttuṟi payilum iṭam eṉavum poruḷurippar. atuvē kampar karuttāyiṉ pōrkkaliyiyum kalvit tuṟi kaḷuḷ oṉṟāka aṭakkik koḷka. tivākaram, paṭikkalam, maṟpōr mutaliyaṉa payilum iṭattiyum kaḻakam eṉak kuṟikkum.
ērkkaḷattilum avikkaḷattilumpōl pōrkkaḷattilum makkaḷ kūṭuvatāl, kaḷam eṉṉum col am mūṉṟiṭattiyum kuṟittatu. cūtāṭutaṟkum makkaḷ kūṭuva tālum, cūtum oruvakip pōrātalālum kaḻakam eṉṉum col mūṉṟāvatāka allatu iṟutiyākac cūtāṭumiṭat tiyum kuṟittatu. cūtum oruvakip pōr eṉpatic cūtu porutal eṉṉum vaḻakkiṉālum, cūtu pōrc carukkam eṉṉum villi pāratac carukkap peyarālum, uttiyālum, aṟiyalām, vēttaviyiluḷḷārellām ovvoru tuṟiyil tērcci peṟṟirupparātaliṉ, ōlakkamum pulavaraviyiṉ aṭaṅkum. ākavē, pulavaravi, kalvi payiliṭam, cūtāṭumiṭam ākiya mūvēṟu poruḷik kaḻakam eṉṉum col kuṟikkum eṉpatu teḷivām.
paḻakiya celvamum paṇpum keṭukkum
kaḻakattuk kāli pukiṉ.  (937)
 eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, cūtāṭumiṭattiyum,
kaḻakat tiyaluṅ kavaṟṟi ṉiliyum  (358)
eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālit toṭaril, cūtāṭṭiyum, kaḻakam eṉṉum col kuṟittatu.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟippiṉum itu arukiya vaḻakkē. iṟṟi ulaka vaḻakkilum, nāṭṭāṇmik kaḻakam, centamiḻk kaḻakam, civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, māṇavar kaḻakam, eṉak kaḻakam eṉṉum col cūtāṭukaḷamallāta piṟa kaḷarikaḷiyē kuṟittal kāṇka.
kuḻu eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟē kuḻām, kuḻumam mutaliya palvēṟu tokutip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruppiṉum, avi yāvum parupporuḷilaṉṟi nuṇporuḷil ottaṉa valla.
kuḻu = ciṟukūṭṭam (committee).
kuḻūu = maṟiporuṭ kuṟiyīṭu vaḻaṅkum toḻiṟ kulam allatu vakuppu.
kuḻām = kuḻuviṉum caṟṟup periya kūṭṭam (party).
kōṣṭi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku nērāṉa teṉcol kuḻām.
pajaṉik kōṣṭi eṉpatit tiruppāṭṭuk kuḻām eṉṉalām.
kuḻumal, kuḻuval = kuḻāttiṉum periya kūṭṭam (gathering).
 kuḻumam = vaṇikar caṅkamum toḻilāḷikaḷ caṅkamum pōṉṟa kūṭṭam (guild).
kuḻumpu = yāṉi niri, tōḻamik kūṭṭam (herd, company).
kaḷiṟṟuk kuḻumpiṉ  (maturik. 24).
kaḻakam = caṅkam (society, association).
ākavē, kuḻu, kuḻūu, kuḻām, kuḻumam eṉpaṉa muṟiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu periya tokutikaḷām. kaḻakam eṉpatu uyartiṇikkē yuriyatāyp perumpālum kalvipaṟṟiyatum niliyāṉatum kuḻumattiṟkuc camamāṉatumāṉa kūṭṭattiyē kuṟikkum. itaṉālēyē, paṇṭit tamiḻc caṅkattik kaḻakam eṉṟār parañcōti muṉivar. talikkaḻakap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar: iṭikkaḻakap pulavar aimpattoṉ patiṉmar: kaṭik kaḻakap pulavar nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar. ākavē, kaḻakam eṉpatu ciṟu kuḻuvik kuṟippataṉṟu.
iṉi cūtāṭu karuvikkuk kaḻaṅku, kaḻaṅkam eṉap peyariruppa tāl, kaḻaṅkam allatu kaḻaṅkakam eṉṉuñ col kaḻakam eṉat tokkuc cūtāṭu miṭattik kuṟittatōveṉa aiyuṟavum iṭa muṇṭu. āyiṉum, āyntu nōkkuvārkku, mēṟkāṭṭiya varalāṟē uṇmiyāṉa teṉpatu pulaṉām.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, kaḻakam eṉpatu kuḻu eṉṉum vēraṭi yāyp piṟantateṉṟum, kūṭṭam allatu kūṭumiṭam eṉpatiyē aṭippaṭip poruḷākak koṇṭateṉṟum, mutaṟkaṇ kaṟṟōraviyi allatu kaṟpōr kūṭṭattik kuṟittē piṉṉarc cūtāṭumiṭattik kuṟittateṉṟum, caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟ poruḷil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya tūya paḻan tamiḻc colleṉṟum, paṇṭiyilakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantu paṭṭa ikkālattil tirukkuṟaḷik koṇṭu coṟ poruḷ variciyik kāṇamuṭiyāteṉṟum collārāycci yiṉ tuṇiyiṉālēyē atit tuṇital kūṭumeṉṟum, teṟ ṟeṉat terintukoḷka. (teṉmoḻi)
kaḻañcu
kaḻañcu - kalañca
kaḻaṅku - kaḻañcu = oruniṟi. kaḻaṅku = kaḻaṟcikkāy. (t.mū.ï.) (va.va: 111)
kaḻiyal viḷiyāṭṭu
oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyiṉar, kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil neruṅki niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoru kiyilum ovvoru kuṟuṅkaḻi ēnti avaṟṟip piṟar kaḻikaḷōṭu tākkiyaṭittu, piṉṉip piṉṉiyum cuṟṟic cuṟṟiyum varum āṭṭu, kaḻiyal eṉappaṭum.
itu pakalilum āṭaṟkuriyatāyiṉum, potuvāka iravilēyē āṭappeṟum. itaṟkum kummikkup pōl oru taṉivakip pāṭṭuṇṭu. atu,
taṉṉaṉa taṉṉaṉa taṉṉāṉa - taṉa
taṉṉaṉa tāṉaṉa taṉṉāṉa
- eṉṉum vaṇṇam paṟṟiyatākum.
kaḻivu vaki
cakki muntiri elāmicci mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaḻivu;
kotakku puḷiyiṉ kaḻivu;
kūnti paṉaṅkāyiṉ kaḻivu;
kōtu karumpiṉ kaḻivu.  (col: 69)
kaḷ
kaḷ eṉṉum mutaṉili muṟkālattil kaḷavu ceytalik kuṟittatē. kaḷḷam, kaḷḷattaṉam, kaḷḷaṉ, kaḷavu eṉṉum coṟkaḷi nōkkuka. piṟkālattil kaḷ eṉṉum mutaṉili taṉ poruḷi iḻantapiṉ, toḻiṟ peyaroṭu tuṇiviṉi cērtta kaḷavu cey eṉṉum kūṭṭuc col mutaṉili tōṉ ṟiṟṟu. kaṭkuvāṉ parikkil ñēluvāṉ parikkēṇam eṉṉum maliyāḷap paḻamoḻiyiyum nōkkuka. (ti.ma. 166.)
kaḷ uṇṇāmi
nīrākavō kaṭṭiyākavō pukiyākavō iruntu, veṟiyiṉāl uṇarvi maṟikkum poruḷkaḷi uṇṇāmi.
kaḷḷutal maṟittal. ivviṉi iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
kaḷ - kaḷḷam = maṟippu. kaḷ - kaḷavu = maṟippu.
kaḷ eṉṉum peyar munnilip poruṭkum (nīr, kaṭṭi, puki) potuvēṉum, peruvaḻakkup paṟṟi nīrvaṭivāṉa poruḷiyē kuṟikkum. atu iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyum eṉa iruvakittu muṉṉatu paṉi teṉṉi mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu iṟakkuvatu, piṉṉatu arici kāykaṉi mutaliyavaṟṟip puḷikka vittuk kāycciyum eṭuppatu. (ti.ma. ati. 93.)
kaḷmayakku
aimpulaṉum aṭaṅki aṭiyōṭu uṇarvu iḻattalum, vāykā vātu maṟiveḷippaṭuttalum, pittar pōṟpitaṟṟalum, āṭi vilakalum, aṟṟam maṟiyāmiyum, tī nāṟṟam vīcutalum, vāy nuri taḷḷutalum, vaḻiyil kiṭattalum, vaḻip pōkkar paḻitta lum, īmoyttalum, iḷañciṟār cirittalum (kaḷmayakkam ākkum). (ti.ma. 923)
kaḷvaṉ
kaḷvaṉ - kalam
kaḷḷutal = tiruṭutal. kaḷ - kaḷavu - kaḷvu - kaḷvaṉ. (va.va: 111)
kaḷaṅkam
kaḷaṅkam - kalaṅkam
kal - kaḷ = karumi. kaḷ - kaḷam = karumi. kaḷam - kaḷaṅku = karu mi, kuṟṟam. kaḷaṅku - kaḷaṅkam = karumi, kaṟi, maṟu, kuṟṟam. kaḷaṅkaṉ = maṟuvuḷḷa mati.
mā.vi.a. mūlam aiyuṟaviṟkiṭamāṉatu (“etym. doubtful”) eṉṟu, kuṟittiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. (va.va: 111)
kaḷac ceyal
kaṇṇit tōṇṭi viṭuvēṉ mūḷiyi uṟiñci viṭuvēṉ
 tōli urittu viṭuvēṉ īralit tiṉṟuviṭuvēṉ
kuṭali māliyākap pōṭṭuviṭuvēṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa accuṟuttukkaḷ, anākarikak kālap pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷil ceyalaḷa vāy nikaḻntaviyē. (col. 25.)
kaḷappāḷar (kaḷappirar)
(tōrā. ki.pi. 300 - 590)
kaṭikkaḻaka muṭiviṟkuppiṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭi munnūṟṟāṇṭu āṇṭa, kaḷappāḷar vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavareṉṟum, pal vakuppiṉareṉṟum, palavāṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. avar palvēḷviccāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti pirāmaṇarkku aḷitta vēḷvikkuṭip paṭṭiyatti maṟuttataṉālum, muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṉar eṉṟu collappaṭuvataṉālum, tamiḻarākavum iruntirukkalām. kaḷapputal kāṭu veṭṭit tiruttutal, nelli māvaṭṭattiṟ caṅkaranayiṉār kōvil vaṭṭattil, kaḷappāḷar kuḷam eṉṟu ōr ūr uḷḷatu. kaḷappilār eṉpatu kaḷḷar vakuppāriṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka, paṇṭitar na.mu. vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭār kuṟittuḷḷār.
kaḷam
kaḷam1 - kala (kh) - i.vē.
kaḷḷutal = kūṭutal. kaḷ - kaḷam = uḻavar kūṭi vēli ceyyumiṭam .
kaḷam - kaḷamar = uḻavar. ērkkaḷam, pōrkkaḷam (pōraṭikku miṭam) eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka. kaḷam - kaḷaṉ - kaḻaṉi = vayal.
kaḷam2 - kaḷa (g)
kaḷḷutal = kalattal, poruntutal. kaḷ - kaḷam = taliyi uṭaluṭaṉ poruttum kaḻuttu, toṇṭi.
pāṭukaḷa makaḷirum  (cilap. 6: 157).
kaḷam - (kaḷattu) - kaḻuttu.
vaṭavar kal eṉṉum ceyaṟkiyaṭiyi kru (viḻuṅku) eṉṉum colliṉ tiripākak koṇṭu, kala eṉpataṟku viḻuṅkum uṟuppu eṉṟu poruṭkāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvar. (va.va: 111 - 112)
kaḷi
kaḷi(ma.) - kēl - viḷiyāṭu.
kaḷittal = viḷiyāṭutal. kaḷi = viḷiyāṭṭu. kaḷi - kīl (pirā.) - kēl. (va.va: 112)
kaḷi
kaḷi - klam = kaḷi.
kaḷittal = aluttal, ayartal, iḷittal.
klam = cram eṉṟu poruntāvāṟu poruttik kāṭṭuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. (va.va: 112)
kaṟkālam  (Stong Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 5,00,000 - 50,000.)
muntiyal tamiḻarāṉa anākarika māntar kallāṟ palvēṟu karuvi kaḷic ceytu koṇṭa kālam kaṟkālamākum. atu paḻaṅkaṟ kālam, putukkaṟkālam eṉa iru piriviṉatu.
(1) paḻaṅ kaṟkālam (Old Stone Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 5,00,000 - 1,00,000.)
tamiḻariṉ mutaṟkāla muṉṉōrāṉa kumarināṭṭu māntar, poṉṉam (metal) oṉṟum kaṇṭupiṭikku muṉ, killi (kalli), veṭṭi, kutti, katti, uḷi, cutti (cuttiyal), camaṭṭi (cammaṭṭi), kuntam, kūntālam, kōṭari, kuttuk kōṭari mutaliya karuvi kaḷik kallāl muraṭṭu vēlippāṭākac ceytu, payaṉpaṭutti vanta kālam paḻaṅkaṟkālamākum.
avar vāḻnta iṭam kumarimalit toṭariṉ aṭivāramāṉa kuṟiñci nilam.
avar ceyta toḻil kāykaṉi paṟittal, kiḻaṅkakaḻtal, tēṉeṭuttal, vēṭṭiyāṭal eṉpaṉa. kiḻaṅku tōṇṭaṟkuk kōṇalillāta kompiyum kūrākac cetukkiya vaṉkucci 
yum, vēṭṭiyāṭaṟkuk kalliyum kuṟuntaṭiyiyum neṭuntaṭiyiyum payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar uṇṭavuṇavu pacciyum cuṭṭaṉavumāṉa iyaṟki viḷivu kaḷum vēṭṭik kaṟiyum ākum. vēṉiṟ kālattil mūṅkilum maraṅkaḷum urāyntu paṟṟiya neruppil akappaṭṭuc cetta vilaṅku paṟaviyūṉ, cuviyākavum metuvākavum iruntatu kaṇṭu, vēṭṭikkaṟiyic cuṭṭut tiṉṉum paḻakkatti mēṟkoṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar uṭuttiya uṭi, kōri taḻit toṭiyum tiyili yum, marappaṭṭiyum vilaṅkuttōlumākum. āppirikka anākarika māntar, innūṟṟāṇṭu muṟpakutiyilum, cila marappaṭṭikaḷi ūṟavittut taṭṭi virivākki āṭiyāka aṇintaṉar. kumarināṭu teṉṉāppirikkāvuṭaṉ iṇin tiruntataṉāl, kumarināṭṭu māntarum anākarika niliyil attakiya maravuriyi aṇintiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar uṟiyuḷ (taṅkumiṭam) malikkuki, palkavar marakkavaṭu, paraṇ, kallāl amitta vaḷimaṟi (kāṟṟi maṟikkum ciṟu kuṭil) ākiyavi.
avar aṇinta aṇikaḷ, marukkoḻuntu pōlum naṟuntaḻi, maṇamuḷḷa allatu aḻakiya malar, mayiṟpīli, cēvaliṟaku, pulippal tāli mutaliyaṉa.
avar puḻaṅkiya nīrkkalam mūṅkil nāḻiyum curik kuṭukki pōṉṟa neṟṟuk kūṭum.
avar maṇamuṟi, paruvam vantapiṉ, perumpāṉmi āṇ pālār, orōviṭattup peṇpālār, iṇiviḻiccu vēṭki nērnta pōtellām etirp pālāri icivittō iciyumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyō valintō puṇarum potumaṇam (Promiscuity) ākum. kūṭivāḻum kuṭumpa vāḻvu avariṭi yilli. ataṉāl, oru talivaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭa vāḻvum (Community Life) avariṭiyilli. vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷum pōl, pakalil uṇavutēṭiyuṇpatum, uṟiviṭattil irāttaṅkuvatumē avar iyalpāyiruntatu.
koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷ etirppaṭṭapōtu allatu iruppiṭam vantu tākkiyapōtu, avar tammiṭamuḷḷa kaṟkaruviyun kanta maramuṅ koṇṭu cilavaṟṟi etirttuk koṉṟiruttal vēṇ ṭum; kollamuṭiyāta valiyavaṟṟiṟku marattiṉ mītēṟiyō putaruḷḷum potumparuḷḷum maṟintō tappiyiruttal vēṇṭum; arimāvum yāṉiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟi iravuk kālattil tī vaḷarttu viraṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum. vilaṅkuka ḷoṭum pakivaroṭum pōriṭa nākarip pōl taliyiluṅ kompukaḷi aṇintirukkalām.
mutaṟkālattil tīyik kaṇṭu mikamika añciṉārēṉum, piṉpu mella mella ataṉ palvakip payaṉpāṭṭik kaṇṭu, vilaṅ kāṇṭi māntarum atip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉar. aṇukātum akalātu miruntu, kuḷirkāytal, iṟicci cuṭutal, iruḷ nīkkal, kuḷaviyik kalittut tēṉeṭuttal, koṭu vilaṅku veruṭṭal mutaliyaṉa tīyiṉ payaṉkaḷ. nimirnta kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecanthropus Erectus), eṉṉum cāli (cāvaka) māntaṉiṉum muntiyavaṉāṉa pīkkiṉ māntaṉ eṉṉum cīṉa māntaṉ (Sinanthropus Pekinensis), neruppit taṉ kukiyil vittup payaṉpaṭuttiyatākac collappaṭuvatāl, cālimān taṉi yotta kumarimāntaṉ neruppip payaṉpaṭuttiyatil viyappoṉṟumilli.
naṉmiyō tīmiyō iraṇṭumō ceytavaṟṟiyum ceyva tākak karutappaṭṭavaṟṟiyum, mutaṟkāla māntar teyvamāka vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. avi tī, katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, iṟantōr āvi pēy, nākam mutaliyaṉa. teyvam eṉṉum peyar tīyik kuṟikkuñ colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatē. maraṅkaḷiṉ urāyvu, iṭi, miṉṉal ākiya mūvakikaḷil iyaṟkit tī uṇṭā yiruttal vēṇṭum. tīppaṟṟi eriyuṅ kiḷikaḷiṉiṉṟu, koḷḷik kaṭṭikaḷip piritteṭuttuk koṇṭupōyp paḻaṅkaṟ kāla māntar payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
marantoṟum, malitoṟum, nīrnilitoṟum āvi allatu pēy kuṭikoṇṭiruntatākavum, paḻaṅkāla māntar nampiṉar.
iṟantavaruṭampik kuṭiyiruppiṟkuc caṟṟut tolivāṉa iṭattiluḷḷa kuḻiyiliṭṭu, kākaṅkaḻukum nariyōriyum tiṉṉā vāṟu maṇṇāl mūṭiviṭuvatu avar vaḻakkam.
moḻittuṟiyil, iyaṟki moḻi (Natural Language) allatu muḻittal moḻi (Inarticulate Speech) eṉṉum muntu moḻik kuriya.
(1) uṇarcci yolikaḷ (Emotional Sounds)
(2) viḷiyolikaḷ (Vocative Sounds)
(3) oppolikaḷ (Imitative Sounds)
(4) kuṟippolikaḷ (Symbolic Sounds)
(5) vāycceyki yolikaḷ (Sounds produced with oral gestures)
(6) cuṭṭolikaḷ (Deictic Sounds)
ākiya aṟuvaki yolikaḷum, paḻaṅkaṟkālattilēyē muṟiyē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉ iṟutik kālattil āttirēliyā nōkkiyum teṉṉāppirikkā nōkkiyum kumari māntar paṭarntiruttal vēṇṭum. nālvaki yeḻuttil mutalatāṉa paṭaveḻuttu (Picture writing or Pictograph) paḻaṅkaṟ kālattil tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
iyaṟkiyuṇavu tēṭat terintatum, eḷiya uṟiyuḷ amitta tum, aṟṟam maṟittatum, aḻakuṇarcci tōṉṟiyatum, tīyip payaṉpaṭuttiyatum, iyaṟkimoḻi vaḷarttatum, paṭaveḻut tippayaṉ paṭuttiyatum paḻaṅkaṟkāla māntariṉ aṟivunili yeṉṉalām.
(2) putuk kaṟkālam (New Stone Age)
tōrā. ki.mu. 1,00,000 - 50,000
muntiyal kumari māntar, paḻaṅ kaṟkālak karuvikaḷit tiṇkaṉak karuṅkallāl, vaḻavaḻappākavum mikak kūriyaṉavākavum ceytu koṇṭa kālamputuk kaṟkālamākum.
avar vāḻnta iṭam kuṟiñciyum atiyaṭutta mulliyumākum. vāḻkkit tiruttattālum māntar tokip perukkālum, iyaṟkiyākavē avar mulli nilattilum paraviyiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar ceyta toḻil, perumpāṉmi kālnaṭi vaḷarppum vāṉāvārip payir viḷippum; ciṟupāṉmi paḻaṅkaṟkāla māntar ceytaṉa.
maliyaṭi vāraṅkaḷilum malimēlum vāḻum āṭu māṭu erumi ākiya mūvilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷiyum, avar piṭittup paḻakki vīṭṭu vilaṅkākkiṉar. mūṉṟum pāl tantaṉa. āṭu pāl taruva toṭu ūṉuṇavumāyiṟṟu. kāḷimāṭum kaṭāverumiyum ēruḻaviṟkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. māṉiyum āmāṉiyum kāṭṭup paṉṟiyiyum muyaliyum uṭumpiyum piṭittu ūṉuṇaviṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iṉṟu paṉṟiyiṟicci yuṇpavar orucārārē. avariṉuñ ciṟutokiyiṉar māṭṭiṟicci yuṇpavar. paṟavi kaḷuḷ, kōḻi, puṟā, kuyil, kāṭi, katuvāli ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ūṉi virumpiyuṇṭaṉar. kāṭṭukkōḻiyip paḻakki vīṭṭuk kōḻiyumākkiṉar.
vāṉāvārip payirkaḷ eṉpaṉa, ērkkāṭum kottukkāṭu mākiya nilattil maḻiyiṉālēyē viḷinta, tiṉi varaku pōṉṟa ciṟutavacaṅkaḷum avari tuvari pōṉṟa payaṟu vaki kaḷumākum. pulveḷikaḷāyuḷḷavaṟṟik kālnaṭi mēycca lukku viṭṭuviṭṭu, kuṟuṅkāṭum peruṅkāṭumā yuḷḷavaṟṟit tīyiṉāṟ cuṭṭerittuk koṉṟu viḷi nilamākkiya viṭam, piṟkālattiṟ kolli yeṉappaṭṭatu. aṭarnta marañceṭi koṭikaḷāl iruṇṭu kiṭavātu veṭṭaveḷiyāṉa nilamel lām, kaṇṇiṟkup pulaṉāṉa tiṉāl, pulam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pulantoṟum parappiya tēriṉi rāyiṉum  (puṟam, 10, mēṟpaṭi ; 12)
pulaṅkeṭa viṟukkum varampil tāṉi  (mēṟpaṭi 16-9)
 eṉṉum aṭikaḷi nōkkuka.
viḷi nilattilum vīṭṭilumuḷḷa kūlaṅkaḷi elikaḷ tiṉṟu keṭuttataṉāl, avaṟṟik kollak kāṭṭup pūṉiyiyum; āṭukaḷi narikaḷ piṭittut tiṉṟataṉāl, avaṟṟi viraṭṭi mantiyik kākkak kāṭṭu nāyiyum; vīṭṭiṟ paḻakkiṉar.
nilattiṟ pūcippūci metteṉṟu naṭappatāl, pūṉi pūciyeṉap paṭṭatu. pūcu - pūci - pūṉi - pūñi. nāykku āṭpaṟṟuppōl pūṉikku iṭappaṟṟu mikuntiruppatāl, tolvaravuṇarcci mikka āṇpūṉi paruttuk koḻutta niliyil iṉṟum kāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, kaṇṭār añcattakka verukāki viṭukiṉṟatu.
vevvāy verukiṉip pūci eṉṟalum  (tol. mara. 69)
verukku viṭiyaṉṉa veruṇōkku  (puṟam. 324)
piḷḷi verukiṉ muḷḷeyiṟu puriya  (mēṟpaṭi 117)
eṉpavaṟṟāl, tamiḻakattiṟ kāṭṭuppūṉi toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntuvaruvati aṟiyalām.
iṉi, mēliyāriyap pūṉip peyarkaḷ tamiḻccolliṉ tiripāyiruttalāl, tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟē pūṉi airōppāviṟkuc ceṉṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
pūci - E. puss - pussy. MLG. pus, Norw. puse, Du. pces itaṉ mūlam teriyavilli yeṉṟum, orukāl mutaṟkālattil itu oru pūṉiviḷic collāyiruntirukkalāmeṉṟum, erutan tuṟi (Oxford) āṅkila akaramutali kūṟukiṉṟatu. nelli vaṭṭārat til iṉṟum pūṉiyip pūcupūcu eṉṟu aḻippatik kāṇalām.
kotti (ka.) - E. cat, OE. catte, ME, catt(e), LL. cattus, catta, ONF, cat, F. chat, ON. kottr, OHG. kazza. kotti = kuruṭu kuruṭaṉ. pūṉikkup pakaliṟ cariyākak kaṇ teriyāmiyāṟ kotti yeṉappaṭṭatu.
mulli nilatti uḻak kalappiyiyum, vēṭṭiyāṭaṟku vaḷari - vaṇari (vaḷi taṭi), kavaṇ, vil mutaliya karuvikaḷiyuṅ kiyāṇṭaṉar. kallōṭu, maram, kompu, maruppu (tantam), elumpu mutaliyaṉavum mutaṟkaruviyākappayaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. māṭṭuk kompum erumik kompum yāṉi maruppumē mutaṟkaṇ vaḷitaṭiyākap payaṉpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. avi kiṭiyāviṭattē avi pōṉṟa vaḷitaṭikaḷip payaṉ paṭuttiyiruppar.
mulli nilap pāṟikaḷiluḷḷa paḷḷaṅkaḷilum kuḻikaḷilu muḷḷa nīr, katiravaṉ vemmiyālum kāṭṭut tīyālum kāynta pōtu, atiṟkiṭanta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ cuṭṭavuṇa viṉum paruttum meṉmiyākiyum cuvimikkum iruntatik kaṇṭa māntar, kaṟkalaṅkaḷil varaku tiṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ariciyic cōṟākkavum, avari tuvari mutaliya payaṟukaḷi avikkavum, kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. paṭṭa maraṅkaḷ urāyntu aṭikkaṭi neruppeḻak kaṇṭatiṉāl, iyaṟki neruppillāta pōtu ñelikōlāṟ kaṭintu ceyaṟki neruppiyum uṇṭākkik koṇṭaṉar. vīṭutoṟum nāḷtoṟum vēḷi toṟum tīkkaṭiyat tēviyillāvāṟu, ūr muḻuvataṟkum potuvāka ōr iṭattil iravum pakalum kaṭṭiyeriya viṭṭu aviyā neruppip pēṇiyiruttalum vēṇṭum.
uṭukka maravuri pōṉṟa nārāṭiyum, pōrttikkoḷḷa āṭṭu mayirk kampaḷiyum, mutaṟkaṇ kippiṉṉalākavum piṉṉart taṟinecavākavum, avar ceytu koṇṭaṉar. paṭukka mūṅkiṟ pāyum ōlippāyum muṭintu koṇṭaṉar.
cantaṉac cēṟṟālum vaṇṇaccāntālum, irupālārum, ciṟappākap peṇṭir, mēṉimuḻutum palvēṟu ōviyam varintu koḷvatum, niliyāyirukkumāṟu palvaki yuruvaṅkaḷip pacci kuttik koḷvatum, peruvaḻakkāyiruntatu.
kuṭiyirukka vaṭṭamāṉa kūri vīṭukaḷik kaṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. mutalil marattilum marattaṭiyilum vatintataṉāl, vaṭṭamākak kiḷikaḷ paṭarntum nilattiṟpaṭintumuḷḷa marattiṉ pōṅkip piṉpaṟṟi, vīṭṭiṉ vaṭivi amittatākat terikiṉṟatu. cuṟṟuc cuvarik kalluḷḷa viṭattil maṭcāntupūcik kallālum, aḵtillāviṭattil maṇṇālum, amittaṉar.
kūlaṅkaḷum kāykaṟikaḷum pōṉṟa keṭṭip poruḷkaḷi iṭṭuvippataṟku, paṉinārp peṭṭikaḷum mūṅkiṟ kūṭikaḷum muṭiyappaṭṭaṉa. nīriyum nīrpporuḷkaḷiyum vārttu vippataṟku, mūṅkil nāḻiyum marattiṟ kaṭintu koṇṭa kaṭikālum āṭṭut tōṟpiyum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. camippataṟkuk kaṟkalam utaviṟṟu. kallutal tōṇṭu tal, allatu kuṭital. kallappaṭṭa ēṉam kalam eṉṉap paṭṭatu.
āṭumāṭu uṭumpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tōli marattilum kalattiṉ vāyilum kaṭṭi ularttiyapōtu, kuccuṅkiyum paṭṭu iṉṉōci yeḻuntatik kaṇṭu tuṭitoṇṭakam mutaliya tōlicik karuvikaḷum; vaṇṭiṉāl tuḷikkappaṭṭa mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyilum nāṇal taṭṭiyilum kāṟṟuppukuntapōtu, iṉitāy olittatik kaṇṭu pullāṅkuḻalum; muṟukakkaṭṭiya villiṉ nāṇ teṟittapōtu, iṉṉici piṟantatik kaṇṭu vil yāḻ eṉṉum narappicik karuviyum; nāḷaṭiviṟ putuk kaṟkāla māntar puṉintu koṇṭaṉar.
iṉpattiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi, vēḷikkuvēḷi uṇṭi camikka vum, toḻilukkut tuṇiyāyirukkavum, uṭimikaḷip pātukākkavum, nōyniliyil nalam pēṇavum, oru peṇ niliyāka vīṭṭilirukka vēṇṭiyiruntatāl; vīṭṭu vāḻkki ēṟpaṭṭapōtē, ōr āṭavaṉum peṇṭum kūṭivāḻum kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyum ēṟpaṭṭatu. atu ilvāḻkkiyeṉṟu potu vākac collappaṭiṉum, kūṭṭu vāḻkkikkum kumukāya vāḻkkikkum uriya iṉṟiyamiyāta aṟaṅkaḷit taḻuviya tāl, illaṟam eṉac ciṟappittuc collappeṟum.
akkālam ilvāḻkkit toṭakkak kālamātalāl, paṉmaṉi maṇamum (polygamy) tīrvi (divorce) muṟiyum peruvaḻakkā yiruntaṉa. peṟṟōrum peṇṇum iciyāta viṭattu vaṉkavar vum kaḷḷakkaṭattamum, cūḷurittup peṇṇoṭu kaḷavākak kūṭiyapiṉ cūḷi maṟuttalum niṟivēṟṟāmiyum, palaraṟiyak kūṭiyapiṉ oru peṇṇik kiviṭutalum, aṭikkaṭi nikaḻntaṉa.
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyuṅ kūṭiyē makappeṟiṉum, veḷippaṭi yākac cūlkoṇṭu pattu mātam iṭarppaṭṭuc cumantu perunōvoṭu piḷḷi peṟuvatāl, piḷḷikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa urimiyum atikāramum, neṭuṅkālam tāykkē iruntu vantatu. itil tantimār tamakkum paṅkukōṭaṟku, īṉiyaṟpaṭukki (Couvade) eṉṉum oru valakkārattik kiyāṇṭaṉar. atāvatu, maṉivi piḷḷi peṟṟavuṭaṉ, kaṇavaṉum avaḷip pōl nōvuṟṟatāka naṭittuppaṭuttukkoṇṭu. taṉakku makappēṟṟu maruttuvam pārkkac colvatu. kuṟattipiḷḷi peṟa, kuṟavaṉ kāyan tiṉṟāṉām. eṉṉum tamiḻp paḻamoḻi, iṉṟum, paṇṭi āppirikka māntariṉ vaḻakkatti yotta viṉi kumari nāṭṭilum iruntatik kuṟippāka uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
ilvāḻkkiyum niliyāṉa kūṭṭukkuṭiyiruppum ēṟpaṭṭa taṉāl, āṅkāṅkup paṟpala vūrkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiṉa. ovvōr ūrilum kuṭivāṇar perumpālum paltalimuṟip paṭṭa orē māperuṅ kuṭumpa vuṟaviṉarāyiruntataṉāl, akkuṭumpa mutiyōṉē talivaṉāyiruntu, kuṟṟavaḻakkut tīrttut taṇṭittum muṟi ceytum vantāṉ. akkālattil makkaḷ vāḻvu nīṇṭiruntataṉāl, makaṉ, tanti, pāṭṭaṉ, pūṭṭaṉ, ōṭṭaṉ (cēyāṉ) eṉṉum aintalimuṟiyiṉarum orē kālattil vāḻntiruntaṉar.
āṭumāṭukaṭkup pulviḷiyavum vāṉāvārip payirkaḷi viḷivikkavum maḻi iṉṟiyamiyātatā yiruntataṉāl, maḻit teyvam allatu mukil teyvam paḻaṅkaṟkālat teyvaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ putitākavum ciṟappākavum vaṇaṅkappeṟṟatu. kumari moḻi, muḻittalmoḻi niliyiṉiṉṟu iḻittal moḻi yil (Articulate Speech),
 (1) acinili (Isolating or Monosyllabic Stage)
 (2) puṇarnili (Compounding Stage)
 (3) koḷuvunili (Agglutinative Stage)
 (4) pakucoṉṉili (Inflexional Stage)
eṉṉum nālnilikaḷip putukkaṟkālam muṭiyumuṉ kaṭan tiruttal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu karutteḻuttut (Ideograph) tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
acinilikkālattiṟ cīṉariṉ muṉṉōrum, koḷuvu nilik kālattiṟ cittiyariṉ muṉṉōrum, pakucoṉṉilik kālat toṭakkattiṟ cumēriyariṉ muṉṉōrum, kumari nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu pirintu pōyiruttal vēṇṭum.
cumēriyar pirintupōṉati irāmaccantira tīṭcitar eḻutiya tamiḻariṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum (Origin and Spread of the Tamils) eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu teḷika, nakar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ūr eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya nakarp peyar tamiḻāyiruppatiyum, appaṉ eṉṉum muṟip peyar kaltēyar (Chaldees) allatu pāpilōṉiyar moḻiyil āp eṉṟu tirintiruppatiyum, nōkkuka.
tīyuṇṭākkal, camittal, vāṉāvārip payir viḷittal, kāl naṭi vaḷarppu, necavu, ilvāḻkki, ūrāṭci, iḻittal moḻiyamittal, karutteḻuttip payaṉpaṭuttal eṉpaṉa putukkaṟkāla māntariṉ aṟivu niliyik kāṭṭum.
kaṟkāla māntariṭi vakuppu vēṟupāṭiṉmi
kaṟkāla māntar paḻaṅkaṟkālattiṟ kuṟiñci nilattilum putukkaṟ kālattiṟ kuṟiñciyilum atiyaṭutta mulli nilattilum vatintārēṉum, tiṇi nilam paṟṟiyō toḻil paṟṟiyō teyva vaṇakkam paṟṟiyō, avariṭi evvaki vakuppu vēṟu pāṭum iruntatilli. ellārum ellāt toḻilum ceytu orē vakiyāy vāḻntu orē vakuppāyiruntaṉar. paḻaṅkaṟkālat tāḻvu niliyum putukkaṟkāla uyarvu niliyum ellārkkum potuvākum.
nīlamaliyiluḷḷa kōttar pal toḻilum terintavarāy orē vakuppārāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṭavar ovvoruvarum uḻavu, kālnaṭivaḷarppu, necavu, vaṇikam, taccu, kol, taṭṭāra vēli, calavi, mañcikam (muṭitiruttam) ākiya vāḻkkip paṇikaḷ aṉiyavum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. toki paṟṟiyaṉṟit toḻil paṟṟi oruvarkkum iṉṉoruvar utavi vēṇṭi yatilli. iṅṅaṉamē kaṟkāla māntarum vāḻntiruttal vēṇṭum.
kaṟkālaṅ kaḻintu maruta nila vāḻvu toṭaṅkiya piṉṉarē, toḻiṟ pirivum atupaṟṟiya vakuppu vēṟupāṭun tōṉṟiṉa.
kaṟpu
kaṟpu eṉpatu kaṟpōl uṟutiyāṉa irupāliṭik kātaṟ paṇpu. kal - kaṟpu. atu maṇapparuvam vari tōṉṟātiruntu piṉpu oruvariyē kātalippatu. irupāṟkum potuvāṉatu. ātaliṉ maṉiviyiyaṉṟi aṇaṅkiyum nōkkāta āṇ kaṟpum, kaṇavaṉiyaṉṟik kāvalaṉiyum nōkkāta peṇkaṟpum eṉak kaṟpu iru tiṟappaṭṭatām.
kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyiṉ kaṇṇiyam
ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyirvari ellā vuyirkaḷum perum pālum āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupāliṉa. iv virupāluḷ etu ciṟantatu eṉṉum viṉāviṟku viṭiyiṟuppatu aritākum. āṇum peṇṇumākap paṭikkappaṭṭa ellā vuyirkaḷum irupāluṅ kūṭivāḻvatē iṟivaṉ tirunōkka mātalāṉum, ak kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyāṉēyē ulakam iṭi yaṟātu iyaṅki varutalāṉum, av vāḻkkiyil irupālum oṉṟukkoṉṟu tuṇiyāy otta urimiya vātalāṉum, ovvoṉṟum ēṉiya tāṟ ceyyappaṭāta orucār kaṭamiyi mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷami yāṉum, irupālum camam eṉpatē koḷḷattakkatām. ik karuttup paṟṟiyē, mutaṟṟāyāṉa ēvāḷ, mutaṟṟantiyāṉa ātāmiṉ (aṭiyu muṭiyu mallātu iṭippaṭṭa) vilāvelumpi ṉiṉṟu uṇṭākkappaṭṭatākak kiṟittavamaṟi kūṟum.
perumiyum uraṉum āṭuu mēṉa
accamum nāṇum maṭaṉumun tuṟutta
niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriya veṉpa  (tol. kaḷavu. 7, 8)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatu. kaḷavoḻukkattil talivaṉ taliyiṭattuc ciṟantutōṉṟum kuṇaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, irupālukkumuḷḷa ēṟṟat tāḻvaṉṟu. ik kuṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟumuḷḷaṉavāyiṉ, kaḷaviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭātu iruki kōḷukkum potuvāṉa akat tiṇi yiyal allatu poruḷiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum: aṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭāmi kāṇka.
kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭu maṇanikaḻu maṭṭum pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi ākiya iruvakik kuṟiyilum talivaṉ ciṟitum añcātu perumiyōṭum uraṉōṭum ceṉṟu mīḷvatum, talivikkut talivaṉ mītuḷḷa aṉpu kāraṇamāka āṟṟarumipaṟṟi accan tōṉṟuvatum, kāmakkuṟippu allatu kaḷavu veḷippāṭupaṟṟi nāṇam piṟattalum, ceviliyar koḷuttakkoṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmiyākiya aṟiyāmaṭamum, kūṭṭatti nikaḻttiyiruntum aṟiyātatu pōṉṟa aṟimaṭamum nikaḻvatu muṇṭu. talivaṉukku makkaḷum vilaṅkum naccuyirum ākiyavaṟṟāl nērakkūṭiya cētattuṭaṉ. avaṉ teyvatti nōkkiyiṭṭa cūḷurip poyppāl varakkūṭiya ūṟṟukkum talivi añciyiruntami.
antarat teḻutiya eḻuttiṉ māṉa
vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa oḻukalum  (kaṟpiyal. 5)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya aṭikaḷāl uṇaralām.
mēṟkūṟiya irupāṟ kuṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iraṇṭoṉṟu kūṭṭi, aṟivu niṟi ōrppu kaṭippiṭi eṉṉum nāṉkum āṇpāṟ kuṇameṉṟum, accam maṭam nāṇam payirppu eṉṉum nāṉkum peṇpāṟ kuṇameṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar piṉṉōr.
aṟivoṭu niṟiyē yōrppuk
kaṭippiṭi kuṇanāṉ kāmē
nāṇamē maṭamē yacca nāṭṭiya
payirppu nāṉkum
māṇiḻi maṭanallārkku vitta
nāṟkuṇaṅkaḷākum  (cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu. 12: 16, 17)
avviyum kākkipāṭiṉiyumpōlum pulaviyarum, taṭātaki yum maṅkiyumpōlum araciyarum, kaṇṇakiyum tilakavatiyum pōlum kaṟpuṭiyārum. kavuntiyum maṇimēkaliyum pōlum tuṟaviyarum innāṭṭilum iruntirukka, aṟivu mutaliya naṟ kuṇaṅkaḷi āṇpāṟkē ciṟappākak kūṟiyatu, innāṭṭup peṇṭirkku iṟceṟippu kāraṇamāka aṟivuk kuṟivum peṇṭirkkellām iyalpākavuḷḷa meṉmiyum paṟṟiyē yām.
iṟceṟippu peṇṭiriṉ oḻukkakkāppum uyirkkāppum nōkkiyatu. perumpālum veḷiyēṟātu vīṭṭukkuḷḷēyē yiruppavarkku. allatu, ayaliṭañ cellātu uḷḷūrilēyē vatipavarkku aṟivu maṭṭāyiruttal mikuti. namnāṭṭiṟ peṇṭir ciṟṟiḷam paruvattilēyē illaṟattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭu vantamiyiṉ, avarkku uyartarak kalvikkup pōtiya vāyppu miruntatilli. itaṉāl, oppunōkkiya muṟiyil, perumpāṉ mipaṟṟi maṭattip peṇpāṟkurittākkip pēti maṭanti maṭavaral mutaliya peyarkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉar.
potuvākap peṇṭirkkup pūppaṭintavuṭaṉ maṇa nikaḻnta miyiṉ perumpālum patiṉeṭṭu allatu irupatāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kaṇavaṉ, taṉ iḷamaṉiviyip pēti allatu maṭanti yeṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟṟamilli. atu. añcaṉ maṭavaṉamē eṉṟāṟ pōla iḷami nōkkiya arumiviḷiyēyaṉṟi, aṟiyāmi nōkkiya iḻippu viḷiyaṉṟu. mēlum, namnāṭṭil āṭavariṉ maṇapparuvattiṟku variyēyilli; maṇat tokikkum elliyilli. peṇṭirkkō kimpeṇ ṇāyiṉ maṟumaṇa milli yeṉṟum, vāḻnāḷ muḻutum oruvaṉiyē maṇakka vēṇṭum eṉṟum varampuḷatu. itaṉāl kaṇavaṉiṉ mūppum maṉiviyiṉ ciṟṟiḷamiyum peruvaḻakkām, maṭam eṉṉuñ collukku iḷami yeṉṟum poruḷ. iḷamiyil aṟivu mutirāmiyiṉ, eḻutiṟa iḷampeṇ paruvaṅkaḷil mutalatāṉa pētip paruvam aintu mutal ēḻāṇṭu varippaṭṭatātaliyum nōkkuka. itaṉāl, iḷamiyum iṟceṟippum aṟimaṭamum paṟṟiyatē peṇṭir maṭam eṉavum. atu ettuṇiyum iḻivu kuṟitta taṉṟeṉavum, āṭavarum aṟivillākkāl pētip peyarkkuriyar eṉavum. āṉmāvi āṇavamalam piṇittiruppatupōlap peṇ pāliyē pētimikkuṇam iyalpāyp paṟṟik koṇṭiruppataṉ ṟeṉavum aṟintu koḷka.
ōti yuṇarntum piṟarkkurittun tāṉaṭaṅkāp
pētiyiṟ pētiyā ril  (kuṟaḷ. 834)
eṉa irupāṟ potuvāyum.
aṟaṉkaṭi niṉṟāru ḷellām piṟaṉkaṭi
niṉṟāriṟ pētiyā ril  (kuṟaḷ. 142)
eṉa āṭavariyē cuṭṭiyum tiruvaḷḷuvar pētiyeṉṉum peyari vaḻaṅkiyami kāṇka. iṉi peṇṭirkku meṉmit taṉmi ciṟakka, āṭavarkku iṉpañ ciṟappatām. ammeṉmit taṉmikku aṇikalamāyiruppatu pētimiyām. pētimi yeṉpatu mātarkkaṇikalam. peṇṭirkku iyalpākavuḷḷa meṉmit taṉmiyāl irakkam ciṟantu. tuṉpaṅ kaṇṭa viṭattup piṟarporuṭṭu accan tōṉṟum ēṉi nāṇamum payirppum ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷeṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭā.
iṉi, āṭavarkku vaṉmiyum peṇṭirkku meṉmiyum ciṟappiyalātalāṉum, meṉmiyilum vaṉmiyē ciṟantatā talāṉum, āṭavarē ciṟantavar eṉiṉ. av vaṉmi meṉmi yākiya iraṇṭum oṉṟiyoṉṟu iṉṟiyamiyāmiyāṉum, payaṉpāṭṭaḷavil iraṇṭum ottiruttalāṉum meṉmiyit taḻuvit tāṅkuvatē vaṉmiyiṉ payaṉātalāṉum, meṉmiyē iṉpan taruvatā yiruttalāṉum. iru pālarum orupālarē yeṉak kūṟi viṭukka.
 iṅṅaṉam irupālarum orupāla rallareṉiṉ.
 oṉṟē vēṟē yeṉṟiru pālvayiṉ
 oṉṟi uyarnta pāla tāṇiyiṉ
 otta kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyum kāṇpa.  (tol. kaḷavu. 2)
 atuvē, tāṉē yavaḷē tamiyar kāṇak
 kāmap puṇarcci yiruvayi ṉottal.  (iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ . 2)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷ poruḷaṟṟaṉa veṉka.
mēṟkūṟiya accam mutaliya nāṟkuṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ maṇāḷaṉ allatu kaṇavaṉ mītuḷḷa aṉpuñ cērntatu kātal. kātal vēṟu. kaṟpu vēṟu. kātalil kaṟpuṇṭu; kaṟpil kātaliruppatu yāppuṟaviṉṟu. vāḻnāḷ muḻutum maṇam virumpātavaḷukkum tuṟaviyāyiruppavaḷukkum kaṟpirukkalām; āṉāl kātalirātu. acca mutaliya nāṟkuṇaṅkaḷuḷ. nāṇam payirppu eṉṉum iraṇṭum kaṟpā yamiyum; āṉāl kātalāyamivatu oṉṟu milli. ākavē, acca mutaliya nāṉkum (oḻukkamuḷḷa) peṇ pāṟ potukkuṇamum, kātaloṉṟum (aṉpuḷḷa) maṉivi yiṉ ciṟappuk kuṇamumākum.
kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyākiya iruvarkkum kātal oru paṭittāy iṉṟiyamiyātatu. atu illaṟa vāḻkkikkuriya naṟkuṇaṅka ḷellān tiraṇṭu muḻuniṟivāṉatu. atu iru kikōḷukkum potu. cilarkkuk kaḷavilēyē toṭaṅkalām; cilarkkuk kaṟpil maṭṭum toṭaṅkalām. kaṟpellām kātalaṟṟateṉṟu koḷvatu tavaṟu. kaḷavil veḷippaṭa vāyppillāta kātal, kaṟpiltāṉ veḷippaṭum. attakik kātal, karaṇamum vāyilum peṟṟatēṉum, kākki uṭkārap paṉampaḻam viḻuntatu pōṉṟatē. kaḷavilum cūḷuṟavākiya karaṇamum, pāṅkaṉum pāṅkiyumākiya vāyilkaḷumuṇṭē! kaḷaviṟku allatu kātalukku meyyuṟu puṇarcci iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭuva tiṉṟu; uḷḷap puṇarcciyē pōtum.
 uḷḷap puṇarcciyu meyyuṟu puṇarcciyuṅ
 kaḷḷap puṇarcciyum kātalark kuriya.  (nampiyakapporuḷ. 34)
uḷḷap puṇarcciyiyum meyyuṟupuṇarcciyō ṭoppak koṇṭamiyāṉēyē, tilakavatiyār pōṉṟa paṇṭik kaṟpuṭi mātar, maṇappēccu nikaḻntu maṇam nikaḻātavaḻiyum kaṇava ṉiṟantaviṭattu uyir nīkkavum kimmi nōṟkavum talippaṭṭaṉar. uyarntōr uḷḷap puṇarcciyālum iṉpam nukarvar; tāḻntōrē meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṉāya iṉpamē vēṇṭuvar.
iṉi maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum maṇappēcciṟku muṉ oruvari yoruvar oruvakiyālum aṟiyātavarā yiruppiṉum, maṇap pēccuvaḻi aṟintavarēyāvar. avariṭik kātal tōṉṟu māyiṉ irumpik kāntamiḻuppatu pōlum, kaṭṭiyil tīppaṟṟuvatupōlum oruvar maṉatti oruvar maṉam iḻuttup paṟṟikkoḷḷum. vēṭṭañceṉṟa tali makaṉum puṉaṅkātta talimakaḷum kāṭṭil oruvari yoruvar kaṇṭatu pōṉṟē. maṇappēcciṟkuriya maṇamakkaḷum vīṭṭil oruvariyoruvar kāṇkiṉṟaṉar; kaṇṇāṟ kāṇum vāyppiṉṟēl karuttā lēṉuṅ kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. kātalukkuk kāṇuvatuṅ kaṇṇuvatum oṉṟē. uḷḷattāl maṭṭum kalakkum naṭpiṟkē puṇarcciyum paḻakutalum vēṇṭātu uṇarcciyē pōtumeṉiṉ, uḷḷat tālum uṭalālum iraṇṭaṟak kalakkum kātalukku uṇarcci pōtumeṉac collavum vēṇṭumō?
kātal vāḻkkikku allatu uṇmiyāṉa maṇavāḻkkikku, vāyilum karaṇamum iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭupa valla.
kaṇṇōṭu kaṇṇiṉi nōkkokkiṉ vāyccoṟkaḷ
eṉṉa payaṉu mila  (kuṟaḷ. 1100)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kōviyil mutalāvatu kūṟappaṭum iyaṟkip puṇarcci, talivaṉun taliviyum koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟit tāmē tamiyarāyk kūṭip puṇarum teyvap puṇarcciyē. ataṉpiṉ attakip puṇarccikku iṭamiṉmiyāṉēyē, pāṅkaṉum pāṅkiyumākiya vāyilkaḷ vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vāyil kaḷālum iyalātavaḻi kaṟpu allatu karaṇam vēṇṭap paṭukiṉṟatu.
uṇmiyāṉa kaṟpāvatu kaḷavu veḷippāṭē yaṉṟik karaṇa maṉṟu. mutaṟkālattil karaṇamillāmalē makkaḷ illaṟam naṭātti vantaṉar. makappēṟu kātali veḷippaṭuttiviṭu mātaliṉ, vāḻnāḷ muḻutum kaḷavoḻukkam iyaluvataṉṟu. ākiyāl, muṉpu kaḷavum piṉpu kaṟpumāka maṇavāḻkki iru pakutippaṭṭatu.
paruva makaṉum makaḷum mutalāvatu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam, kāmap puṇarcci, kātaṟ puṇarcci eṉa iru vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu kāmattāl maṭṭum nikaḻntu piṉpu tīrvatu; piṉṉatu kāmattōṭu kūṭiya aṉpāl nikaḻntu kālamellām nīṭuvatu. itu eṇṇātu (teyvattāl) nikaḻvatum eṇṇi nikaḻvatum eṉa iruvakittu, iv viruvakiyilum kātal kari puraṇṭōṭi ettuṇit taṭikaḷiyun takartteṟintu iṟutivari iṉpañ ciṟappatē. mutaṟkālattil naṭanta kaḷavellām kātaṟ puṇarcciyākavēyiruntatu. piṉpu, iṭikkālattil cila pala kaḷavukaḷ kāmap puṇarcciyā yoḻiyavē. muṉivarāl karaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatu.
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇa meṉpa  (tol. poruḷ. kaṟ. 4)
āyiṉum, karaṇattāl maṭṭum poyyum vaḻuvum tīrntuviṭātu. uṇmiyāṉa kātal vāḻkki karaṇamaṟṟatēṉum paḻikkap paṭātu. alaṅkāramum makkaṭ kūṭṭamum iṉṉiciyum uṇṭāṭṭum ūrvalamum variciyaḷippum ākiya āravāramē karaṇattic ciṟappippatu, iv vāravāram caṟṟum aṟṟatāyiṉ karaṇamum potumakkaḷāl ciṟitu paḻikkappaṭuvatē. karaṇattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa vāḻkkiyilum poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟumāyiṉ atuvuṅ karaṇamaṟṟatē. karaṇavaḻippaṭṭa pala illaṟa vāḻkki yil poyyum vaḻuvum iṉṟum kaṇkūṭākak kāṇkiṟōm. karaṇamaṟṟatāyiṉum uṇmiyāṉa kātal vāḻkki yāyiṉ iṉṟum paḻikkappaṭātu ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakkatē.
makkaḷ immiyil aṭiyakkūṭiya iṉpaṅkaḷuḷ tali ciṟantatu peṇṇāl varuvatu.
kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyum aimpulaṉum
oṇṭoṭi kaṇṇē yuḷa.  (kuṟaḷ. 1111)
atuvuṅ kātalōṭu, kūṭiyatāyiṉ ciṟṟiṉpamēṉum pēriṉ pattiṟ kiṇiyākak kūṟattakkatām.
tāmvīḻvār meṉṟōṭ ṭuyili ṉiṉitukol
tāmarik kaṇṇā ṉulaku  (kuṟaḷ. 1103)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum,
ellā vuyirkkum iṉpam eṉpatu
tāṉamarntu varuu mēvaṟ ṟākum  (tol. poruḷiyal. 28)
ik kātaliṉpa vāḻkkiyil vaṟumiyilli. nōyilli, tuṉpamilli, kavaliyilli, oru kuṟiyumilli. illateṉ illavaḷ māṇpāṉāl eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. ettāl vāḻalām ottāl vāḻalām. kātalaṉum kātaliyum tāṉeṉ-ṟum avaḷeṉṟum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi ōruyirum īruṭalumāka oṉṟi nukarum iṉpam iruvarkkum potu vēṉum, kātalaṉē mikutiyāka nukarkiṉṟāṉ.
itaṉālēyē,
vilaṅkalik kālviṇṭu mēṉmē liṭaviṇṇu maṇṇu muṉnīrk
kalaṅkalic ceṉṟa vaṉṟuṅ kalaṅkāy  (tirukkō. 24)
tilliccivaṉṟāḷām poṟṟaṭamalar cūṭum  (tirukkō. 21)
āṟṟalakaṟṟap peṟṟu,
maṭukkō kaṭaliṉ viṭutimi laṉṟi maṟitirimīṉ
paṭukkō paṇilam palakuḷik kōparaṉ ṟillimuṉṟiṟ
koṭukkō vaḷimaṟṟu nummiyark kāyakuṟṟēval ceykō
toṭukkō paṇiyī raṇiyīr malarnuñ curikuḻaṟkē  (tirukkō. 63)
eṉa eḷivantum iḻivantum kuṟṟēval ceyyavum.
muḻaṅkā rarimuraṇ vāraṇa vēṭṭicey moyyiruḷvāy
vaḻaṅkā vatariṉ vaḻaṅku  (tirukkō. 157)
maṭalēṟiyum ēṟutaḻuvalum pulippāl kaṟattalum kōḷariyik kollutalum mutaliya maṟacceyal ceytum maṇakkavum, karaṇattiṉ piṉ putalvaṉip peṟṟapiṉpum maṉiviyik kāluṅ kiyum piṭittu vēṇṭavum (tirukkō. 390) tuṇivatu.
iṅṅaṉam kātali allatu kaṟpuṭi maṉivi mutalāvatu taṉ kātalaṉukku iṇiyaṟṟa, iṉpam payakkiṉṟāḷ.
iraṇṭāvatu, kaṇavaṉ kaḷavil kūṭṭan taṭippaṭṭavaḻi maṭa lēṟiyum pitaṟṟiyum, niṟiyaḻintu perumiyiḻanta viṭattum, kātali niṟi yaḻiyātu aṭakka voṭukkamāyiruk kiṉṟāḷ.
kaṭalaṉṉa kāma muḻantum maṭalēṟāp
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka til.  (kuṟaḷ. 1137)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
ettiṇi maruṅkiṉum makaṭūu maṭalmēl
poṟpuṭi neṟimi iṉmi yāṉa  (akattiṇi iyal. 38)
ceṟivu niṟivuñ cemmiyuñ ceppu
maṟivu marumiyum peṇpā lāṉa  (poruḷiyal. 15)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
ākavē, aṟivu, niṟi, ōrppu, kaṭippiṭi eṉṉum nāṉkum āṇpāṟkēyuriyaviyalla eṉpatu veḷiyām. aṟivuḷḷa viṭattil ēṉi mūṉṟum amiyum. peṇpāṟkum aṟivuṇ ṭeṉpatu iruvaki vaḻakkilum kaṇṭatē. kātalaṉ taṉṉik kippaṟṟiyaviṭattuk kattiṉum, tāy vantapōtu avaṉukkut taṇṇīr vikkiyateṉṟu colvatu. aṟivō? aṟiviṉmiyō? peṇṭiriṭik kalvi paraviṉ avarum āṭavarpōl aṟivu peṟutal tiṇṇam.
mūṉṟāvatu, karaṇattiṉpiṉ kaṇavaṉ taṉṉik kiviṭṭup parattiyiṟ pirintaviṭattum maṟumaṇañ ceytaviṭattum, kātal maṉivi avaṉ māṭṭu kaṭukaḷavuṅ kātal kuṉṟātirukkiṉ ṟāḷ. avaṉ āṇi poyttaṉimittam avaṉukkut tīṅku nērātapaṭi teyvattiyum iravum pakalum vēṇṭukiṉṟāḷ. taṉ peṟṟōriyum viṭṭuviṭṭu, ettuṇic ciṟantavaṉā yiṉum vēṟoruvaṉiyum kaṉavilum karutātu. taṉṉiyē teyvamāka iṟukap paṟṟik koṇṭirukkum melliyali maṟantu avaḷ uyirōṭirukkumpōtē avaḷ kaṇṇetirē vēṟoruttiyi maṇakkum āṭavaṉ vaṉmitāṉ eṉṉē!
nāṉkāvatu, talivaṉ taṉakku ettuṇit tīṅku ceyyiṉum atip poruṭpaṭuttātum taṉṉalaṅ karutātum aṉṉiyum aṭiyāḷum amicciyumpōlap palvakiyilum avaṉukkup paṇiviṭi ceykiṉṟāḷ. kaṟpuṭi maṉiyāḷtāṉ kaṇavaṉuk keṉṟē vāḻntu avaṉillātapōtu alaṅkarippatum, ciṟakka uṇṭuṭut tuṟaṅkuvatum oḻikiṉṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ taṉakkuc ceyyuṅ koṭumiyit tāy kūṟiṉum kaṭintu avaṉ iyaṟpaṭa moḻi kiṉṟāḷ.
aintāvatu, irappōrk kīntum aṭiyārp pēṇiyum viruntōmpiyum piṟarkkut toṇṭu ceykiṉṟāḷ kaṟpuṭi yillāḷ.
āṟāvatu, pañca kālattum maḻi poḻivikkavum, tēviyāṉa viṭattu ākkavum aḻikkavum āṟṟaluṭiyavaḷāyirukkiṉ ṟāḷ kaṟpuṭippeṇ. iv vāṟṟal, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam maṇa vāḻkki ākiya munnilik kaṟpuṭip peṇṭirkkum potu vām. illaṟattiliruntu koṇṭē teyvattaṉmi peṟavum, tuṟaviyiyum vekuḷavum valla kaṟpuṭiyāḷariṉ perumi kaṭṭurikkun tiṟattatō!
ēḻāvatu kaṟpuṭi maṉivi taṉ kaṇavaṉiṟantatum uyir nīkkiṟāḷ ; illāviṭiṉ kimmi nōṟkiṟāḷ. ittakik kaṟpuṭip peṇṇi maṉiviyākap peṟuvatē ulakil   oruvar peṟattakka pēṟukaḷil taliciṟantatām.
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka yāvuḷa kaṟpeṉṉum
tiṇmi yuṇṭākap peṟiṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 154)
pukaḻpurin tillilōrk killi yikaḻvārmuṉ
ēṟupōṟ pīṭu naṭi  (kuṟaḷ. 59)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
eṉṉoṭu porutu meṉpa avari
ārama ralaṟat tākkit tēro
ṭavarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṟ ciṟanta
pērama ruṇka ṇivaḷiṉum pirika!  (puṟam. 71)
 eṉpatu olliyūr tanta pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ vañciṉam.
kaṟpuṭip peṇṇi yuṭimipaṟṟip palavaracar pulavarāṟ pārāṭṭap peṟṟaṉar.
ceyirtīr kaṟpiṟ cēyiḻi kaṇava  (puṟam. 31)
ōṭuṅkī rōtik koṭuṅkuḻi kaṇava 
āṉṟōḷ kaṇava
puriyōḷ kaṇava  (patiṟṟuppattu. 14, 55, 70)
kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyi iṅṅaṉam kaṇavaṉukku viḻuccelva mākap pārāṭṭiyatōṭu nillātu. avaḷi iṟantapiṉ teyva mākavum vaṇaṅkiṉar muṉṉōr.
uricāl pattiṉik kuyarntō rēttalum  (cilap.)
eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, kumari mutal paṉimalivari taṉ āṇiyic celuttiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum piṟavaracarum kaṇṇa kikkuk kōyileṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. turōpati yammaṉ occāṇṭammi mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷum kaṟput teyvaṅkaḷē. maṟacceyal purinta kaṟpuṭip peṇṇit teyvamāka vaṇaṅ kum vaḻakkamē. kaṭavuḷiṉ āṟṟalip peṇ pālāka uruvakik kavum pōrt teyvattip peṇpālākak koḷḷavuṅ kāraṇamāyiruntirukkalām.
pōriliṟanta ciṟanta maṟavarum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉarēṉum, avarit tāḻntōraṉṟi uyarntō rētta itukāṟuṅ kaṇṭilam. ākiyāl peṇpāl enniliyiṉum āṇ pālilun tāḻnta taṉ ṟeṉpatum kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyē oruvaṉukku immiyil taliciṟanta pēṟeṉpatum maṟak koṇāta muṭipukaḷām.
paṭṭiṉattārum civappirakācarum pōṉṟa aṟiñar peṇṭirip paḻittuk kūṟiyatellām, vilimakaḷiriṉ tīyoḻukkamum vīṭupēṟṟiṟkut taṭiyāṉa ulakak kavarcciyumpaṟṟiyē yaṉṟip peṇpāliṉ iḻivupaṟṟi yaṉṟu.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, āṇ peṇṇākiya irupālum ōraṉṉa niliyaveṉṟum irupālukkum iyaṟkiyilēyē perumiyilli yeṉṟum, peṇpālariṟpōl āṇ pālariṉum iḻintāruṇ ṭeṉṟum, kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyē maṉiviyeṉṟum, kātal vāḻkkiyē vāḻkkiyeṉṟum, illaṟaviṉpa vāḻkkiyi naṭāttupavar kaṇavaṉ maṉivi yākiya iruvarumē yātaliṉ avarkkup piṟar tuṇi iṉṟi yamiyāta taṉṟeṉṟum, maṉapporuttamē maṇap porutta meṉṟum, uṇmiyāṉa maṇam kūṭṭamēyaṉṟik karaṇamaṉ ṟeṉṟum, kātal kūṭiyapiṉ iṟappiṉālaṉṟip pirippilli yeṉṟum, kātal vāḻkki maṇṇulaki viṇṇulakākkuva teṉṟum, ituvē muṉṉit tamiḻarkaṇṭa aṉpu neṟi akapporuḷiṉpa vāḻkki yeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka!
kuṟippu: kaṟpuḷḷa maṉiviyum kaṇavaṉiṟantapiṉ maṟumaṇañ ceyyalā meṉpatu ikkālak karuttām. maṉivi yiṟantapiṉ kaṇavaṉ maṟumaṇañ ceyyum pōtu, maṉivikku maṭṭum eṉṉō taṭi! nāvalantēyattil āṭavarāṟ peṇṭirkkuc ceyyappaṭuṅ koṭumi aḷavaṟṟatākum. peṇṭiriṉ aṭimittaṉattip pōkkiyapiṉpē āṭavar tam aṭimit taṉattip pōkkaṟpālar. toṉṟutoṭṭu varum peṇṭiriṉ aṭimittaṉamē peṇpālit tāḻvākak karutak kāraṇa mākum. āṉāl uṇmiyil irupālarum oṉṟē. meṉmi yālākum iṉpatti yellām nukarntukoṇṭu ati vaṉmiyāl oṭukkuvatu muṟiyō? kaṇavaṉiṟantapiṉ kaṟpuṭi maṉivi uyirit tuṟappatu aḷavukku miñciyatākak karutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. kapilarum, picirāntiyum poyyāmoḻiyum pōṉṟa vaṉmipeṟṟa āṭavarē naṭpurimi kāraṇamākat tam naṇpar iṟantavuṭaṉ uyirit tuṟappārāyiṉ, meṉmiyiṟ ciṟanta kaṇṇakiyum pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ ṟēviyum pōṉṟa melliyalār tam kaṇavariṟantavuṭaṉ uyirit tuṟavātu vēṟēṉ ceyvar? āyiṉum, pulavarellām tam naṇpariṟantāl uyirviṭu vārallar; atupōṉṟu kaṟpuṭiya maṉivi yārellām taṉ kaṇavariṟanta piṉ uyirviṭuvā rallar. īriṭattum uyir viṭuva taṟkuk kāraṇam, uṇarcci mikutiyē. aḵtuḷvaḻi pōṟṟap paṭiṉum, ivvaḻi ikaḻappaṭātu.  (cakuntalā niṉivu malar.)
kaṉṉaṭam
 kaṉṉaṭam eṉṉum peyar
maliyāḷattiṟku aṭuttut tamiḻōṭu toṭarpuḷḷatu kaṉṉaṭam, kaṉṉaṭam eṉpatu karunāṭam eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripu. itu mutalāvatu kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭik kuṟittup piṉpu aṅku vaḻaṅkum moḻiyik kuṟittatu. itaṉ paḻiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ karunāṭu, karunāṭakam eṉpaṉa. kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭār karunāṭar eṉṟum, karunaṭar eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭār.
karunaṭam allatu karunāṭakam eṉṉum collukku iru poruḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi (1) kariya nāṭu, (2) karuṅkūttu eṉpaṉa.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟ perumpakuti karical nilamāyiruppatāl, kariya nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭaṉar kuṇṭeṭ paṇṭitarum (Dr. Gundert) kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyārum.
kūttukaḷil iḻinta vakikkuk karuṅkūttu eṉṟu peyar.
 mutupārppāṉ vīḻkkip peruṅkaruṅ kūttu   (kalit. 65: 29)
 naṭam = kūttu. naṭaṉ = kūttaṉ
vaḷinaṭaṉ melliṇarp pūṅkoṭi mēvara nuṭaṅka (paripā. 22: 42)
 naṭar = kūttar.
viṭarum tūrttarum naṭarum uḷḷiṭṭa  (kuṟaḷ. parimē. uri)
nāṭakam = kati taḻuvivarum kūttu. mikap paḻimiyāṉa anākarika allatu kaṇmūṭip paḻakkattip paḻiya karu nāṭakam eṉpar; iṅkuk karunāṭakam eṉpatu paḻimi yāṉa anākarikattik kuṟikkalām. ākavē, karunaṭam allatu karunāṭakam eṉṉum peyar karuṅkūttu nikaḻum nāṭu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭatāyirukkalām.
cilappatikārattil karunāṭar kuṟikkappaṭum pōtellām tiruntāmiyik kuṟikkum koṭu eṉṉum aṭi koṭuttē kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅkaru nāṭar  (cilap. 25: 156)
koṅkaṇak kūttaruṅ koṭuṅkaru nāṭarum  (cilap. 26: 106)
iṉṟum yakṣakāṉam eṉṉum karuṅkūttu kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil naṭikkappaṭṭu varuvatākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āyiṉum karunaṭarik karunāṭar eṉṉum vaḻakkum uṇmi yāṉum, kūttākiya kāraṇattiṉum nilavakiyākiya kāraṇam peyarppēṟṟiṟkuc ciṟattalāṉum, karicaṟpāṅkāṉa nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvatē poruttamām. kari nāṭu eṉpatu karunāṭu eṉa maruviṟṟeṉpar cilar.
 kaṉṉaṭanāṭ ṭelli
teṟkē nīlamali (nīlakiri) yiliruntu vaṭakkē pīṭārp (Bidar) pakuti vari. micūr kaṉṉaṭam teṉmarāṭṭam (makārāṣṭiram) aitarāpāttiṉ teṉmēṟkup pakuti ākiya cīmikaḷiṟ perum pālum tāymoḻiyākap pēcappaṭuvatu kaṉṉaṭam.
nīlamaliyiluḷḷa paṭakar (vaṭakar), eṉṉum malivāṇar kaṉṉaṭattiṉ tiripāṉa oru moḻiyip pēcukiṉṟaṉar.
16ām nūṟṟāṇṭil teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vanta makamatiya maṉṉar, aṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉmēṟ karunaṭa maṉṉar āṇi celutti yami kāraṇamākat tamiḻnāṭṭiyum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭōṭu cērttuk karnāṭṭak (karunāṭakam) eṉṟaṉar. ati āṅki lēyar karnāṭṭik (Carnatic) eṉṟu tirittaṉar.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭu varalāṟu
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟ ciṟanta pakuti micūrc cīmiyākum. micūril taṟpōtu haḷepīṭu (Helebid) eṉa vaḻaṅkum tuvari nakā (tuvāra camuttiram) ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarc centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērntatāyiruntatu. itu tuvarāpati eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, akattiyaṉār..... tuvarāpatip pōntu nilaṅ kaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇal vaḻikkaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmariyum patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷiruḷḷiṭṭāriyum aruvāḷariyuṅ koṇṭu pōntu kāṭukeṭuttu nāṭākki eṉat tol kāppiyap pāyiravuriyilum, maliya mātavaṉ nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇaluḻi narapatiyaruṭaṉ koṇarnta patiṉeṇ vakik kuṭippiṟanta vēḷirkkum eṉa akattiṇi yiyal 32 ām nūṟpāvuriyilum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
paṇṭic cēranāṭṭil micūrc cīmiyiṉ teṉ pakutiyum cērntiruntatu.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil micūrt tuvarinakari āṇṭavaṉ iruṅkōvēḷ eṉṉum tamiḻac ciṟṟaracaṉ. avaṉ vaṭapakkat til oru muṉivariṉ ōma kuṇṭattil tōṉṟit tuvari nakari nāṟpattoṉpatu talimuṟiyākat toṉṟutoṭṭu āṇṭu vanta vēḷirkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ eṉṟum, oru muṉivar tavañceytu koṇṭirukkiyil avarkku iṭiyūṟu ceyyavanta oru puliyi avar ēvaṟpaṭi koṉṟamiyāl pulikaṭi māl eṉap paṭṭāṉeṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvaṉ.
piṟkālattil 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tuvāracamuttirattil (Halebid) niṟuvappaṭṭa hoycaḷa pallāḷa marapu kaṭikkaḻakak kālap pulikaṭi māliṉ vaḻiyatē. pallāḷaṉ eṉṉum peyar vallāḷaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripu. va-pa, pōli, ō. nō: vaṇṭi - paṇṭi, vaku - paku, vallāḷaṉ = valiya āṇmiyi yuṭiyavaṉ. oru maṟavaṉuṭiya illiyum ūriyum iyalpiyum colli avaṉ āṇmit taṉmiyi mikuttuk kūṟum puṟattuṟikku vallāṇmulli (pu.ve. 177) eṉṟu peyar.
naḷḷātār miṭalcāytta
vallāḷaniṉ makiḻirukkiyē  (puṟam. 125)
eṉat tērvaṇ maliyaṉum,
vicitteṟi kūṭamoṭu porūum
ulikkal laṉṉa vallā ḷaṉṉē  (puṟam. 170)
eṉap piṭṭaṅkoṟṟaṉum,
aṭi poliyak kaḻaṟiiya
vallāḷaṉi vayavēntē  (puṟam. 40)
eṉac cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉum pāṭappaṭṭaṉar.
kaṭuntiṟamiyuḷḷa irupālāriyum vallāḷa kaṇṭaṉ vallāḷa kaṇṭi eṉap pukaḻvatu iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭulakavaḻakku.
tiruvaṇṇāmaliyil vallāḷa makārācaṉ eṉṉum ōr aracaṉ iṭikkālattil āṇṭatāka aruṇācalapurāṇam kūṟum.
mēṟkut toṭarcci malikku mēṟkum arapik kaṭalukkuk kiḻakkum kūrccarattiṟkut teṟkum kōvāvukku vaṭakkumāka vuḷḷa koṅkaṇa tēcam paṇṭik kālattil koṭuntamiḻa nāṭukaḷuṉ oṉṟāyiruntatāka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ kūṟum.
kaṉṉit teṉkarik kaṭpaḻan tīvam
ciṅkaḷam kollam kūviḷam eṉṉum
elliyiṉ puṟattavum īḻam pallavam
kaṉṉaṭam vaṭuku kaliṅkam teliṅkam
koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟam
eṉpaṉa kuṭapāl irupuṟac ciyat
tuṭaṉuṟipu paḻakun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum
muṭiyuṭi mūvarum iṭunila vāṭci
aracu mēmpaṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭikaḷ
patiṉmarum uṭaṉirup piruvarum paṭitta
paṉṉiru ticiyil coṉṉaya muṭiyavum
eṉṟār akattiyaṉār eṉpatu naṉṉūl mayili nātar uri. (pa. 161.) iv akattiya nūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭum peyarkaḷ mutalāvatu nāṭṭik kuṟittavi yeṉṟum piṉpu moḻi tirintapiṉ moḻiyik kuṟittaṉaveṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
cēranāṭu kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē kuṭa (mēṟkut toṭarcci) malikku mēṟpāl vēṟum kīḻppāl vēṟumākap pirintu pōyiṟṟu. kīḻppāl nāṭu mīṇṭum teṟkil koṅku nāṭum vaṭakkil kaṅkanāṭum iṭiyil atikināṭu tuvarināṭu mutali yaṉavumākap pirintuviṭṭatu. atikināṭu takaṭūri (iṟṟit tarmapuriyi)t talinakarākak koṇṭu atikamāṉ marapiṉar āṇṭu vantatu, kaṅkanāṭu, ataṉ vaṭakkil kaṅkamarapiṉar kuvaḷāla puratti (kōlār)t talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭu vantatu. itu kaṅkapāṭi eṉṟu kalveṭṭukaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
vēṅki nāṭuṅ kaṅka pāṭiyam  (S.S.I. I. 94)
kaṅka marapiṉarāṉa ciṟṟaracar kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilē, maṟattiṟ ciṟantu peyar peṟṟavarāyiruntaṉar.
naṉṉa ṉēṟṟi ṟaṟumpū ṇatti
tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭuntiṟaṟ kaṅkaṉ kaṭṭi  (akam. 44)
paṅkaḷar kaṅkar palvēṟ kaṭṭiyar  (cilap. 25: 157)
eṉap paḻiya nūlkaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka.
ik kaṅka marapic cērntavaṉē, 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ kālattaṉum, amarāparaṇaṉ srīmat kuvaḷālapura paramēcuvaraṉ, kaṅkakulōṟpavaṉ eṉṟu taṉ meykkīrttikaḷiṟ pārāṭṭappeṟupavaṉum, pavaṇanti muṉi varik koṇṭu naṉṉūli ākkuvittavaṉumākiya cīyakaṅkaṉ eṉpavaṉ. ivaṉ oru tamiḻilakkaṇatti iyaṟṟu vittati ṉālum, naṉṉūṟ ciṟappuppāyiram,
kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭam
eṉunāṉ kelliyiṉ iruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ
eṉṟu kūṟuvatālum, micūr nāṭṭiṉ vēṅkaṭa nēr elli vari 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyumāvatu tamiḻ tavira vēṟoru moḻiyum vaḻaṅkavilli yeṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum.
ākavē, pampāy mākāṇamum aitarāpāttuc cīmiyum 
ceṉṉi mākāṇamum kūṭukiṉṟa iṭattuk koṭuntamiḻ vaḻakku, vaṭacoṟ kalappāl 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ kaṉṉaṭameṉa vēṟu moḻiyākap pirintu, piṉpu ciṟitu ciṟi tākat teṟkē taḷḷivantu taṟpōtu nīlamali vari paraviyuḷḷa teṉka.
koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅkaru nāṭar
koṅkaṇak kūttaruṅ koṭuṅkaru nāṭarum
eṉṟu cilappatikāraṅ kūṟuvatāl koṅkaṇa nāṭṭic cērnta karunaṭa nāṭṭilēyē kaṉṉaṭam mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ippōtu koṅkaṇa nāṭṭiṟkum malapārukkum iṭippaṭṭa mēlkari nāṭē, teṉ kaṉṉaṭam vaṭakaṉṉaṭam eṉa iru pakutiyāyp pakukkappaṭṭuk kaṉṉaṭam (kannada) eṉ ṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. vaṭakaṉṉaṭam koṅkaṇa nāṭṭup pakutiyē, itaṉāl, karināṭu eṉpatē karunāṭu eṉa maruviṟṟu eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
koṅkaṇam eṉpatu koṇkaṉam eṉṉum colliṉ tiripu. koṇkaṉ eṉpatu neytalnilat talivaṉ peyar. koṅ kaṇam oru neytal nāṭātalāl, koṇku (neytalnilam) eṉṉum peyar muṟiyē koṇkaṉam - koṅkaṇam eṉṟu tirintirukkalām. koṅkaṇa nāṭṭiṉar, koṅkaṇar eṉṟum koṅkaṇavar eṉṟum koṅkaṇiyar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvar. koṅkaṇar eṉṟu oru paṇṭit tamiḻc cittar iruntār.
micūrc cīmiyiluḷḷa palavūrkaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻc coṟ kaḷiyē tam peyarīṟākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
(e-ṭu): ūr (micūr), puram (kuvaḷālapuram), paḷḷi allatu haḷḷi (cikṉavakaṉ haḷḷi), paṭṭiṉam (cīraṅkapaṭṭiṉam), kara (aricikkari), pēṭṭi mutaliyaṉa. kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷa kuṭar (kavuṇṭar), tikaḻar (tamiḻar), kuṟumpar, uppārar, korakar (kuṟavar), holiyar (puliyar), pēṭar (vēṭar) mutaliya kulattiṉar tamiḻ marapiṉarē. kaṉṉaṭiyar eṉṉum peyarum moḻipaṟṟik kulaṅkuṟitta tamiḻccollē, micūr aracariṉ iṟpeyarāṉa uṭiyār eṉpatu taṉit tamiḻc collāyiruppatu mika mika makiḻattakkatu.
koṅkaṇa muṉivar vācukiyammiyārāl kaṭintu koḷḷap paṭṭatiṉālō, koṅkaṇa nāṭṭāriṉ iyalpāṉa pētami yālō, koṅkaṇaṉ koṅkaṇavaṉ koṅkaṇiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ pētimi kuṟitta peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṉṉaṭam tirintataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1)	kaṉṉaṭac cīmiyil tamiḻ maṉṉarāṭci oḻintami.
(2)	aṅkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷ vaḻaṅkāmi.
(3) 	tamiḻar viḻippiṉmi.
(4)	taṭpaveppa niliyāl tamiḻ oliyum collum tirintami.
(5)	vaṭacoṟ kalappum vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇa vamippum.
 kaṉṉaṭam tirinta muṟikaḷ
(1) 	olittiripu
 	e-ṭu: pa-ha, paḷḷi - haḷḷi, pāṭu - hāṭu.

	e-ṭu: etir - eturu, iruntēṉ - irutteṉe.
(3)	tokuttal tiripu
	e-ṭu: iruntēṉ - ittēṉ, iruvar - ipparu.

	e-ṭu: niṉakku - niṉake, ōlikkāraṉ - ōlkāra.

	e-ṭu: motalāṉaya, mattontu (maṟṟoṉṟu) illa.

 			avukaḷu, ivukaḷu

 3. muṟip peyar
	tamiḻ 	kaṉṉaṭam 	tamiḻ 	kaṉṉaṭam

ticic coṟkaḷ
muttappa allatu muttātta = pūṭṭaṉ (pāṭṭaṉ tanti), muttamma allatu muttajji = pūṭṭi (pāṭṭitāy), ajja = pāṭṭaṉ, ajji = pāṭṭi, toṭṭappa = periyappaṉ. toṭṭamma allatu toṭṭavva = periyammi, cikkappa = ciṟṟappaṉ, cikkamma allatu cikkavva = ciṟiya tāy.
kuṟippu: mutu + appa = muttappa, accaṉ - ajjaṉ, acci - ajji, toṭṭa (tōṭu) = periya, cikka = ciṟiya.

eṇṇaṭi uyartiṇip peyar
x¥gD (xUt‹), x¥gS (bg.gh.), opparu (oruvar), ipparu (iruvar), mūvaru, nālvaru, aivaru, nālkumanti (nāṉ māntar), kelavaru (cilar), halavaru (palar),
 22. nōyp peyar
huṇṇu (puṇ), paṉi, cuḷukku, rūru, tuṟi (coṟi)

kuruṭi (kuruṭu), kūṉ, kiviṭu (ceviṭu)
 24. palporuṭ peyar
uṇṭe (uṇṭi), añce (añcal = tapāl), aṭayāḷa (aṭiyāḷam), mañcu, maṇṇu, ucuru (uyirppu = cuvācam), cava (cavam). nūlu, tuṇṭu, hoke (puki), hecaru (peyar), tūḷ (tūḷi), kōlu (kōl), pūti (puḻuti), kurutu (kuṟi), kāṇikke (kāṇikki), kampa (kampam), ptare (tiri), koṇṭi halike (palaki), nora (nuri), uyyal (ūcal), coṭar (cuṭar), cuttam (cuṟṟam), cēla (cāḷi), puṭi (poṭi), pāṭike (vāṭaki), hore (poṟi), kata (katavu), miñccu (miṉṉal), oṭam paṭikke, maraḷu (maṇal), kaṭiyāra, campaḷa, maḻe, kallu, haṇavu (paṇam), kumpa, tuṭṭu, oṭave (uṭimi), aṭṭi, kūṭu carakku, piḷakku (viḷakku), kācu, koḷḷi, haruku (paluku), paḷe (vaḷi), vāṭikke (vāṭikki), kūṭu, accu, ale (ali), āki (ālaṅkaṭṭi), āvi, oṭampi (uṭampu), oṭal (uṭal), ucir (uyir), uruḷi, iruḷ, erupu (eru); iṭaru, ekumati (ēṟṟumati ), au (oḷi), olaka (olakkam), kaṭṭe (kaṭṭi), kaṭavaḷ (kaṭavuḷ), katavu, karuṭi (karaṭi = cilampam), kari, kaḻu, kāppu, kāre (kāri = cāntu), kuṭikke (kuṭukki), kuṇṭa (kuṇṭu), kuttike (kuttaki), kuppe (kuppi), koral (kural), kūli, kūṟu, koḷḷi, kōḻe (kōḻi), caṭṭa (caṭṭam), ciṭṭike (ciṭṭiki), cillaṟe (cillaṟi), ciḷḷu (cīḻkki), cīṭṭi (cīṭṭu), cuṭṭi (curuṭṭu), kēr (cuvar), cūṭu (arikkaṭṭuk kuviyal), cūl, cūruḷ (cūḷ), cette (cetti), kemparaku (cevvarakku), toṭṭu (coṭṭu), joṇṭu (coṇṭu), nēcaṟu (ñāyiṟu), takku, takaṭu, tattu, taṇal, taḷe (taḷi), toḷe (tiraḷi), tere (tiri), tīvaṭṭi, tūcu, tūmpu, heṇṇu (peṇ),
 25. toḻiṟpeyar
(1) 	mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kaḷ, hake, tappu.
(2) 	mutaṉili tirinta toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kēṭu, īṭu.
(3) 	mutaṉili tirintu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: tāha (tākam).
(4)	mutaṉili vali yiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: ūṭṭe (ūṟṟu, ākupeyar).
(5) 	mutaṉili tirintu vali yiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kuti - kūttu
(6) 	mutaṉili vali yiraṭṭittu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar
 	e-ṭu: āṭṭa (āṭṭam).
(7) 	vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: vikuti peyar poruḷ
 	ke (ki) aṭike (aṭuki = camittal)
 	te (ti) naṭatte (naṭatti)
 	ū (uḷ) peḷeyu (viḷiyuḷ)
 	alu (al) pikkalu (vikkal)
 	vu (u) aṭavu (aṭivu), cāvu.
 	pu atirppu
 	aṇa (aṇam) kaṭṭaṇa (kaṭṭaṇam)
 	pi (vi) kalpi (kalvi)
(8) 	mutaṉili valittu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: paḻakke (vaḻakkam), aṭakka (aṭakkam).
(9) 	pakuti vaḻakkaṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kaṭṭaḷi.
cila toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ
oṇakilu (uṇakkam), auvu (uḷavu), ēlām (ēlam), oṭṭa (oṭṭa m), oppiṭi (oppaṭi = aṟuvaṭi), oppanta, oppa (oppam), okkari (okkāḷam), jūtu (cūtu), tappaṭi (tavaṟu) tāṭṭavāṭṭa (tāṭṭōṭṭam), tīrppu, tīrmāṉa (tīrmāṉam), ammāle (ammāṉi).
viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar
nōṭitavaṉu (pārttavaṉ), nōṭuvavaṉu (pārkkiṟavaṉ), (pārppavaṉ) mutaliyaṉa.
vēṟṟumip peyar
 nāṉ maram kutiri aracu
 1. 	nāṉu maravu kutureyu aracu
 2.	naṉṉaṉṉu maravaṉṉu kutireyaṉṉu aracaṉṉu
 3.	naṉṉinta maratinta kutireyinta araciṉinta
 4. 	naṉake marakke kutureke aracike
 5. 	naṉṉinta maratinta kutureyinta araciṉinta
 6. 	naṉṉa marata kutureya araciṉa
 7. 	naṉṉalli maratalli kutureyalli araciṉalli
 8. 	maravē kutureyē aracē

viṉip puṭipeyarcci
 kare (kari = aḻi) eṉṉum viṉi
 muṟṟu: 	i.kā. 	ni.kā. 	e.kā.
 taṉmi: 	o. kareteṉu 	kareyuttēṉē	kareyuveṉu
	pa. karetevu 	kareyuttēve	kareyuvevu
 muṉṉili: 	o. kareti 	kareyuttī 	kareyuvī
 	pa. karetiri 	kareyuttīri 	kareyuvivu
 paṭarkki: 	ā. karetaṉu 	kareyuttāṉe 	kareyuvaṉu
 	pe. karetaḷu 	kareyuttāḷe	kareyuvaḷu
 	palar karetaru 	kareyuttāre	kareyuvaru
 	o. kareyittu 	kareyuttare	kareyuvutu
 	pa. karetavu 	kareyuttāve 	kareyuvuvu
 ēval: 	o. kare 	pa. kareyiri
	 peyareccam 	viṉiyeccam
 i.kā. 	karate 	karetu
 ni.kā. 	kareyuva 	karaye
 e.kā. 	kareyuva 	kareyate
 etirmaṟi: 	kareyuta
 toṭarcci viṉiyeccam: kareyuttā
 ceyappāṭṭu viṉi: kareyalpaṭu
 piṟaviṉi: kareyicu
 kalavik kāla viṉi
 karetu 	itteṉu 	= 	karintiruntēṉ
 karetu 	iruntēṉe = 	karintirukkiṟēṉ
 karetu 	iruveṉu 	=	karintiruppēṉ
 kareyuttā	itteṉu 	= 	karintukoṇṭiruntēṉ
 kareyuttā	irutteṉa 	= 	karintukoṇṭirukkiṟēṉ
 kareyuttā	iruveṉu 	= 	karintukoṇṭiruppēṉ

tuṇiviṉikaḷ
alla 	: 	itu maravalla = itu maramalla.
 āku 	: 	aracaṉāki, kaṭṭiyāki, tāṉāki, hōyittu=
 		tāṉākap pōyiṟṟu, nōṭuvatakkāki =
 		pārppataṟkāka, ūṭṭa vāyittu = 				uṇavāyiṟṟu, naṉṉintāvakuvatilla = 
		eṉṉālākuvatilli, pareyalāyittu = 
		variyalāyiṟṟu (variyappaṭṭatu)
 āṭu 	: 	māttāṭu = māṟṟamāṭu (uriyāṭu).
 āṟu (āṟṟu): 	āṟeṉu = āṟṟēṉ (eṉṉāl muṭiyātu).
 illa 	: 	paralilla = varalilli (varavilli), illa 
		(No).

 		maraviruttate = maramirukkiṉṟatu.
 uṇṭu 	: 	naṉakeholavuṇṭu = eṉakkup pulam (nilam) 
		uṇṭu.
 ol 	: 	olleṉu = olleṉ (iciyēṉ).
 kūṭu 	: 	kūṭatu = kūṭātu (takātu).
 koḷ 	: 	nittukoḷ = niṉṟukoḷ.
 taku 	: 	parattakkatu = varattakkatu.
 pal 	: 	palleṉu = vallēṉ (eṉṉāl muṭiyum).
 pēkku 	: 	koṭapēkku = koṭukka vēṇṭum.
 pēṭu 	: 	tiṉṉapēṭu = tiṉṉavēṇṭum.
 pēṭa 	: 	tiṉṉapēṭa = tiṉṉavēṇṭām.

puṉarviṉi
peyaroṭuviṉi: kikkoḷḷu, kāppāṭu (kāppāṟṟu), māttāṭu, īṭēru, neṟavēṟu, eturukoḷḷu, mēlpaṭu, purayēṟu, ceṇṭāṭucu mutaliyaṉa.
 viṉiyākuviṉi
 ākiru (= ākiyiru), uṇṭāku, koṇṭāṭu, illātē 
hōku, piṭṭupiṭu, ōṭi hōku, teḷukoḷ oppikkoḷ, koṇṭu paru  (koṇṭuvā), koṇṭu koḷḷu, vāṅku, pīcāṭu (vīcāṭṭu) mutaliyaṉa.
kuṟippup pereccam
kela (cila), hala (pala), koñca, ellā, caṉṉa (ciṉṉa), toṭṭa (=periya), eḷaya (iḷiya), haḷē (paḻa = paḻiya), hoca (putu).
kuṟippu viṉiyeccam
taṇṇake (taṭpamāy), neṭṭaṉe, pakkeṉa (pokkeṉa), pēkaṉe (vēkamāy), mellaṉe mutaliyaṉa.
	iṭiccol
kaṭṭiṭac coṟkaḷ
ā, akō, atō, āka = aṉṟu, hāke (appaṭi), antu (aṉṟu), antta (aṉiya), anttu (appaṭi).
viṉāvaṭic coṟkaḷ
ē, ēke (ēṉ), ēṉ entta (eṉiya). enttu (eppaṭi), ō.
kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ
ayyō (aiyō), kuyyō, morrō (muṟiyō), cī, ammamma, jalajala (calacala), cummaṉe, curīreṉa, cuṟucuṟeṉa, taḷataḷa.
toṭarpuc coṟkaḷ
kuṟittu, auke (uḷḷē), keḷake (kīḻē), munte, hinte (piṉpu), horatte (puṟattē), eturu (etir), mēlē, mēke, kūṭa, naṭuve, peḷike (veḷukka), muñcce (muntē), cuttalu (cuṟṟilum), motalu, uḷa, kīḻ, munte (muṉpu), hinte (piṉpu), hoṟake (puṟakē).
kālampaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ
iṉṉu (iṉṟu), moṉṉe (mūṉṟām nāḷ), niṉṉe (nerunal), nāḷe (nāḷi), nāḷittu (nāḷiniṉṟu), oṭaṉe (uṭaṉē).
aḷavupaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ
maṭṭi ke (maṭṭukku), vare, varikū (varikku), iṉṉu (iṉṉum), mattu (maṟṟu), pēre (vēṟe), kūṭa.
iṇippuc coṟkaḷ
ātare (āṉāl), akali (akal = allatu), ātaru (āṉālum), ātutarinta (ātaliṉālē), um, allate (allātē), illate.

viḷipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ.
 ōyi, ō, elē.
pālkāṭṭum vikutikaḷ
 ā.pā: aṉ > aṉe, aṉu, a. kāraṉ > kāra > āra.
 pe.pā: aḷ > aḷe, aḷu.
 i, iti, itti > vitti > kitti. aṉi > iṉi
 palarpāl: ar > are, aru. kaḷ > kaḷu.
 o.pā: atu > tu, itu, utu.
 pa.pā: kaḷ > kaḷu, avi > ave. avu.

kāṭajēṉu (kāṭṭuttēṉ), tikkillāta, kaṇṇīru, nelamaṭṭa, motalāta (mutalāṉa), motalukoṇṭu.... varekū, illavē yilla, nampattakkatu, tuṇṭutuṇṭāki, pērepēre (vēṟē vēṟē), naṭṭanaṭuvē, āṭṭa pāṭṭakaḷu, ontontu (ov voṉṟu), iṉṉentu, mattontu (maṟṟoṉṟu), pērontu (vēṟoṉṟu), ontuvēḷi (oru vēḷi), naṭavaḷikka (naṭa paṭikki), kaṇṇāre, talekaṭṭu, aṭṭuppu, eḷanake (iḷa naki), kaṭahuṭṭu (kaṭikkuṭṭi), kumpaṭṭe (kumpu kaṭṭi), cutmutta (cuṟṟumuṟṟum), keṅkaṇ (ceṅkaṇ), kentaḷir (centaḷir), kentūḷ (centūḷ), maḻekāla, toṭevāḻi (toṭivāḻi), nāṭāṭi (nāṭōṭi), paccakallu, paṭṭacāle, halacarakku, haḷḷata nāle (paḷḷanāli), haḷḷamaṭa (paḷḷa maṭi), paṉṉāṭe, paṉṉīr, hāvāṭicu (pāmpāṭṭu), hāḻūru, pāḷeyappaṭṭu, hiṅkaṭṭu murikaṭṭu (piṉkaṭṭu maṟikaṭṭu), pīkkālaṭṭa, perpaciṟu (peruvayiṟu), mālukaṇ (māṟukaṇ), mōṭāmōṭi, piḷinari (veḷnari), paccela, peḷḷāṉe (veḷḷāṉi), perpaṭe (perum paṭi), pennīr (vennīr), hulitokalu (pulittōl), tāyitantekaḷu, aṇṇa tamman tiru, ententikū (eṉṟeṉṟikkum).
coṟṟoṭarkaḷ
atu yāru? kaḷḷaru ōṭi hōtaru (pōṉār), kōlu muritu hōyīttu (muṟintu pōyiṟṟu), maṉeke hōkuva hot tāyittu = maṉikkup pōkiṟa poḻutāyiṟṟu.
ī ūriṉalli naṉake ontu maṉeyu itē = ivvūril eṉakku oru maṉi irukkiṟatu. huṭu karu tōṭṭatalvi tirukāṭuttā marakaḷaṉṉu hattūttā haṇṇu tiṉṉuttā opparaṉopparu kūkuttā hekaraṉṉu hiṭitu kariyuttā nakuttā āṭuttā ittaru = piyaṉkaḷ tōṭṭattil tirintu koṇṭum maraṅ kaḷil ēṟikkoṇṭum paḻaṅkaḷ tiṉṟu koṇṭum oruvari yoruvar kūvi (kūppiṭṭu)k koṇṭum peyariṭṭuk karintu (aḻittuk) koṇṭum nakittuk koṇṭum viḷiyāṭik koṇṭum iruntaṉar. huṭukaru = poṭiyar allatu piṭukar.
nīvākali nāṉākali hōkuvatilla = nīṅkaḷākal nāṉākal pōkuvatilli. ākal = āvatu. nīvu ā kateyaṉṉu ōtiti pus tavaṉṉu naṉake tōṟiciri = nīṅkaḷ ak katiyi ōtiṉa (paṭitta) puttakatti eṉakkuk kāṭṭuṅkaḷ. tōṟu > kitāṉṟu. tōṟiciri (pi.vi.)
naṉṉa ūraṉṉu cēritakūṭale nimake ontu kātakavaṉṉu pareyuveṉu = eṉ ūr pōyc cērntavuṭaṉ umakku oru kākitam varivēṉ.
makkaḷellaru pāṭacāleyaṉṉu piṭṭu, tamma tamma maṉa kaḷike hoṟaṭṭu hōta paḷikka javāṉaṉu pākilukaḷaṉṉu mucci piṭuvaṉṉu = makka (piḷḷika), ḷellārum pāṭacāliyi viṭṭut tamtam malikaḷukkup puṟappaṭṭup pōṉa piṟaku cēvakaṉ vācalkaḷi mūṭiviṭuvāṉ.
nāḷevāra ūriṉalli iruvirō emputāki avaraṉṉu kēḷu = nāḷi (aṭutta) vāram ūril iruppīrō eṉpatāka avarik kēḷ.
maḻeyu pāratittare peḷe yākuvavutilli = maḻi varātirun tāl viḷi (viḷiyuḷ), ākuvatilli.
vitikkiṟavaṉ uvami
ikō, pittuvavaṉu pitta horaṭṭaṉu, avaṉu pittuvāka kelavu mārkata hattara pittu. paṣikaḷu pantu ataṉṉutintu piṭṭavu. pēre kelavu pahaḷa vaṇṇu illāta paṇṭestaḷa kaḷalli pittu; atakke āḷavāta maṇṇu illata kāraṇa, caṅkaṭalē moḷiyittu; ātare cūryaṉu mūṭitāka atu pentu atakke pēru illātatarinta oṇaki hōyittu. pēre kelavu muḷḷukaḷa mēlē pittu, muḷḷukaḷu peḷetu, aṇakicitavu, pēre kelavu oḷḷē pūmiya, mēlē pittu, ontu nū, raṣṭu, ontu aravattaṣṭu, ontu mūvattaṣṭu pala koṭṭattu, kēḷuvataṟke kivikaḷuḷḷavaṉu kēḷali.
itō, vittukiṟavaṉ vittap puṟappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ vittukiyil cila vaḻiyarukē viḻuntu, paṟavikaḷ vantu atit tiṉṟuviṭṭaṉa. vēṟē cila mikunta maṇ illātapāṟi yiṭaṅkaḷil viḻuntatu. ataṟku āḻamāṉa maṇ illāta kāraṇamāy virintu muḷit tatu; āṉāl veyil ēṟiṉapōtu atu ventu, ataṟku vēril lātatāl uṇaṅkip pōyiṟṟu, vēṟe cila muṭkaḷ mēlē viḻuntatu. muṭkaḷ vaḷarntu ati nerukki viṭṭaṉa. vēṟē cila nalla nilattil viḻuntu oṉṟu nūṟākavum oṉṟu aṟupatāka vum oṉṟu muppatākavum palaṉ koṭuttatu. kēṭkiṟataṟkuc cevikaḷuḷḷavaṉ kēṭkakkaṭavaṉ.
kuṟippu: meymaṟi (cattiya vētam) eṉṉum kiṟittava maṟiyiṉ moḻivaṭi viṭittoṇṭarātalāl, mēṟkūṟiya uvami yil aḵṟiṇip paṉmiyeḻuvāykaḷ orumi viṉiyoṭu muṭintaṉa; paṉmic coṟku orumic col vantatu.  (ti.tā.)
kaṉṉam
 kaṉṉam - karṇa
kal - kaṉ = tuḷi. kaṉ - kaṉṉam = 1. tuḷiyuḷḷa kātu,                           2. yāṉic cevi (tivā.). 3. kātiyaṭutta alakup pakkam.                ma. kaṉṉam, ka. kaṉṉa.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum krut, krū eṉṉum mūlaṅkaḷ poruntā.
krut = 1. ceytukoṇṭu, 2. citivu, 3. tiruku, cuṟṟu.
kru = 1. koṭṭu, eṟi, citaṟu. 2. citi, 3. aṟi, aṟivi. (va.va: 112)

kaṉam - kaṉa (gh)
kal - kaṉ = 1. fš (Nlh.), 2. uṟutippāṭu (īṭu, 5: 8: 3).
kaṉ - kaṉa. kaṉattal = paḷuvātal, mikutiyātal, paruttal, kural taṭittal, perumiyuṟutal.  (va.va:)

kaṉa - kaṉam = paḷu, parumaṉ, perumi, ceṟivu, tiraṭci, uṟuti, mikuti, kūṭṭam, mummāṉa vaṭivu.
potuvākak kaṉattiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākac colvatu kallē. kallippōṟ kaṉakkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. paṇṭi nāḷiṟ kaṉatta eṭikkellām kalliyē payaṉpaṭuttiyatāl, paṭikkal eṉṉum vaḻakkeḻuntatu. iṅkilāntilum avvaḻakka miruntati stone (14 pavuṇṭu) eṉṉum col uṇarttum.
vaṭamoḻiyil kaṉattal eṉṉum viṉiyilli. mā.vi. akara mutali kāṭṭiyirukkum haṉ (kol) eṉṉum mūlam, vaṭa moḻiyiṟ kaṉa eṉṉum coṟkuḷḷa palvēṟu poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkut tāṉ ēṟkum. (va.va. 121-113)
kaṉi vakikaḷ:
 kāy teṅku pūcaṇi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṉi;
 kaṉi muntiri nelli mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṉi; (kaṉivāyiruppatu);
 paḻam mā vāḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṉi (paḻuppu niṟamāṉatu);
 neṟṟu vērkaṭali pōṉṟatiṉ mutirvu;
 kāyappaḻam paḻukkat toṭaṅkiya kāy;
 ōtappaḻam puḷiyiṉ kāyp paḻam. (col: 68)

kātal 
kātal eṉpatu, oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāk kaḻiperu nēyamāy iruvariṭi nikaḻvatu. atu, kōpperuñ cōḻaṉum picirāntiyārum pōlum naṇpariṭattum, peṟṟōrum piḷḷiyum pōlum uṟaviṉariṭattum, pūta pāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ tēviyum pōlum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviya riṭattum amivatu, atu ariya piṟavik kuṇam. (ta.ti.muṉ VII)
kātal poruttam
maṇamakkaṭku mutaṉmiyāka vēṇṭum poruttam ituvē. iḵtillāviṭiṉ, ēṉic ciṟappellāmiruntum ētum payaṉilli. ātalāl, maṇamakkaḷ icivu peṟṟē avar peṟṟōr avarkku maṇañ ceytu vittal vēṇṭum. (ta.ti. 43)
kātalmaṇam
kātal maṇamāvatu, ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum peṟṟōrik kēḷātum piṟarukkut teriyātum oruvariyoruvar kātalittu, tāmē kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumākak kūṭik koḷvatu. (ta.ti.6)

L. candela, a wax or tallow candle, AS. candel OE. candel, AN, OF, candel (I)e E. candle (f. candere, to glow), cylinder of wax, tallow spermaceti, cte., enclosing wick, for giving light.

L. candidatus, clothed in white; a candidate for office, who among the Romans was always clothed in white, for the consulship. E. candidate, one who offers himself or is put forward to be elected to an office, orig. white-robed.
