kummutal = kuvital. kum - kumi - kumiḻ - kumiḻi.
kum - kumpu - kūmpu. kūmputal - kuvital. kumputal = kuvital.
kumpu - kumpam = kuṭam, tēr muṭi, yāṉit talik kuvavu, kaṭṭiṭak kuvi mukaṭu (dome)
kumpaccuri = kuvinta curikkāy.
kumpam - kumpā = kīḻ nōkkik kuvinta uṇkalam.
kumpu - kumpiṭu. kumpiṭutal = kikuvittal.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum kump (b, bh) eṉṉum mūlam kumpu eṉpataṉ citivē. āyiṉ, avar koḷḷum poruḷ kavital; teṉṉavar koḷḷum poruḷ kuvital. muṉṉatu kīḻnōkkiyateṉṟum, piṉṉatu mēl nōkkiyateṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟika. (va.va : 126)
kummal
um - kum. kummutal = kūṭutal, mikutal, tiraḷtal, niṟital.
kūṭutaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kuvitaṟ karuttum; kuvitaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kūmputal, mūṭutal, maṟital, aṭakkamātal, amitiyātal, kuṟital mutaliya karuttukkaḷum; piṟakkum.
ik karuttukkaḷil tōṉṟum viṉic coṟkaḷ, ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiya viṉiyākavum kuṉṟā viṉiyākavu mirukkum.
kūṭutal viṉi, kāṭcip poruḷatum karuttup poruḷatu mākap pala tiṟattatu.
kum - kummal = 1. arici, uppu, kāykaṟi mutaliya poruḷkaḷiṉ kuviyal. cantiyiṟ kāykaṟikaḷik kummal kummalākak kuvittu vittiruppārkaḷ. 2. makkaṭ kūṭṭam, kumpal.
kummali - kummal = paruttavaḷ. (yāḻp.)
kummiruṭṭu = tiṇinta iruṭṭu, kāriruḷ.
kum - kama - kamam = niṟivu. kamamniṟin tiyalum (tol. uri.57).
kam - kama. kamattal = niṟital. kamanta mātirak kāvalar (kamparā. mitilik. 132).
kum - kumu - kumukku = 1. kūṭṭam (i.va.). 2. peruntoki. (W.) 3. mottam. paṇṭaṅkaḷi yellām kumukkāy vāṅkiṉāṉ. (W.)
 kummeṉal = maṇam vīcutal.
koṇṭimalarēkum kumkum eṉṉak
kōyamputtūrnāṭi naṭantuvā piṉkūṭi  (nāṭōṭippāṭṭu)
 kumuku meṉal = maṇam vīcutaṟ kuṟippu.
maṉantāṉ kumukumeṉ ṟaṭikka villi  (taṉippā. 1, 189 : 44)
kumukumuttal = maṇam vīcutal. pacumañcaḷ kumukumukku (aḻakarkala . 10).
kum - kam. kammeṉal = maṇam vīcutaṟ kuṟippu.
oruṅkupiṇi yaviḻak kāṭē kammeṉ ṟaṉṟē  (akanā. 23).
pūkkaṭikkup pōṉāl maṇam kamkammeṉ ṟaṭikkum. kam - kama. kamakamattal = mikamaṇattal.
kama - kamaḻ. kamaḻtal = naṟumaṇam vīcutal. tēṅkamaḻ nāṟṟam (nālaṭi. 199).
camiyalaṟiyil tāḷikkiṟatu iṅkē kamakamaveṉṟu kamaḻkiṟatu.
maṇam kāṟṟoṭu allatu oru poruḷoṭu kalakkun taṉmiyatu . ātalāl, kalattaṟ collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
 pūvoṭu cērnta nārum maṇam peṟum  (paḻa.)
o.nō : maṇattal = kalattal, kamaḻtal, maṉṟal = kalattal, kamaḻtal, veṟuttal = ceṟital. veṟu - veṟi = maṇam.
kum - kumpu. kumputal = kūṭutal.
kumpu = tiraḷ, kūṭṭam. te. kumpu (gumpu), va. kumpa (gumpha).
 aṅkē oru kumpu kūṭiyirukkiṟatu.
kumpu - kumpal = kūṭṭam. āṭkaḷ kumpal kumpalākak kūṭi niṟkiṟārkaḷ.
kumpu - kumpi = kuṭicit tiraḷ, cēri. paṟikkumpi. (u.va.) te. kompa (kompa).
kumpu - kuppu. kuppam = 1. kūṭṭam. kuppamantiraṅkaḷellām (kuṟṟā. tala. kavuṟcaṉa. 8). 2. kuviyal. 3. cempaṭavar kuṭicit tiraḷ, neytal nilacciṟṟūr. e-ṭu : tāḻaṅkuppam, mañcak kuppam. 4. nāṭṭuppuṟac ciṟṟūr. kuppakkāṭu = paṭṭikkāṭu. 5. aṭavi, kāṭu. (piṅ.). marā. kumpa (kumpa).
kuppam (kūṭṭam) - te. kuppa, ka. kuppe.
kuppam (ciṟṟūr) - te. kuppamu.
kuppam - kuppal = 1. kūṭṭam (w). 2. kuviyal. 3. tiraṇṭa mēṭu (tivā.).
kuppu - kuppi = 1. kuviyal, uppiṉ peruṅkuppi (tirikaṭu. 83). 2. neṟkuviyal. kuṉṟeṉak kuviiya kuṉṟāk kuppi (poruna. 244). 3. tiraḷ. maṉperumpavaḻak kuppi vālaṇi kalañcey kuppi (cīvaka. 114). 4. kūṭṭam. kōṭcuṟā viṉat toṭu mutalik kuppikaḷ (cīvaka. 95). 5. urak kuviyal. 6. vīṭṭi lum teruvilum kūṭṭi vāravēṇṭiya kaḻivup poruṭkaḷ. 7. malam (iṭakkaraṭakkal).
 k., te. kuppa, ka. kuppe.
kuppi - (kuviyal) - AS, heap, OS. hop, OE. heap ME. heep, E. heap, Du. hoop, Ice. hopr, Dan. hob. Swed. hop, G. hufe, OHG. hufo, Russ, kupa, Lith. kupas.
kum - kumi. kumital = kuvital, tiraḷtal.
ma. kumi, te. kumi (g).
kumittal = tiraḷac ceytal.
kumi - kumiḻ = 1. nīrkkumiḻi. 2. pātakkuṟaṭṭiṉ uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa puṭippu allatu muḷi. 3. eruttiṉ timil.
kumiḻ - kumiḻi = 1. nīrkkumiḻi. kumiḻiviṭ ṭumiḻkuruti (cīvaka. 2239). 2. pātak kuṟaṭṭiṉ kumiḻ.
kumiḻttal = kumiḻiyiṭutal. kumiḻittal = kumiḻi koḷḷutal.
kum - kumar = 1. tiraṇṭa iḷami. o. nō : viṭittal = vīṟu koṇṭu viṟittal. viṭi = valimiyuḷḷa iḷam paruva vuyiri. 2. kaṉṉimi. kumarirukkuñ caci pōlvāḷ (kuṟṟā. tala. tarumacāmi. 47). 3. aḻiyāt taṉmi. kumaruṟap piṇitta pim poṟ koṭi (pārata. intirap. 32.)
kumar - kumaraṉ = 1. iḷiñaṉ. iruntakulak kumarartami yirukaṇṇiṉ mukattaḻaku parukanōkki (kamparā mitili. 10.). 2. iḷiñaṉāṉa murukaṉ. kuṉṟutōṟāṭiya kumararp pōṟṟu vām (kantapu. kaṭavuḷ. 16).
kumar - kumari = 1. pūppaṭinta iḷampeṇ, iḷiñi, kumari maṇañ ceytu koṇṭu (tiv. periyāḻ. 3 : 8 : 3). 2. pūp paṭintu maṇavāta peṇṭu. 3. aḻiyāt taṉmi, aḻiviṉmi. kumarikkūṭṭiṟ koḻumpal luṇavu (cilap. 10 : 123). 4. eṉṟuṅ kaṉṉiyā yiruppavaḷākak karutappaṭum kāḷit teyvam. viḻinutaṟ kumari (cilap. 11 : 214). 5. kāḷi peyar koṇṭa (paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭu) malit toṭar. kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa (cilap. 11 : 20). 6. avaḷ peyar koṇṭa āṟu. vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari (tol. pāyi.) 7. kumari maliyit teṉṉelliyākavum kumariyāṟṟi vaṭa velliyākavuṅ koṇṭa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭu, kumarikkaṇṭam.
kumariruṭṭu (kumariyiruṭṭu), kumarivāḻi eṉpaṉa iḷamiyi yum; kumari vēṭṭam, kumarippōr eṉpaṉa mutal nikaḻcciyi yum; kumarippaṭi, kumarimatil eṉpaṉa aḻiyā nilimiyiyum uṇarttum.
kumar, kumaraṉ, kumari, eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭkut tiraṇṭa iḷami eṉpatē aṭippaṭipporuḷ. o. nō : muruku = iḷami, muruku - murukaṉ = iḷiñaṉ, kumaraṉ, vaḷḷimaṇāḷaṉ, kuṟiñci nilatteyvam.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, kumara kumariyari āriyatteyvamākavum, avar peyari āriyac collākavuṅ kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, kumaraṉ eṉpatik kumāra eṉṟum kumari eṉpatik kumārī eṉṟum īṟṟayaluyir nīṭṭi, makaṉ makaḷ eṉṟu muṟiyē poruḷ kuṟittu, kumāra eṉpatik ku + māra eṉṟu citittu, eḷitil iṟappatu (“easily dying”) eṉṟu poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟuvar.
kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭkut tamiḻil iḷiñaṉ iḷiñi eṉṟē yaṉṟi, makaṉ makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷilli. ivviru coṟkaṭkum kum eṉpatē mūlam eṉṟum, āriyat toṉmak katippaṭi murukaṉ civaṉukku makaṉ eṉiṉum, kāḷi avaṉukku makaḷākāḷ eṉṟum, eḷitiliṟappatu kuḻap paruvattilum kiḻapparuvattilumaṉṟi maḻapparuvattililli yeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
intac cumiyit tūkkamuṭiyāta nī oru kumaraṉ eṉṟu irukka lāmā eṉṟu peṇṭir viṉavuvatiyum, kōṭic cēlikku oru veḷḷi, kumarippeṇṇiṟku oru piḷḷi eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiyum, nōkkuka. kum - kummi - kommi = iḷami. (tivā.).
maṇamākāta iḷiñaṉiyum iḷiñiyiyum celvaṉ celvi eṉṟu aṭikoṭuttup peyar kuṟippatu, kumaraṉ kumari eṉpaṉa vaṭacolleṉṉum aṟiyāmiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu. celvaṉ celvi eṉṟu maṇamakkaḷik kuṟippatē marapātalāl, Master, Miss eṉṉum aṭikaḷi iṉik kumaraṉ kumari eṉṟē tamiḻiṟ kuṟikka.
kum - kummi = 1. ki kuvittaṭittal. 2. aṅṅaṉam aṭittāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu. 3. av viḷiyāṭṭup pāṭṭu.
ma. kummi, te. koppi (gobbi.)
kum - kom - kommi. kommi koṭṭutal = ki taṭṭi yaḻittal.
kum - kumpu - kumpiṭu. kumpiṭutal = kikuvittu vaṇakkañ ceytal. ma. kumpiṭu.
kumpiṭu - kumpīṭu = kikuvikkum vaṇakkam.
kumpu - kūmpu. kūmputal = 1. kuvital. ceyya kamala malar kūmpa (niṭata. cantirō. 2.). 2. oṭuṅkutal. 3. ūkkaṅ kuṟital. vaṭavar vāṭak kuṭavar kūmpa (paṭṭiṉap. 276).
ma. kūmpu.
kūmpu = 1. pūmoṭṭu. tāḻik kūmpaviḻnta voṇpū (ain. aim. 49). 2. tērmoṭṭu (tivā.). 3. kappaṟ pāymaram. kūmpu mutaṉmuṟiya vīṅkupiṇi yaviḻntu (maṇi. 4 : 30).
ma. kūmpu, ka. kūvē.
kūmpu - kūppu. kūpputal = kuvittal. kāliyu māliyuṅ kikūppik kāltoḻutāl (taṉippā.).
kum - kumutu - kumutam = katiroḷiyiṟ kūmpuvatākac collap paṭum āmpal. kummutal = kūṭutal. kuvital.
kumutam - va. kumuta.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kumutam eṉṉum colli vaṭacollākak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, atik ku + muta eṉṟu pirittu, eṉṉa makiḻcci! eṉṉum uṇarcci yūṭṭuvatākap (exciting what joy!”) poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kūṟuvar. avar coṟporuḷ koṇṭa vaki varumāṟu :
kū (oru viṉāccol) - ku = evvāṟu (how), muta = makiḻcci (joy).
kumutattiṉ maṟupeyarāṉa āmpal eṉṉuñ collum, tamiḻp poruṭkaraṇiyattiyē valiyuṟuttum.
ampal = pērarumpu. ampal - āmpal = katiroḷiyiṟ pērarumpupōṟ kuviyum malar.
kummutal = kuvital, mūṭutal, aṭittal, olikuṟital, oḷi kuṟital. kum - kam.
kammeṉal = ōciyaṭaṅkaṟ kuṟippu. kāṉamuṅ kammeṉṟaṉṟē (naṟ. 154).
kūṭattiluḷḷa vācal palakaṇikaḷi yellām aṭittu viṭṭāl, uḷḷē kammeṉṟirukkum (u.va.).
kammeṉṟiru = pēcātu amitiyāyiru.
kammutal = 1. kural kuṉṟutal. meṉkural kammāmē (kumara. pira. muttuk. 18) 2. oḷi kuṟital.
kam - kammal = 1. kuralaṭippu, toṇṭikkammal. 2. viḷiccaṟ kuṟivu. payir kammalāyp pōyiṟṟu. 3. vilik kuṟivu. vili kammalāyirukkiṟatu. (W).
kam - kammi = kuṟivu. patturūpā kammiyāyirukkiṟatu. kammi - u. kamī.
mūṭutaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu maṟippu, aṭakkam, marumam ākiya karuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum.
kum - kumu - kumukku - kamukku - kamukkam = maruma vaṭakkam. mūṭik koṇṭiruntāl kamukkam, tiṟantāl veṭṭaveḷi. (paḻa.)
kamukkakkāraṉ = aṭakkamuḷḷavaṉ.
avari antap patavikku amarttiṉatic ceytit tāḷil veḷivarumvari kamukkamāy vittiruntārkaḷ.
kum - kumpu = kumpam = kuvintu tiraṇṭirukkum kalam. 2. kumpa vaṭivāṉa mēṟkaṭṭiṭam (dome). 3. kumpam pōṉṟa yāṉi maṇṭi. 4. kumpa vōri. 5. kumpamātam (māci). kumpam - va. kumpa (kumpha).
kumpāpiṣēkam eṉṉum vaṭacollik kumpamuḻukku eṉṟē collalām; kuṭamuḻukku eṉṟu colla vēṇṭiyatilli. kumpam - kumpā = arik kumpa vaṭivāṉa māḻiyuṇkalam.
kumpak kuṭam = tēriṉmuṭi.
kūṭu eṉṉum viṉiyiṉ nikaḻkāla veccam (Infinitive mood), uṭaṉ oṭu ōṭu eṉṉum karuvi (3 ām) vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ pōl uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷ tarum.
e-ṭu : nāykūṭa (nāyoṭu) nampi vantāṉ.
tantikūṭa (tantiyoṭu) makaṉum pāṭiṉāṉ.
kum eṉṉum viṉi kūṭu eṉpataṉ oruporuṭ col (synonym) lātalāl, kumma eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam kūṭa (with, together, together with) eṉpatupōl uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷ taral vēṇṭum.
kummal (kuviyal) - L. cumulus, a heap, pile, mass.
 L. cumulo, to heap up.
āṅkilam cumulus eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu cumulate (kummaliṭu) eṉṉum viṉic collit tōṟṟuvikkum. atiṉiṉṟu accumulate (ad+cumulate) eṉṉum viṉiccol eḻum.
cumulus eṉṉuñ colliṉ aṭiyāṉa cum eṉpatu, ilattīṉil uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷ muṉṉoṭṭākap (pref.) peruvaḻak kāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu : 	cum bona venia = with your kind indulgence.
	cum grana salis = with a grin of salt.
 	cum multis aliis = with many other things.
 	cum notis variorum = with notes of various. (critics).
 	cum privilegio = with privilege.
cum eṉṉum ilattīṉ muṉṉoṭṭu, mutaṟkaṇ com eṉṟu tirintu, piṉṉar varuñcol mutaleḻuttiṟkēṟpa con, col, cor eṉṟum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu.
 e-ṭu : 	combustum, commodus,
 	communico, compenium;
 	concretus, condemno, confero, conservus;
 	collabor, collega; corrector, corruptus.
āṅkilattil nūṟṟumēṉi nāṟpāṉ coṟkaḷ ilattīṉāta lāl, com, con, col, cor eṉṉum muṉṉeṭṭukkaḷik koṇṭa coṟkaḷ ērāḷamāy vaḻaṅkuvatik kāṇalām.
 e-ṭu:	combine, commence, compos, communicate;
	concord, condole, confuse, console;
	collect, college; correspond, corrode
com eṉṉum ilattīṉ muṉṉoṭṭu, co eṉṟum kuṟuki, ilattīṉiṟ cila kuṟippiṭṭa eḻuttukkaṭku muṉṉum, āṅkilattil ellā eḻuttukkaṭku muṉṉum, vaḻaṅkum.
“co -, pref. L short form of com - (cum prep. with), used in - L only before vowels, h, gr, and (in the correct classical form) n, but in E as living pref. before any letter.” eṉṟu The Concise Oxford Dictionary kūṟutal kāṇka.
 e-ṭu : ilattīṉ.
 coarguo, cognosco, cohabitare.
āṅkilam
co-adjust, co-oeducation, co-operate, cheir, co-partner, co-religionist, co-tenant, co-worker.
uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷuṇarttum kum (cum) eṉṉum ilattīṉ muṉṉoṭṭu, kirēkkattil cum (sum) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. ati āṅkilar cim (sym) eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅki vantiruk kiṉṟaṉar. attiripum, syn, syl eṉṟu varuñcoṉ mutaliṟkēṟpa vēṟupaṭum.
e-ṭu : symbol (sumbolon), sympathy (sumpatheia), symphony (sumphonia), symposium (sumposion), symptom sumptoma);
synagogue (sunagoge), syndic (sundikos), synonym (sunonumon), syntax (suntaxis), synthesis (sunthesis);
syllable (sullabe), syllepsis (sullepsis), syllogism (sullogismos).
cum (sum) eṉṉum kirēkka uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷ muṉ ṉoṭṭu, kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkuk kiḻakkil tōṉṟiya kīḻi yāriyattil sam (sam) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu.
iṅkuk kīḻiyāriyam eṉṟatu, vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr, intiyāviṟku varumuṉ īrāṉukkum kāntārattiṟkum iṭip paṭṭa nilappakutiyil vatintiruntapōtu vaḻaṅkivanta moḻi yākum.
uṭaṉ (kūṭa), uṭaṉ kūṭiya, kalanta, kalantu, niṟivāka eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum sam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭip peṟṟa mutaṉmi yāṉa coṟkaḷ, iṉṟu camaṟkirutattil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāka vuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu : samkyā, samkraka, samkrānti, samkruta, samka, samkaṭa, samkati, samkama, samkara, samkalpa, samkīta, samkīrtatana, samkīraṇa, samcar, samcita, samjīvin, samtarpa, samtāna, samtōṣa, samnyāsa, sampat, sampanta, sampava, sampanta, sampāra, sampāvana, sampāṣaṇa, samprata, sampratāya, sampratāna, samprōkṣaṇa, sampūrṇa, sampōka, sampōtana, sammati, sammēḷana, samyutta, samyōka, samrakṣaṇa, samvatsara, samvāta, samaṣṭi, samaya, samarppaṇa, samasta, samāsa, samāccāra, samuccaya, samutāya, samūha, samēta, samskruta, samskāra, samjñā, samstāna, samsarka, samsāra, samhāra, samhitā.
sam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu saha eṉṟum tiriyum.
o.nō : sahakamana, sahōtara.
sam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu sa eṉṟum kuṟukum.
e-ṭu. sakala (cakaḷaṉ) = uruvat tirumēṉi.
iṅṅaṉam, kum eṉṉum teṉcol, āriya moḻikaḷil muṟiyē, kum - kam. kam - kaṉ - kō. kum - sum, sum - sam - sa eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu.
āṅkilam kum eṉpatik kam eṉṟu oli tirittum vari (eḻuttu) tirikkātu, iṉṟum iṇippuc collāka (conjuction) vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu : pension-cum-provident fund.
		  agriculture-cum-industry.
sam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu camaṟkirutattiṟ pōṉṟē vēta moḻiyilum peruvaḻakkāka vuḷḷatu. mēliyāriyamum pirākirutamuṅ kalantatu vētamoḻi yeṉṟum, vētamoḻiyum tamiḻuṅ kalantatu camaṟkiruta meṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇ ṭum. ivviru moḻiyum orukālum vaḻaṅkiyirāta ilakkiya moḻikaḷē.
sam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭi nīkkiviṭiṉ, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa mutaṉmic coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi lirā. avi nīṅkiṉ, vētamoḻiyum camaṟkiruta moḻiyum moḻi yeṉṉun takuti yiṉṟi, iruperuñ coṟṟotikaḷākavē muṭiyum.
samskrutam eṉṉum peyarē, sam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭāl ēṟpaṭṭatē. vētamoḻiyun tamiḻuṅ kalantu ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṉum poruḷil, am moḻi appeyar peṟṟatu. sam = uṭaṉ, kalantu. kruta = ceyyappaṭṭatu. tamiḻiyum oru pirākirutamākak koṇṭu, atit trāviṭī prākrut eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. prā = munti. muntic ceyyappaṭṭatu pirākirutam; pintik kalantu ceyyappaṭṭatu camaṟkirutam. samkruta eṉṟālē pōtum. piṟkālattil s eṉṉum eḻutti iṭicceruki samskruta eṉṟaṉar.
mēl varinta kum eṉṉuñ col varalāṟṟiṉiṉṟu teṉ moḻiyiṉ (tamiḻiṉ) muṉmiyiyum vaṭamoḻiyiṉ piṉmi yiyum teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
kummi viḷiyāṭṭu
pala pētiyarum1 petumpiyarum, vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟivantu pāṭik kikuvittu aṭikkum kūttu kummi eṉappaṭum. vaṭārk kāṭṭu vaṭṭārattār itik koppi eṉpar.
kummutal kikuvittal allatu kikuvit taṭittal. kikuvit taṭikkum viḷiyāṭṭātalāl, itu kummiyeṉappaṭṭatu.
kummi yāṭṭattiṟkeṉṟu taṉivakip pāṭṭuṇṭu. atu kummi yaṭi eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatōṭu, attoṭariyē ovvōr uruvilum (caraṇattilum) makuṭamākavuṅ koṇṭirukkum.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu :
 kummiyaṭi peṇṇē kummiyaṭi - nalla
 koṉṟi malarcūṭik kummiyaṭi
 nammiyāḷum taṉi nāyakam nammiṭam
 naṇṇiya teṉṟunī kummiyaṭi
 āṭcimoḻiyiṅkē āṅkilamāy - eṉṟum
 ākiviṭiṉ atu kēṭākum
 māṭci mikuntamiḻ mānilat tāḷuki
 mātaracē varak kummiyaṭi.
ikkāli, oṟṟit tāḷattiṟkum aṭittāḷattiṟkum ēṟkum ellāp pāṭṭukkaḷum kummikkum pāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṟṟi = ēkam. aṭi = āti.  (ta.nā.vi.)
kumaraṉ
kumaraṉ - kumāra (i.vē.)
kum - kummal = kumpal, kūṭutal. kummali = paruttavaḷ.
kum - kom - kommi = 1. parumi (peruṅ. uñcik 40 : 210). 2. tiraṭci (cūṭā). 3. iḷami (tivā.).
kum - kumar = 1. tiraṇṭa iḷiñi, kaṉṉi.
2. kaṉṉimi. kumarirukkuñ cacipōlvāḷ (kuṟṟā. tala. tarumacāmi. 47).
3. aḻiyāmi. kumaruṟap piṇitta (pārata. intirap. 32). maṇap paruvattil āṇum peṇṇum tiraḷvatu iyalpu.
o.nō : viṭi - viṭali = iḷiñaṉ. viṭali = iḷiñi.
viṭi = iḷa āṇ vilaṅku, paṟaviyiṉ tiraṇṭa iḷami. viṭittal = paruttal.
kumar - kumaraṉ = iḷiñaṉ, murukaṉ. kumar - kumari = iḷiñi. kaṉṉiyākak karutappeṟum kāḷi.
āriyar varukikkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, kumarik kaṇṭamākiya paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭil kumari (kāḷi) pālinilat teyvamum veṟṟit teyvamum kuru (ammi) nōyt teyvamumāyiruntāḷ. avaḷ peyarāl oru malit toṭarum oru pērāṟumiruntaṉa.
vaṭavar kumara eṉṉum vaṭivik kumāra eṉṟu nīṭṭi ataṟku makaṉmip poruḷ cērttatuṭaṉ, ku + māra eṉṟu coṟ citittu, eḷitāyiṟappavaṉ (easily dying) eṉṟu poruntāp poruṭ kāraṇamuṅ kāṭṭuvārāyiṉar.
iṉi, uṇāti cūttiram kam (virumpi, kāpi) eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭum.
kumaraṉ, kumari, eṉṉum iraṇṭum tūya teṉcoṟkaḷātaliṉ, maṇamākāta iḷiñaṉ peyarkkumuṉ kumaraṉ (Master) eṉṟum, maṇamākāta peṇṇiṉ peyarkkumuṉ kumari (Miss) eṉṟumē, aṭikoṭuttal vēṇṭum. celvaṉ, celvi eṉpaṉa maṇamakkaḷ peyarkkē poruttamāṉavi.
makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṟu muṟiyē poruḷtarum kumāra(ṉ), kumārī eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷē vaṭacoṟkaḷ.
eḷitāyiṟappatu kuḻavip paruvattilum kiḻapparuvattilu mātaliṉ, kaṭṭiḷaṅkāḷip paruvattik kuṟikkuñ coṟku apporuṭ kāraṇam poruntātu. kāmuṟuvatum kāḷip paruvamē. āyiṉ, kum eṉṉum coṟkum kam eṉṉuñ coṟkum toṭarpilli. kam eṉpatu kām eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam.
ku + māra eṉṉum tavaṟṟup pirippuc coṟkaḷum teṉ coṟṟiripē. kuḷ - ku. maṭi - mari - mru = māra (va.va: 126 - 127.)
kumari 
tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭum teṉ māvāriyil muḻukippōṉa perunilamāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭi yaṭuttu, paṉimali (imayam) pōlum oru māperu malit toṭar iruntatu. ataṉ peyar kumari. ataṉālēyē muḻukippōṉa nilamum kumarik kaṇṭam eṉappaṭṭatu.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭum koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa  (cilap. 11:19-20)
kumari eṉpatu kāḷiyiṉ peyar. kāḷi tamiḻar teyvaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu . iṟavātavaḷ allatu eṉṟum iḷamiyāyiruppavaḷ eṉṉum karuttil, kāḷiyik kumari eṉṟaṉar.
kummutal tiraḷtal. kummal - kummali = paruttavaḷ kum - kumal kumar = tiraṭci, tiraṇṭa kaṉṉippeṇ, kaṉṉimi.
kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭa iḷiñaṉ, iḷiñaṉāṉa murukaṉ. kumar - kumari = tiraṇṭa iḷiñi (kaṉṉi).
maṇapparuvamāṉa iḷami varumpōtu uṭal tiraḷutal iyalpu. poliyum paruva vilaṅkukaḷiyum muṭṭiyiṭum paruvap paṟavikaḷiyum viṭi eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. viṭittal paruttal.
iḷamikkuppiṉ mūppum cākkāṭum nērvatāl iḷamik karuttiṉiṉṟē aḻiyāmik karuttut tōṉṟum. evvaḷavu uṭaluram peṟṟavarāyiṉum, nakiccuviyum uvamiyum paṟṟiyaṉṟik kiḻavaṉ kiḻaviyik kumaraṉ kumariyeṉṉum vaḻakka miṉmi kāṇka.
kumarik kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyil, kumari eṉa oru pēriyāṟu miruntatu.
 maturi
maturi eṉpatu kumari malit toṭariṟ piṟantu kiḻakku mukamāy ōṭik kīḻ kaṭaliṟ kalantatum, kaṅki pōlum periyatumāṉa paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅkariyil aminta pāṇṭiyariṉ mutal talinakarum talikkaḻaka irukkiyumākum.
pāṇṭiyar matik kulattārākaliṉ, tam kula mutalākak karutiya matiyiṉ peyarāl, tam mutal talinakarkku matiri eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. atu piṉṉar maturi eṉattirintatu.
o.nō: kuti-kutiri, etirki-etuki
kumarimali muḻukumuṉpō muḻukiya piṉpō, kumari nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar vaḻiyiṉarē, kaṇṇapirāṉ vāḻnta maturiyiyum amittaṉar. ataṟku appeyariṭṭatu, avar tam muṉṉōr iṭatti niṉivu kūrtaṟ poruṭṭākum. kaṇṇapirāṉ oru tiraviṭa maṉṉaṉē.
kaṇṇapirāṉ kālamākiya pāratak kālattil viki maturi yilli. ātalāl, nāvalan tēyattil iraṇṭāvatu ēṟpaṭṭa tum vaṭamaturi yeṉap paṭṭatum kaṇṇaṉ maturiyē. ataṉ peyar annāṭṭu moḻikkēṟpa matrā eṉap piṉṉart tirintatu.
civaperumāṉ taṉ caṭimuṭiyiluḷḷa matiyiṉiṉṟu matuvip poḻinta iṭam maturi eṉpatu. tolkatikkaṭṭu.
viki maturiyic cārnta tirumaruta muṉṟuṟiyāl maturip peyar vanta teṉpatu, pēraṉ peyarāl pāṭṭaṉ peyar peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum kūṟṟi okkum. maturiyeṉṟu mutaliṟ peyar peṟṟatu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅkariyatu, eṉpati maṟantu viṭal kūṭātu.
kaṉṉi
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyira aṭiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu kumariyāṟē.
parataṉ eṉṉum māvēntaṉiṉ makaḷākiya kumariyiṉ peyarāl, peyar peṟṟatu kumarik kaṇṭam eṉpatum, tolkatik kaṭṭē.
kumari kaṉṉi eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ coṟkaḷātalāl, kumariyāṟu kaṉṉiyeṉavumpaṭum.
maṉṉu māli veṇkuṭiyāṉ
vaḷiyāc ceṅkō latuvōccik
kaṉṉi taṉṉip puṇarntālum
pulavāy vāḻi kāvēri.
eṉṉum cilappatikārak kāṉal varippāṭal, kumariyāṟṟik kaṉṉiyeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṉṉi eṉpatum kāḷiyiṉ peyarē.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum col, makkaḷiṉattup peṇṇik kuṟikkum pōtu , pūppaṭintu maṇamākāta peṇṇik kuṟikkum. oru peṇ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum maṇamākātirukkalāmātalāl, iḷaṅkaṉṉi kaṉṉikiyeṉappaṭuvāḷ. ki eṉpatu oru kuṟumip poruḷ piṉṉoṭṭu (Diminutive suffix.)
 o. nō : kuṭi (vīṭu) - kuṭiki (ciṟu vīṭu) - kuṭici.
pūppaṭiyāta ciṟumiyiyum maṇamāṉa peṇṇiyum, kaṉṉiyeṉṟu collum vaḻakkamilli. kaṉṉi kaḻital, kaṉṉiyaḻital, kaṉṉiyaḻittal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum collik kaṉyā eṉṟum, kaṉṉiki eṉṉum collik kaṉyakā eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tirittu, ciṟumi, makaḷ eṉṟa poruḷkaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvar. ataṟkēṟpa, kaṉ (tikaḻ), kaṉa (ciṟu) eṉpavaṟṟi vērākak kāṭṭuvar.
tikaḻtalik kuṟikkum kaṉṉeṉum col valintu poruttu vatākum. ciṟumiyik kuṟikkum kaṉa eṉṉum col pūp paṭinta peṇṇiṟkup poruntātu. mikac ciṟiyatik kuṉṉi yeṉpatu tamiḻmarapu. kul - kuṉ - kuṉṉi. kul - kuḷ - kuṟu, naṉṉi yum kuṉṉiyum eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku. kuṉ - kaṉ - kaṉa (kana).
kaṉṉutal eṉpatu paḻuttalik kuṟikkum ōr aruntamiḻc col. veppattiṉālāvatu aḻuttattiṉālāvatu uḷḷaṅki yilum uḷḷaṅkālilum arattaṅkaṭṭic civantuviṭṭāl, ati arattaṅ kaṉṉutal eṉpar. kaṉi (paḻam) eṉṉum col kaṉṉi (paḻuttatu) eṉpataṉ tokuttalē. naku eṉṉum mutaṉili vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉapiṉ, naki eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyar mutaṉiliyāy vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatē kaṉi eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum. pūppu eṉṉum coṟpōṉṟē, kaṉṉutal eṉpatum nilittiṇikkuriyatāyiruppatum, Mature eṉṉum āṅkila vaḻakkum, iṅkuk karutattakkaṉa.
kumarik kaṇṭam 
1. maṟinta kumarik kaṇṭam (The Lost Lemuria)
nam ñālattiṉ mēṟparappil, iṉṟu kāynta nilamāyiruppatu oru kālattiṟ kaṭalaṭi nilamāyum, iṉṟu kaṭalaṭi nilamā yiruppatu oru kālattiṟ kāynta nilamāyum, irunta teṉṟu aṟiviyalāṟ potuvāka oppuk koḷḷappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. nilanūlār cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, inta nilamūḻkalkaḷum eḻuccikaḷum nērntuḷḷa nilapparappiṉ tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa pakutikaḷik kuṟittukkāṭṭak kūṭiyavarāy iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. maṟinta aṟṟilāṇṭicuk kaṇṭa vuṇmi ituvari aṟiviyalulakiṟ ciṟṟaḷavē oppam peṟṟiruppiṉum. ilemuriyā eṉṟu peyariṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oru paranta teṉkaṇṭam varalāṟṟiṟku muntiyatoru kālattil iruntatupaṟṟi aṟiviyalāriṉ potuk karuttoṟṟumi nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu.  (The Lost Lemuria by Scott Elliot. p. 1)
kumarik kaṇṭamē māntaṉ piṟantakam (tōrā. ki.mu. 1,00,000)
intumāvāri muṉpu oru kaṇṭāyiruntatu. atu cantāt tīvu kaḷiliruntu āciyāviṉ teṉkari vaḻiyē āppirikkāviṉ kiḻakkukkarivari parantiruntatu. ippaḻaṅkālak kaṇṭattiṟku, atil vāḻntirunta māntaṉ pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷ paṟṟi, kiḷēṟṟar (Sclater) eṉṉum āṅkilēyar ilemuriyā (Lemuria) eṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟār. atōṭu, atu māntaṉ piṟantaka māyiruntirukkak kūṭumeṉpatu paṟṟi, mikunta mutaṉmi vāynta tākum.  (History of Creation by Earnst Haeckel, Vol. 1, p. 361).
ilemuriyarē varalāṟṟiṟkut terinta mutal tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa māntaṉ vakuppāreṉpatiyum, ilemuriyāk kaṇṭamē māntaṉ nākarikattiṉ uṇmiyāṉa piṟappiṭam eṉpatiyum, karutip pārkkumpōtu, ilemuriyāviṉiṉṟum ataṉ makkaḷi ṉiṉṟumē māntaṉiṉ iṟṟi vakuppiṉaṅkaḷellām tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa eṉṉum iṉpuṟuttum uṇmiyik kāṇkiṟōm.  (Lemura - The Lost continent of the Pacific, p. 181)
iṉṟuḷḷa aiṅ kaṇṭaṅkaḷuḷ, āttirēliyāvēṉum amerikkāvēṉum airōppāvēṉum (māntaṉiṉ) immuntaka mākavō paratīcu eṉṟu collappaṭum viṇṇulakamākavō mānta iṉattiṉ piṟantakamākavō iruntirukka muṭiyātu. perumpāṟ cūḻnilikaḷ teṉṉinti yāviyē nāṭumiṭamākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟuḷḷa kaṇṭaṅkaḷuḷ, teṉṉāciyā vallātu ivvakiyiṟ karutakkūṭiya orē kaṇṭam āppirikkāvē. āyiṉ, māntaṉiṉ muntakam, kiḻakkil appāli yintiyāvum cantāt tīvukaḷum variyum, mēṟkil maṭakācukkarum āppirikkāviṉ teṉkiḻakkuk kari variyum, āciyāviṉ teṉkari yoṭṭi, atu iṉṟu uḷḷa aḷavu (orukāl ataṉoṭu oṉṟāka iṇintu) parantiruntatum, iṉṟu intumāvāriyiṉ kīḻ mūḻkik kiṭappatumāṉa oru kaṇṭamāyirunta teṉṟu uṉṉuvik kum cūḻnilikaḷ (ciṟappāka vaṭṭāraviyal uṇmikaḷ) pala vuḷḷaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷum nilittiṇiyum paṟṟiya pala ñāla nūluṇmikaḷ, attakiya teṉṉintiyak kaṇṭattiṉ muṉ ṉuṇmiyip peritum nampattakka tākkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu, muṉpē kūṟiyuḷḷōm............. inta ilemuriyā māntaṉiṉ muntakamā yiruntateṉṟu koḷvataṉāl, makkaḷiṉaṅkaḷiṉ nāṭupeyarvā lēṟpaṭṭa ñālaviyaṟ pātīṭṭiṉ viḷakkattiṟku mikat tuṇi ceykiṉṟōm.  (History of Creation, Vol. II, pp. 125-6)
māntaṉ tōṉṟi vāḻtaṟkum paṭippaṭiyāka nākarika vaḷarcci yaṭitaṟkum ēṟṟa nāṉila amippu, teṉṉāṭṭilēyē iṉṟum ciṟappākavuḷḷatu.
iyaṟki allatu anākarika māntaṉ, iyaṟkiyāka viḷiyum kāykaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum vēṭṭiyāṭip peṟum vilaṅku paṟavi yiṟicciyiyum, uṇṭu vāḻvataṟkēṟṟa iṭam, kuṟiñci eṉṉum malinilam; ataṟkaṭuttapaṭiyāka, āṭumāṭukaḷic ciṟappāka vaḷarttum vāṉavārip payirkaḷi viḷittum vāḻvataṟku ēṟṟa iṭam, pulveḷiyuṅ kuṟuṅkāṭumuḷḷa mulli nilam; ataṟkaṭuttapaṭiyāka, naṉceyp payirkaḷiyum, puṉceyp payirkaḷiyum naṉṟāka viḷivittu, niliyāka vāḻntu, nāḷaṭiviṟ ciṟṟūr pērūrum pērūr mūtūrum āki, kittoḻilum vāṇikamum vaḷarntu, kalviyum aracum nakarum nakaramum tōṉṟi, makkaḷ nākarikam aṭitaṟkēṟṟa iṭam, nilavaḷamum nīrvaḷamum mikka maruta nilam; ataṟkaṭuttapaṭiyāka, palavaki marakkalaṅkaḷ puṉintu kaṭal kaṭantu nīrvāṇikañ ceytu palvakip paṇṭaṅ koṇarntu nāṭṭi vaḷampaṭuttu taṟku ēṟṟa iṭam, kaṭal cārnta neytal nilam. innāl vaki nilamum aṭuttaṭuttiruntatum iṉṟum iruppatum muṟiyē, muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭamum iṟṟit tamiḻakamumē.
pāli yeṉpatu, mulliyum ciṟumalik kuṟiñciyum mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil nīrnilikaḷellām vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭu nilamuñ cuṭum nili. piṉṉar maḻikkālattiṟ pāli nilam taḷirttum nīr nirampiyum mīṇṭum mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyumāka māṟiviṭum. ātalāl, aintiṇikaḷuṭ pālikku niliyāṉa nilamilli. ataṉāl ñālatti nāṉilam eṉṟaṉar.
iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pōṉṟē, paṇṭit tamiḻakamākiya kumari nāṭṭiluḷḷa mēl kōṭiyilēyē oru paṉmali yaṭukkattup perumalit toṭariruntatu. atu kumari yeṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarāṟ kumarimali yeṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉ teṉ kōṭiyiṟ paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉum kaṅki pōlum māpēriyāṟum, vaṭa kōṭiyiṟ kumariyeṉṉum kāviri pōlum pēriyāṟum, tōṉṟik kiḻakku mukamāyp pāyntōṭiṉa. nilam mēṟkil uyarntum kiḻakkil tāḻntum iruntataṉāl kuṭatici mēltici (mēl - mēṟku) yeṉṟum, kuṇatici kīḻt tici yeṉṟum (kīḻ-kīḻkku-kiḻakku), peyar peṟṟaṉa. iṟṟit tamiḻakattilum oruvaṉ mēṟṟiciyiṉiṉṟu kīḻttici variṉ, kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, eṉṉum nālvaki nilamum muṟiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tāḻntu aṭuttaṭuttirukkak kāṇ pāṉ. naṇṇilakkaṭal (Mediterranean Sea) oru kālattil āciyāvi ūṭaṟuttuc ceṉṟu amiti māvāriyiṟ (Pacific Ocean) kalattatākat terivatāl, viviliyat toṭakkap pottakattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, ētēṉ tōṭṭam eṉṉum māntaṉ piṟantakam, mecoppottāmiyā nāṭṭil irun tirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉiṉ, annilappakuti paṇṭu kaṭalā yiruntirukkum.
nilanūliyal ūḻikaḷuḷ (Geological Eras) iṟutiyatāṉa puttiya luyir ūḻiyic (Cainozoic Era) cērnta nāṉkām maṇṭalattuk (Quarternary Period) kaḻipallaṇmik (Pleistocene) kālappirivil ki.mu. 4,75,000 mutal ki.mu. 50,000 vari nāṉmuṟi paṉikkaṭṭip paṭala muṟpaṭarcci (Glacial Advance) vaṭa kōḷattil nikaḻnta tākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, airōppāvilēṉum vaṭa amerikkāvilēṉum māntaṉ piṟantakam iruntirukka muṭiyātu.
mutaṟkāla māntaṉukkēṟṟa veppamum, iyaṟki vaḷamum, naṇṇilak kōṭṭic (Equator) cārnta veppa nāṭṭilēyē irukka muṭiyum. attakiya nāṭu muḻukippōṉa kumarināṭē.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu allatu paṭikkappaṭṭatu eṇṇiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmik kālamātalāl, attakiya toṉmi vāyntatum kumarināṭē.
iṟṟi nila amippiṉpaṭi, irutiṇiyuyirikaḷum pāreṅ kum paraviyirukkum nilimi, kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉ kōṭiyi māntaṉ piṟantakamākak koṇṭāltāṉ viḷaṅkum.
kumarināṭē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam (tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000)
(1) 	intiyāviṟkuḷḷēyē vaḻaṅkum tamiḻ uḷḷiṭṭa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ allatu teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ, teṟkil varavarat tiruntiyum colvaḷam mikkum tūymippaṭṭum ilakkiya vilakkaṇamuṟṟum ceṟintum uḷḷaṉa.		tirāviṭac coṟkaṭkellām perumpālum mūlamum muntiya vaṭivum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa.
(2) 	paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa mutal karu eṉṉum iruvakip poruḷkaḷum, iṉṟum teṉ ṉāṭṭiṟkuriyavākavē uḷḷaṉa.
(3) 	paṇṭit tamiḻar iṟantu pōṉa tam muṉṉōrit teṉpulattār eṉṟē kuṟittaṉar.
teṉpulattār teyvam viruntokkal tāṉeṉṟāṅ
kimpulattā ṟōmpal tali.  (kuṟaḷ. 43)
teṉpula vāḻnark karuṅkaṭa ṉiṟukkum
poṉpōṟ putalvar peṟāa tīrum.  (puṟam-6)
(4)	paṇṭit tamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ, mutaliraṇṭum muḻukippōṉa kumarināṭṭilēyē iruntaṉa.
(5) 	ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntavarum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ iḷavalārum, palkalip perumpulavarum, muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻumuṉivarum, naṭunili tiṟampātavarumāṉa iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi.  (cilap. 11 : 19-22)
eṉṟum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālap pulavaruḷ oruvarāṉa neṭṭi miyār
 	cennīrp pacumpoṉ vayiriyark kītta
 	munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
     naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē  (puṟam.9)
eṉṟum, talikkaḻakat teṉmaturiyit taṉ karimēṟ koṇ ṭirunta kumari nāṭṭup paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiyum, akkālattup pāṇṭiyariyum pāṭiyuḷḷār.
(6)	naṭavaracaṉ tilli maṉṟu, tamiḻ ñālattiṉ neñcat tāvākiya naṭuviṭamākavē pāṇṭiyaṉāl amikkap peṟṟatu.
 	tillimaṉṟu vaṭapāṟ paṉimalikkum teṉpāṟ kumari malikkum naṭuviṭattil amintataṉālēyē, pērulakattiṉ neñcattāvi nikarttāyiṟṟu.
 (7) cilappatikāra vēṉiṟkāti mutalaṭikku varinta vuriyil,
 	toṭiyōḷ - peṇpāṟ peyarāṟ kumariyeṉpa tāyiṟṟu. ākavē, teṉpāṟ kaṇṇatōr āṟṟiṟkup peyarām. āṉāl, neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamum toṭiyōḷ natiyumeṉ ṉātu puvamumeṉṟatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, mutalūḻiyiṟutik kaṇ teṉmaturiyakattut talic caṅkattu ......... nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar eṇṇiṟanta paripāṭalum mutunāriyum mutukurukum kaḷariyāviriyu muḷḷiṭṭa vaṟṟip puṉintu terintu nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟi yāṇṭu irīiyaṉār kāyciṉa vaḻuti mutaṟ kaṭuṅkōṉ īṟāyuḷḷār eṇpattoṉpatiṉmar ........... ak kālattu avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavelli yākiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉumāṟṟiṟkum kumari yeṉṉumāṟṟiṟku miṭiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmali vāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅkanāṭum, ēḻmaturi nāṭum, ēḻmuṉpālināṭum, ēḻpiṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟa nāṭum, ēḻkuṇakāri nāṭum, ēḻkuṟumpaṉi nāṭu eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum, kumarikollam mutaliya paṉmali nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrk kumari vaṭaperuṅkōṭṭiṉ kāṟum, kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāṟ kumariyākiya puvameṉṟā reṉṟuṇarka. eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār ciṟappuk kuṟippu varintuḷḷār. itu avar kaṭṭik kūṟiya ceytiyāka irukka muṭiyātu.
 	ivaṟṟāl, kumarināṭē tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam allatu tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam eṉpatu, teritaru tēṟṟamām.
kumarikkaṇṭap parappu
oru mutaṉmiyāṉa iṉaviyal uṇmiyum ciṟanta poruṭ pāṭuḷḷatum etuveṉiṉ, pōrṉiyōviluḷḷa tayakkar eṉṉum marapiṉar maramēṟuvatip paṟṟi uvālēcu varin tuḷḷa varaṇaṉi, teṉṉintiya āṉimali vāṇarāṉa kāṭarukku ovvoru nuṇkuṟippilum muṟṟum poruntu mātalāl ammaliyilum eḻutappaṭalām eṉpatē. intiyat tīvak kuṟiyil, (Peninsula) eṉakkut terintavari, kāṭariṭi yum tiruvāṅkūr malivēṭariṭiyum maṭṭum peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkam, veṭṭup paṟkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum allatu avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum, arampam pōlaṉṟik kūr  nuṉik kuviyamākac cīvik koḷvatākum. intac cīval, kāṭariṭiyē piyaṉkaḷukkup patiṉeṭṭām akaviyilum peṇpiḷḷi kaṭkup pattām akaviyil allatu ati yaṭuttum. uḷikoṇṭō aṟuvāḷ koṇṭō araṅkoṇṭō ceyyappaṭuvatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. kīṟṟum (Skeat) piḷākutaṉum (Blagden) maliyāt tīvakkuṟi cākkuṉar (Jakuns) tampaṟkaḷik kūrākkic cīvik koḷḷum vaḻakkattik kavaṉittirukkiṉṟaṉar. tiru. kirāpōrṭu (Crawford) maliyāt tīvakaṇattil palli arāvuvatum kaṟuppākkuvatum tirumaṇat tiṟku munti nikaḻavēṇṭiya caṭaṅkeṉṟum, palliyarāviyirup patu orupeṇ mūppaṭintiruppatik kāṭṭum potuvāṉa vakiyeṉṟum namakkut terivikkiṉṟār par. (Dr.) kē.ti. (K.T.) pireyacu (Preuss) oru kaṭṭuriyil malākkāviluḷḷa ciṟunīka rōvariṉ mūṅkiṟ cīppukaḷiluḷḷa kōlaṅkaḷi nuṭpamāka varaṇittu, avaṟṟit teṉṉintiyak kāṭar aṇiyum mūṅkiṟ cīppukaḷiluḷḷa viyakkattakkavāṟu ottuḷḷa kōlaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oppu nōkkukiṉṟār. antak kōlam, nāṉ coṉṉavāṟu aṇiyiyal vaṭivaḷavik kōlamaṉṟeṉṟum, paṭaveḻuttuk kaḷiṉ toṭareṉṟum, oru koḷkiyi nuṇṇitāka viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟār. kīṟṟum piḷākutaṉum cemaṅkup (Semang) peṇṭir tam muṭiyil aṇiyum viyappāṉa cīppuvaki, muṟṟi lum nōykkāppākavē aṇivatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟateṉṟu kavaṉittuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. intac cīppukaḷ perumpālum mūṅkilālēyē ceyyappaṭṭu, kaṇakkaṟṟa kōlaṅkaḷāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ enta iraṇṭum eṉṟēṉum muṟṟum ottatilli. ovvoru nōykkum oru ciṟappukkōlam uriyatākac collappaṭṭatu. attakiya cīppukaḷ perakki luḷḷa paṅkaṉ (Pangan) cemaṅku cākki (Saki) marapiṉarālum, cemaṅkucākkik kalappumarapiṉaruḷ māperumpāṉmi yarālum, aṇiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tiru. viṉceṉṟu (Vincent) avarukkut terintavari, kāṭar cīppukaḷ nōykkāppākak karutappaṭa villiyeṉṟum, avaṟṟiluḷḷa varivukaḷ mantirak kuṟippuk koṇṭaṉavallaveṉṟum, eṉakkut terivittiruk kiṉṟār. oru kāṭaṉ eppōtum oru cīppuc ceytu. taṉ maṉivikkut tirumaṇattiṟku muṉpu allatu tirumaṇac caṭaṅ kiṉ iṟutiyil, koṭuttal vēṇṭum. iḷaṅkāḷiyar tammuḷ yār intac cīppuc ceyya muṭiyumeṉṟu, oruvarō ṭoruvar icalik koṇṭu ceykiṉṟaṉar. cila camiyaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ cīppukkaḷiṟ putumiyāṉa uruvaṅkaḷip poṟik kiṉṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tiru. viṉceṉṟu oru cuvark kaṭikāra mukappi mika naṉṟākap poṟitta oru cīppip pārttiruk kiṉṟār.
kumarikkaṇṭam kiḻakkil āttirēliyāviyum mēṟkil teṉṉāppirikkāviyum vaṭakkil intiyāviyum taṉṉuṭaṉ iṇittuk koṇṭirunta perunilapparappātalālum māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatiliruntu tamiḻar muḻunākarikam aṭintatu vari, ēṟattāḻa īrilakkam āṇṭu makkaḷ atiṟ kuṭiyirunta ṉālum, annilavāṇar palvēṟu nākarika nilippaṭṭavarāyum palvēṟu moḻiyiṉarāyum iruntaṉar. āyiṉum, mēṟpākattiṉar perum pālum tamiḻarum kīḻppākattiṉar perumpālum nākarum āvar. nākarikarum anākarikarumāka nākar irucārār. nākarika nākaruḷ oru vakuppārē kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ perumpakuti mūḻkiya piṉ amerikkāviyaṭintu māyā nākarikatti vaḷarttākat terikiṉṟatu.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻ (tōrā. 1,00,000-50,000)
ulakil mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiya tiruntiya moḻi tamiḻē
moḻiyamippu
uṇarcci yolikaḷ (Emotional Sounds) viḷiyolikaḷ (Vocative Sounds) kuṟippolikaḷ (Symbolic Sounds) vāyviṉi yolikaḷ kuḻavivaḷarppolikaḷ (Nursery Sounds) cuṭṭolikaḷ (Deictic Sounds), eṉṉum eḻuvaki yolikaḷik koṇṭa iyaṟki moḻi (Natural Language) allatu muḻittal moḻi (Inarticu-late Speech) muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭintapiṉ, paṇpaṭṭamoḻi (Cultivated Language) allatu iḻittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) tōṉṟiṟṟu. muṟiyē cēymiyaṇmi muṉmiyuṇart tum ā, i, ū eṉṉum muccuṭṭukkaḷiṉiṉṟē, iḻittal moḻi perumpālum uruvāyiṟṟu. ammūṉṟaṉuḷḷum muṉmiccuṭṭākiya ūkāramē, tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ mukkāṟ paṅkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakutiyip piṟappittuḷḷatu.
īkārattiṉiṉṟu ēkāramum ūkārattiṉiṉṟu ōkāramum, mōṉit tirivākat tōṉṟiṉa. mutaṟkaṇ neṭilākavē tōṉṟiya uyirkaḷ piṉṉark kuṟilākak kuṟukiṉa. akaramum ikaramuñ cērntu ai eṉṟum, akaramum ukaramuñ cērntu au eṉṟum, iru puṇarolikaḷ (Diphthongs) eḻuntaṉa. avi oliyaḷavil muṟiyē ay, av eṉa olittaṉa.
kocci vaḻakkil, ili eṉpatu eli eṉṟum uṉakku eṉpatu oṉakku eṉṟum olippatiṉiṉṟu, ēkāra ōkāram allatu ekara okaram mōṉit tirivākat tōṉṟiyami aṟiyap paṭum.
itaḻ kuvintolikkum muṉmic cuṭṭāṉa u eṉṉum viti yeḻuttu, lakarameyyīṟu peṟṟu ul eṉṉum mūlavēriyum ataṉoṭu coṉmutal meykaḷ cērnta kul, cul, tul, nul, pul, mul eṉṉum vaḻivērkaḷiyum tōṟṟuvikka, av veḻuvēriṉiṉṟum mūlavaṭiyum vaḻiyaṭikaḷum tirintu, muṉmi, muṉvarutal (tōṉṟutal), muṉcellal (cellal), neruṅkutal (kūṭutal) poruntutal, vaḷital, tuḷittal, turuval ākiya eṇperuṅ karuttukkaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa kiḷik karuttuk kaḷiyum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa nuṇkaruttukkaḷiyuṅ koṇṭu, māperum pāl tamiḻc coṟkaḷip piṟappittuḷḷaṉa.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum, ulaka vaḻakkumoḻi potumakkaḷ amippum, ilakkiyam pulamakkaḷ amippum ākum. kumari nāṭṭu makkaḷ nākarikam mutirntu paṇpaṭṭavarum nuṇmati yiṉarumāyiruntataṉāl, ellā eḻuttolikaḷiyum cevvi yāka olittum, ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum viṉikaḷiyum nuṭpamāka vēṟupaṭutti avaṟṟiṟ kēṟpac ciṟappuc coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyum, pakuttaṟivaṭippaṭiyiṟ poruḷkaḷip pāku paṭuttiyum, tam moḻiyi vaḷarttum vaḻaṅkiyum vantaṉar.
oliyeḷimi
avar ulakamutal māntaṉiṉattārātaliṉ, avar vāyiṟ piṟkālattilum, piṟamoḻikaḷilum tōṉṟiya vallolikaḷ eḻavēyilli. tamiḻiluḷḷa ka ca ṭa ta pa vum camaṟkirutam eṉṉum vaṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa ka (kka) ca (cca) ṭa (ṭṭa) ta (tta) pa (ppa) vum, vaṉmiyil ottaṉavalla. tamiḻkkakaram iraṭṭittāl tāṉ vaṭamoḻittaṉikkakaratti ottolikkum. āṅkilattilum aṅṅaṉamē maker eṉṉum col, mēkkar eṉṟē iraṭṭitta kakaravoli koṇṭiruttal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi valliṉamum.
mūccoliyum (Aspirate Sound) taṉitta eṭuppoliyum tamiḻuk kiṉmiyāl, mūccoli koṇṭa valleḻuttō eṭuppoli (Voice Sound) koṇṭa taṉi valleḻuttō tamiḻil illavē yilli. melliṉa meykaḷiṉ piṉ aṭuttu varum valliṉa meykaḷē, tamiḻil eṭuppoli koḷḷum.
mūccoliyum taṉiyeṭuppoliyuṅkoṇṭa teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam, mutaliya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻi ṉiṉṟē tirintaṉavēṉum, mikamikap piṟkālattaṉa vātaliṉ av vāriyat taṉmi yaṭintaṉaveṉa aṟika.
ivvuṇmiyaṟiyātār, tamiḻilum mutaṟkālattil eṭuppoli kaḷ (g, j, d, d, b,) iruntu, piṉṉar eṭuppilāvolikaḷāka māṟiṉa eṉap pitaṟṟuvar. tamiḻ tōṉṟiyatu kumari nāṭeṉṉum uṇmi yaṟiyiṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟār. eṭuppilāvoli eṭup poliyāka māṟumēyaṉṟi, eṭuppoli eṭuppilāvoli yākat tiriyātu. piñcu muṟṟik kāyāvatēyaṉṟi, kāy iḷantu mīṇṭum piñcāvatilli. māntaṉ varalāṟṟil tamiḻaṉ ciṟu piḷḷi pōṉṟavaṉātaliṉ, avaṉ vāyiṟ piṟkāla māntariṉ vallolikaḷ eḻavilli.
tamiḻiluḷḷa cakaram, camaṟkirutak kicukicuppolikaḷuḷ (Sibilants) mutalatiyē ottatākum. ati eṭuppum mūccoliyumuḷḷa (Voiceless Unaspirate) camaṟkiruta aṇṇa valleḻuttiṟkuc (Pala-tal Surd) camamākak koḷvatu, tamiḻiyalpi aṟiyāmiyēyām.
ḻa, ṟa potumakkaḷē tōṟṟuvitta olikaḷātalāl, avi tamittum colluṟupākavum varumpōtellām, avaṟṟi avarkaḷ muḻuc cevviyākavē olittu vantaṉar.
coṟpuṇarcciyum potumakkaḷ ceyalē. teṟkutteru kaṟṟāḻi, mutumakkaṭṭāḻi, pāṟcōṟu, āṭṭi vāriyam, aṟṟik kūli, karumakkuṟi eṉa mūvakit tiripup puṇarcci yiyum, pēccilum eḻuttilum iṟukak kaṭippiṭittaṉar.
poruṭpākupāṭu
ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum uyir, mey, uyirmey (uyirōṭu kūṭiya mey) eṉa mūṉṟākap pakuttum; avaṟṟuḷ uyiri nilit tiṇi iyaṅkutiṇi eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttum; avaṟṟuḷ nilittiṇiyip pul, pūṇṭu, ceṭi, koṭi, maram eṉap palavākap pākupaṭuttiyum; avaṟṟiṉ uṟuppukkaḷi vēr, tūr, aṭi, kavi, (kompu, kiḷi, pōttu, kuccu) ili, pū, kāy eṉap pirittum; avaṟṟuḷ iliyit tāḷ, tōki, ōli, tōṭu, ili, aṭi eṉṟum; pūvi arumpu, pōtu, malar, vī cemmal eṉṟum, kāyip piñcu, pirukku, kāy, kaṉi, neṟṟu, vaṟṟal eṉṟum; nuṇṇiya vakiyil vēṟupaṭuttiyum; piñci yum kaccal (vāḻi), vaṭu (mā), mūcu (palā) kurumpi (teṉṉi, paṉi) eṉat taṉittaṉic ciṟappup peyariṭṭum; vaḻaṅkiṉar.
viṉivēṟupāṭu
poruḷkaḷip pōṉṟē viṉikaḷiyum nuṇṇitāka vēṟupaṭutti, ciṟappuc coṟkaḷāl, avaṟṟik kuṟittaṉar.
 e-ṭu :

		viruppoṭu pārttal
nōṭutal 	= 	āyntu pārttal

ulaka vaḻakkil, iḻintōṉ, ottōṉ, uyarntōṉ eṉṉum muttiṟattik kuṟikka, nī, nīm, nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ nīṅkaḷ eṉpati yoṭṭit taṉmiyilum ēṅkaḷ eṉṉum iraṭṭip paṉmic col tōṉṟiviṭṭatu.
ēṉ, ēm eṉpaṉa iṟṟit tamiḻil eḻuvāyāka vaḻaṅkā viṭiṉum, viṉimuṟṟīṟukaḷāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
e-ṭu : vantēṉ, vantēm.
illiruntu. iṉiṉṟu eṉṉum 5-ām vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ, ikkālattiṟpōl iṭattiliruntu iṭattiṉiṉṟu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiyirukkalām.
ē eṉṉum aṭi yā eṉṟu tirintapōtu, yāṉ, yām, yāṅkaḷ eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āyiṉ, avi vēṟṟumi yēṟkātu eḻuvāyaḷavil niṉṟuviṭṭaṉa. ātalāl, avaṟṟiṟkum ēm, ēṅkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumi vaṭivukaḷē uriyavāyiṉa.
yā eṉṉum tirivaṭi nā eṉṟu tirintapōtu, nāṉ, nām, nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avi piṉvarumāṟu vēṟṟumiyēṟṟaṉa.

nā eṉṉum tirivaṭiyāl orumippeyar vaṭiviṟ poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṟku iṭamiṉṟēṉum, paṉmip peyar vaṭiviṟ ciṟanta poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṟku iṭamuṇṭāyiṟṟu. nām, nāṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa. taṉmiyōṭu muṉṉiliyi uḷappaṭuttum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip paṉmip peyarkaḷāyiṉa.

ēṅkaḷ eṉpataṟkuc coṉṉa kuṟippi nāṅkaḷ eṉpataṟkuṅ koḷka.
nāṅkaḷ eṉpatu, iṟṟit tamiḻil muṉṉiliyi uḷappaṭuttātu tavaṟāka yāṅkaḷ eṉpataṟkup pakaramāka (patilāka) vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
nām eṉṉum peyar, viṉimuṟṟīṟākumpōtu ām eṉṟu mutaṉmey nīṅkum; am eṉṟu kuṟukavuñ ceyyum.
 e-ṭu : naṭantām, naṭantam
ēm eṉṉum paṉmip peyar, taṉit taṉmip peyarāyiruppa toṭu, paṭarkkiyi uḷappaṭuttum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip peyarākavum āḷappeṟum.
e-ṭu ; ēm 1. yām (taṉit taṉmi)

e-ṭu : naṭantēm, naṭantem.

5.	nuṉṉiliruntu, 	nummiliruntu. 	nuṅkaḷiliruntu,
 	nuṉṉiṉiṉṟu 	nummiṉiṉṟu 	nuṅkaḷiṉṟu
6.	nuṉ 	num 	nuṅkaḷ
 	(nuṉatu, nuṉa) 	(numatu, numa) 	(nuṅkaḷatu, nuṅkaḷa)
7.	nuṉṉiṭam 	nummiṭam 	nuṅkaḷiṭam
 nū aṭi piṉṉar nī eṉṟu tiriyavuñ ceytatu.
		orumi 	paṉmi 	uyarvuppaṉmi
1. 	nīṉ 	nīm 	nīṅkaḷ
2. 	niṉṉi 	nimmi 	niṅkaḷi

7.	niṉṉiṭam 	nimmiṭam 	niṅkaḷiṭam
nīṉ eṉṉum orumip peyar, piṉṉē nī eṉa īṟukeṭṭu, ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peruvaḻakkākavum ilakkiya vaḻakkil muḻu vaḻakkāka vum vaḻaṅkattalippaṭṭatu. nīṉ eṉṉum peyar vaḻakki, iṉ ṟum pāṇṭi nāṭṭu nāṭārkula vaḻakkiṟ kāṇalām. kaṉṉaṭat til appeyar ukaravīṟu peṟṟu nīṉu eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
nīm eṉṉum paṉmip peyarum, iruvaki vaḻakkilum arukiya vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl nī eṉṉum īṟukeṭṭapeyar ir eṉṉum paṭarkkip palarpālīṟu peṟṟu nīyir eṉṟāki nīm eṉpataṟkut talimāṟāka (patilāka) vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
nīm eṉṉum peyar vaḻakki, iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭṭuc ciṟṟūrp pakutikaḷiṟ kāṇalām.
nīyir eṉpatu. nāḷaṭivil nīvir eṉat tirintu piṉṉar nīr eṉat tokkatu.
nī, nīr (nīyir, nīvir) eṉṉum iruvaṭivukaṭkum taṉ vēṟṟumi yilli. ataṉāl, muṉṉataṟku ūṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ vēṟ ṟumiyum, piṉṉataṟku nūm allatu ūm eṉṉum peyariṉ vēṟṟumiyum, vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. nīr eṉṉum vaṭiviṟkuc coṉṉatē, nīyir, nīvir eṉṉum vaṭivukaṭkum okkum.
teṟkil ēṟattāḻa īrāyiraṅkal tolivu parantirunta kumari nāṭu muḻukippōṉamiyāl, atil vaḻaṅkiya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. mutaliru kaḻakattālum iyaṟṟappaṭṭum pōṟṟappaṭṭum vanta āyirakkaṇakkāṉa taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭamiyāl, avaṟṟil vaḻaṅkiya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ātalāl, mēṟkāṭṭiya taṉmi muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṟkum, avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumi vaṭivukaṭkum, iṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭilliyeṉa aṟika.
eṭṭiṟkumēl aṭutta eṇṇiṉ peyar akkālattil toṇṭu eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
 toṇṭu 	= 	9
 toṇpatu 	= 	90
 toṇṇuṟu 	= 	900
 toḷḷāyiram 	= 	9,000
 toṇpatiṉāyiram 	= 	90,000
 toḷḷilakkam 	= 	900,000
 toṇpatilakkam 	= 	9,000,000
 toṇkōṭi 	= 	90,000,000
oṉṟu mutal pattuvari eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ taṉic collāka vum ukara vīṟu koṇṭumē iruntaṉa.
palukkaṟ cemmi :
kaṇ, māṉ, ūr, kal, kūḻ, āḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum eṇ, īṉ, pār, col, umiḻ, kēḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉi kaḷum, ukara vīṟṟit tuṇikkoḷḷāmalum, tāy, vāy eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, cey, cāy eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉikaḷum, ikara vīṟṟit tuṇikkoḷḷāmalum olittaṉa.
 maram, avaṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, vantāṉ, pōṉāṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉikaḷum, iṟuti melliṉamey mūkkoli yaḷavilaṉṟi muṟṟum cevviyāka olittu vaḻaṅkiṉa.
 avaḷ, makaḷ eṉpaṉapōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, vantāḷ, pōṉāḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉikaḷum, iṟuti ḷakaramey olittē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
ilakkaṇa naṭi
irukkiṟatu, iruntatu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paṭarkki yoṉṟaṉ pāl viṉikaḷellām, koccivaṭivilaṉṟi ilakkaṇa vaṭivilēyē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
ellā viṉimuṟṟukkaḷum eḻuvāyk kēṟṟa īṟu peṟṟē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
e-ṭu :	nāṉ (yāṉ)	allēṉ
	yām, nām 	allēm, allōm
	nī 	alli
	nīm 	allīm
	nīr (nīyir, nīvir) 	allīr
	nīṅkaḷ 	allīṅkaḷ
avaṉallaṉ, avaḷallaḷ, avarallar, avarkaḷallarkaḷ, atuvaṉṟu, aviyalla.
aṉmi yiṉmip poruṭ coṟkaḷ teḷivāka vēṟupaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu : 	avaṉ vīṭṭil illi.
 	avaṉ pulavaṉ allaṉ.
irutiṇip pakuppu
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭilum pakuttaṟivāṟṟalum taliciṟantiruntataṉāl, pakuttaṟi vaṭippaṭiyilēyē uyar vakuppu tāḻ vakuppu eṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum iruvakup pāka vakuttu. pakuttaṟivuḷḷa uyirmeykaḷik kuṟikkuñ coṟ kaṭkē pālīṟu koṭuttum, aḵtillāta uyir meykaḷik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭku eṇṇīṟē koṭuttum, coṟṟoṭar amittaṉar.
 e-ṭu :	uḻavaṉ uḻukiṉṟāṉ
	uḻatti kaḷiyeṭukkiṉṟāṉ 	uyar vakuppu
	avaṉ vantāṉ
 	avaḷ pōṉāḷ 
 	kāḷi uḻukiṉṟatu
 	āvu mēykiṉṟatu
	āṇpaṉi vaḷarkiṉṟatu 	tāḻ vakuppu
	peṇpaṉi kāykkiṉṟatu.
	atu vantatu.
	avi pōyiṉa. 
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇam iyampal eṉṉum muṟi yil, ilakkaṇa nūlār pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaḷi uyartiṇi yeṉṟum, avarallāta uyiruḷḷaṉavum illaṉavu māṉa ellā vaṟṟiyum aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟum, kuṟittaṉar.
makkaḷip paṭittavarum ellām aṟintavaruvamāṉa, iṟivaṉ uyartiṇiyeṉpatic colla vēṇṭuvatilli.
ivvirutiṇip pakuppu tamiḻukkē ciṟappām. teluṅkil ittakiya toṉṟiruppiṉum, atu tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyatē.
colvaḷam
kumarināṭṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa piṉpum, tamiḻ oppuyarvaṟṟa colvaḷam mikka moḻiyāka uḷḷatu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, iṉṟum, collutalik kuṟikka aṟi iyampu, ici, uri, eṉ, ōtu, kiḷa, kiḷattu, kūṟu, cāṟṟu, ceppu, col, navil, nutal, nuval, noṭi, pakar, paṟi, paṉṉu, paṉuvu, pukal, pēcu, māṟu, miḻaṟṟu, moḻi, viḷ, viḷattu, viḷampu mutaliya coṟkaḷum.
ōṭutalik kuṟikka, ōṭu, kiṇṇu, toṅku, pari mutaliya coṟkaḷum,
yāṉiyik kuṟikka, āmpal, umpal, uvā, eṟumpi, ōṅkal, kaṭamā, kari, kaṟiyaṭi, kuñcaram, kimmali, kimmā, tumpi, tūṅkal, tōl, nālvāy, pūṭki, poṅkaṭi, maṟamali, mātiram, moy, vaḻuvi, vāraṇam, vēḻam mutaliya potuc coṟkaḷum; cinturam piṇimukam, pukarmukam mutaliya ciṟappuc coṟkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
aṟi mutal viḷampu īṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa coṟka ḷellām, collutal eṉṉum potupporuḷil ottaṉa vēṉum, collutal vakiyāṉa ciṟappup poruḷil vēṟu paṭṭaṉavām. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyavum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tirāviṭa moḻikaṭ kellān tāyāta lāl, muṉṉataṉ oruporuṭ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē, piṉṉavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum ovvoru collit terinteṭuttāḷu kiṉṟatu. itu tāy vīṭṭiluḷḷa oru viṉikkup payaṉpaṭum palvakik kalaṅkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi ovvoru makaḷum eṭuttup paḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatu.
 e-ṭu:	ceppu - ceppu (teluṅku)
		kiḷa - hēḷu (kaṉṉaṭam)
		paṟi - paṟa (maliyāḷam)
ceppu eṉṉum viṉic col, tamiḻil viṭi collutal eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷilum, teluṅkiṟ collutal eṉṉum potup poruḷilum, vaḻaṅkum. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉic coṟkaḷum taṉtaṉ ciṟappup poruḷi iḻakkum.
paṟital eṉṉum col, ētampaṟin talla ceytu (tiv. tiruvāy. 4; 6; 8) eṉṟu ilakkiya vaḻakkilum, collāmaṟ paṟiyāmal ōṭip pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
vīṭṭik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷuḷ, il eṉṉum teluṅkuc collum maṉi yeṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac collum maṭṭumaṉṟi, camaṟkirutattilum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkum kuṭi yeṉṉum collum tamiḻiluḷḷateṉṟu, tamiḻiṉ oru poruṭ colvaḷattik kālṭuvelār viyantiruttali nōkkuka.
camaṟkirum pirākiruta moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum tamiḻiṉiṉṟum, āṅkilam ulaka moḻikaḷuṭ palavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum, ērāḷamākak kaṭaṉ koṇṭiruppatāl, avaṟṟiluḷḷa oru poruṭ col vaḷatti viyattaṟkilli.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷ iṟṟit tamiḻil, illiyeṉiṉ, avi kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻil vaḻaṅki iṟantu paṭṭaṉa eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. colliṟkuc coṉṉatē col vaṭiviṟkum.
colvaṭivukaḷuḷ muṉṉatip piṉṉatiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭutti yaṟitaṟku, moḻiyārāycciyoṭu kūṭiya collārāycci vēṇṭum.
nāl - nālku - nāṉku
te. nāluku (g) ka. nālku (k)
 vāluḷip puravi..... nālkuṭaṉ pūṭṭi  (perumpāṇ. 489)
 nālku eṉpatu tamiḻvaṭivē.
tūymi
mutaliru kaḻakak kālattilum āriyar teṉṉāṭu varāmiyāl, akkaḻakaṅkaḷ irunta kumarināṭṭuttamiḻ, oliyiṟ pōṉṟē collilum poruḷilum ilakkiyattilum karuttilum muḻut tūymi peṟṟiruntatu. ilakkiyattuḷ ilakkaṇamum aṭaṅkum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar (tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000 - 10,000)
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar, perumpālum aintiṇippaṭṭa nāṭṭup puṟattārum, ciṟupāṉmi tiṇimayakkuṟṟa nakaravāṇarumāka, iruvaki vāḻcciyarāy iruntu vantaṉar.
kuṟiñcinili māntar, kolliyum paṟampum pōṉṟa mali kaḷiyaṭuttuk kūṭṭaṅkūṭṭamākak kuṭicikaḷil taṅki, vēṭṭiyāṭiyum kiḻaṅkakaḻntum tēṉeṭuttum tiṉi viḷittum, muraṭṭuk kampaḷiyuṭuttum murukaṉi vaṇaṅkiyum, vāḻntu vantaṉar. uṇavup poruḷkaḷic cuṭuvataṟkum eḷiya muṟiyiṟ camippataṟkum, iraviṟ koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷi ōṭṭuvataṟkum, tīkkaṭi kōlāl tīyuṇṭākkik koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu kuṟicciyeṉṟum ciṟukuṭiyeṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kuṟiñcinila makaḷir kuṟicolvatu kuṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉāṟ kuṟavar eṉṟum, vēṭṭiyāṭuvatāl vēṭṭuvar eṉṟum, kuṟiñci nila māntarkku irupeyarkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
mulli nila māntar, pulveḷiyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷiyaṇṭi, āṭumāṭerumiyākiya munnirikaḷiyum vaḷarttu pāl tayir mōr veṇṇey ney ākiya āṉinti maruta nilattum pāli nilattum viṟṟum, varaku, cāmi avari tuvari mutaliya vāṉavārip payirkaḷi viḷittum, pañcāṭiyum meṉ kampaḷiyum uṭuttum, pulvaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta maḻi poḻiyum mukil vaṇṇaṅkoṇṭa māyōṉ (kariyōṉ) eṉṉum teyvatti vaṇaṅkiyum, vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu, pāṭiyeṉṟum cēriyeṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
āyar, iṭiyar, potuvar, toḻuvar, toṟuvar, kōvar, kōvalar eṉpaṉa mulli nilattār peyarkaḷ.
kō (āṉ) mēyppatil vallavar kōvalaṉ āṉ vallōr eṉṟu tivākarattilum, āṉvallavar eṉṟu cūṭā maṇiyilum, kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
kaṇṇaki kaṇavaṉākiya kōvalaṉ peyarē kōpāla eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripākum. atilum kō eṉpatu teṉcollē.
kāṭṭumāṭukaḷip piṭippataṟkum vīṭṭumāṭukaḷi aṭakkutaṟ kum maṟavali vēṇṭiyiruntataṉāl, āyar tam makaḷirik kol lēṟu piṭittaṭakkiya iḷiñarkkē maṇañceytu koṭut taṉar. kollēṟaṭakkal ēṟutaḻuval eṉappaṭṭatu. āṭṭiṭi yar (pulliṉattāyar) eṉṟum māṭṭiṭiyar (nalliṉattāyar) eṉṟum, āyar iruvakiyarāyp pirintaṉar.
marutanila makkaḷ nilavaḷamum nīrvaḷamum mikka iṭaṅkaḷil taṅki, nel karumpu vāḻi mutaliya naṉceyp payirkaḷiyum cōḷam kēḻvaraku mutaliya puṉceyp payirkaḷiyum viḷittu, pañcāṭiyum paṭṭāṭiyum kampaḷiyāṭiyum aṇintu vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) eṉṉum vāṉavar kōṉi vaṇaṅki, maṇ ṇāluṅ kallāluñ ceṅkalālum kaṭṭiya maccu vīṭukaḷilum ōṭṭu mukaṭṭu vīṭukaḷilum kūri vīṭukaḷilum vatintu. nili yāka vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu maṭṭum ūr eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟatu.
ur-uṟu. uṟutal = poruntutal, nilattōṭu poruntutal, niliyākak kuṭiyiruttal. ur - uṟu. ur-ūr o.nō; uṇ ūṇ.
payirttoḻilil mutalviṉi uḻavātalāl, attoḻilum uḻaveṉappaṭṭatu. atic ceytavar uḻavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. pōraṭikkuṅ kaḷattil vēli ceyvatu paṟṟi kaḷamar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉar.
perunilakkiḻārellārum piṟarik koṇṭē viṉi ceytu, vīṭṭiliruntu niḻalil vāḻntataṉāl, caṟṟu veḷuttup pōy veḷḷāḷareṉṟum veṇkaḷamareṉṟum; ciṟu nilakkiḻārum nilamilikaḷum kaṭuveyililuṅ kāṭṭilum vēli ceytu karuttup pōṉatiṉāl, kārāḷareṉṟum karuṅkaḷamareṉṟum; collappaṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, uḻutuṇpār, uḻuvittuṇpār eṉa uḻavar iru vakuppārāyiṉar.
marutanila makkaḷ niliyāka vāḻntataṉāl, avarkaḷ kuṭiyirup pukkaḷ nāḷaṭiviṟ pērūrum mūturumāyiṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila nakarum nakaramum āyiṉa. nakaram mānakar.
uḻavarukkup pakkattuṇiyāka, neyavar, kuyavar, taccar, kol lar, paṟampar (tōlviṉiñar) mayirviṉiñar, vaṇṇār, cekkār mutaliya patiṉeṇ toḻilāḷarum, piṟa nuṇviṉiña rum; paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉar. nakarattil nākarikan tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu.
kaṭal nīrāl uppu viḷittaṟkum, āṟu kuḷaṅkaḷ nīr vaṟṟiyapiṉ kaṭal mīṉ piṭittaṟkum, muttuk kuḷittaṟkum, cila kūṭṭattār neytal nilattil vatintaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu, kuppam, kari, tuṟi, kāyal, kāṉal eṉap palvēṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa.
neytal nilattār, iṭattiṟkum karuvikkum toḻilukkum ēṟpa, kariyār eṉṟum, paṭavar - paravar eṉṟum, mukkuvar eṉṟum, aḷavar eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar. avar vaṇaṅkiyatu vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum kaṭal teyvam.
mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyum kaṭuṅkōṭik kālattiṟ cilaviṭaṅ kaḷil vaṟṟivaṟaṇṭu viḷiccaliṉṟip pañcanili ēṟpaṭṭa pōtu, pāliyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. attakiya camiyaṅkaḷil, an nila vāṇar vaḻippaṟikkavum akkam pakkaṅkaḷiṟ koḷḷi yaṭikkavum nērntatu. mūvēntarum avarit tam paṭi maṟavarāk kiyum, tām pakitta aracariṉ nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu ānirikaḷik kavarntu vara ēviyum, vantaṉar.
pālinilavāṇar, kaḷḷar, maṟavar, eyiṉar, vēṭṭuvar eṉap palavakuppiṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu, kuṭikkāṭu, nattam, paṟantali, cēri eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ teyvam kāḷi.
vaṇikam
marutanilap pērūrkaḷil, mutaṟkaṇ ovvoru toḻilāḷarum tattam viḷiporuṭkaḷiyum ceyporuṭkaḷiyum nelliṟkum piṟavaṟṟikum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytu vantaṉar.
piṉṉar ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum vāṅkiviṟkum vaṇikar tōṉṟiṉar. mutalil uḷḷūrp poruḷ viṟpaṉiyum, piṉṉar veḷiyūrp poruḷ viṟpaṉiyum, ataṉpiṉ veḷināṭṭup poruḷ viṟpaṉiyumāka, vaṇikam vaḷarntu vantatu. nila vāṇikattiṉ piṉ nīrvāṇikam tōṉṟiṟṟu. nilavāṇikar cāttuvar eṉṟum, nīrvāṇikar nāvikar (nāykar) eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar.
makkaḷ toki perukiyapiṉ kaḷavuṅ kavarvum mikuntataṉāl poruṭkāppiṟkum vaḻakkut tīrppiṟkum āṭcikkum kāvalaṉ ēṟpaṭṭāṉ. kuṭumpattalivaṉ nilimiyil toṭaṅkiya āṭcip patavi, ūrttalivaṉ, vēḷ (kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ), kō (perunila aracaṉ), vēntaṉ, māvēntaṉ (pēraracaṉ) eṉappaṭip paṭiyāka vuyarntatu. vēntaṉ ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ avaṉukkaṭaṅkiya ciṟṟaracarkku makuṭam aṇiyum urimi illātu pōyiṟṟu. iyalpākavē paṇṭamāṟṟiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa tiṇimayakkam, aintiṇi nilaṅkaḷiyum cērttāṇṭa vēntaṉ kālattil, mika virivaṭintatu. vēyntāṉ - vēyntaṉ - vēntaṉ = muṭiyaṇin tavaṉ vēytal = talimēlaṇital. iṅṅaṉam kuṟunila aracar, perunila aracar eṉa āḷvār iruvakiyarāyiṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉ cōḻaṉ cēraṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil mukkuṭi vēntar tōṉṟi, āṭcikku maruta nilattil oṉṟum, nīr vāṇikattiṟku neytal nilattil oṉṟumāka, iru mānakaramittu āṇṭaṉar. maruta nilittali nakarellām pērāṟṟaṅkari mēlēyē amin tiruntaṉa.
nākarikam vaḷarntu pātukāppum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, uḻuvit tuṇ ṇum uḻavarāṉa veḷḷāḷar tam oḻivu nēraṅkaḷiṟ kalviyi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. immikkuriya ulakiyaṟ kalvikkuppiṉ, maṟumikkuriya mataviyaṟ kalvi tōṉṟiṟṟu. ulaka vāḻkkiyil veṟuppuṟṟavar tuṟavi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. kaṟṟōruḷ illaṟattār nūlkaḷip pārppataṉāṟ pārppār allatu pārppaṉar eṉṟum, tuṟavaṟattār ellāvuyirkaḷiṭattum aruḷ pūṇṭamiyāl antaṇar eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, kaṟṟōr pārppārum antaṇarum eṉa irutiṟattārāyiṉar.
cilar pārppaṉar eṉṉum col pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripeṉṟum, tuṟavu nili pirāmaṇaṉukkē yuriya teṉṟum, pitaṟṟip pētiyari ēmāṟṟuvar. kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻar kālam āriyar eṉṉum iṉak karuvē tōṉṟāta kālam. pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟa pūṭṭaṉ maṇavāta iḷamik kālattil koṭpēraṉ eṅṅaṉam tōṉṟavilliyō, aṅṅaṉamē kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻaṉ kālattilum āriyaṉ tōṉṟavilli. ataṉāl avaṉ tirimoḻiyum tōṉṟavilli.
kaṇṇuṭiyar eṉpavar kaṟṟōr mukattiraṇṭu
puṇṇuṭiyar kallā tavar
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiṉiṉṟu (33), pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyark karaṇiyattiyum,
antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrman ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ.
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiṉiṉṟu (30), antaṇaṉ eṉṉum peyark karaṇiyattiyuṅ kaṇṭu koḷka.
cilar, pārppaṉaṉ eṉṉum colvaṭivu pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpatip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉpar. āṉ aṉaṉ eṉa iruvakiyīṟukaḷip peṟuvatu tamiḻviṉi muṟṟukkaḷiṉ iyalpē.
 e-ṭu:
 i. kā. 	ni. kā. 	e. kā.
 pārttaṉaṉ 	pārkkiṉṟaṉaṉ 	pārppaṉaṉ
 pārttaṉaḷ 	pārkkiṉṟaṉaḷ 	pārpapaṉaḷ
 pārttaṉar 	pārkkiṉṟaṉar 	pārppaṉar
 pārttaṉṟu 	pārkkiṉṟataṉṟu 	
 pārttaṉa 	pārkkiṉṟaṉa 	pārppaṉa
 aṉatu - aṉṟu (aṉ + tu). pārttaṉṟu = pārttatu.
marutanilattil, uḻavar, vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār, eṉṉum nālvakuppārum tōṉṟiyapiṉ. vēḷāṇmi ceytu viruntōmpuvatil veḷḷāḷarum kārāḷarumāṉa iruvakiyuḻa varum taliciṟantataṉāl, vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
tiṇimayakkam
maruta nilattuk kōnakarkaḷum talinakarkaḷum nakaraṅkaḷāka (mānakarkaḷāka) virivaṭintapōtu, mulli nilamum cila viṭattuk kuṟiñci nilamum paṭippaṭiyāka aṭuttu vantaṉa. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam pōṉṟa neytal nakaraṅkaḷil, marutamum mulliyuṅ kalantaṉa. cēranāṭṭu vañci pōṉṟa nakaraṅkaḷil aintiṇiyuṅ kalantaṉa.
neytal nakaraṅkaḷellām paṭṭiṉam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ pirivukaḷ pākkam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
maruta nilattūrkaṭku, mutaṟkaṇ, neytal nila māntar uppum mīṉum caṅkum pavaḷamum pōṉṟa kaṭalpaṭu poruḷkaḷiyum, mulli nilamāntar āṉintiyum, kuṟiñci nilamāntar tēṉum iṟicciyum maruppum (tantamum) kācaṟiyum (kastūriyum) pōṉṟa malipaṭu poruḷkaḷiyum, koṇarntu nelliṟkum āṭikkum māṟṟiṉar. piṉṉar, piḻippiṟkākac cilar nakar toṟum vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. pāli nila maṟavarum kāval toḻiṟkamarntaṉar.
tirunāḷ, perunāḷ, porunāḷ ākiya munnāṭkaḷilum, aittiṇi makkaḷum kalantaṉar. aṅṅaṉaṅ kalantu uriyāṭum pōtu, maruta vāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ ūr etuveṉṟum, mulli vāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ pāṭi (allatu cēri) etu veṉ ṟum, kuṟiñci vāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ kuṟicci etuveṉṟum, neytal vāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ kuppam (allatu tuṟi) etu veṉṟum, pāli vāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ kuṭikkāṭu etuveṉ ṟum, viṉavuvatē iyalpum vaḻakkamum. pērūrkaḷilum mūtūrkaḷi lum pala toḻilāḷarum nilattārum kalantu. tiṇimayakkam mikutiyāka ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, aintiṇik kuṭiyiruppum ūr eṉṉum potuppeyar peṟṟaṉa.
kulavakuppu
mutaṟkaṇ, nilantoṟum oru vakuppāka aintiṇik kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
piṉṉar maruta nilattil, uḻavarkkup pakkat tuṇiyākap patiṉeṇ kittoḻiṟ kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
ataṉpiṉ, vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār eṉṉum kulaṅkaḷ allatu kuṭikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. aracar eṉṟatu, vēntarum vēḷirum pōla vaḻivaḻiyāka vanta aracar kuṭumpaṅkaḷiyē.
kalvic ciṟappum oḻukka vuyarvum paṟṟip pārppārum cila viṭattu antaṇar eṉap peṟṟaṉar.
ovvoru kulamum nāḷaṭivil iraṇṭum palavumākak kiḷittup palkiṟṟu.
ellāk kulaṅkaḷum toḻilaṭippaṭiyilēyē tōṉṟi yiyaṅkiṉa. oruvaṉ ettoḻiliyum mēṟkoḷḷalām. ceyyun toḻil paṟṟiyē oruvaṉ kulam amintatu. āyiṉ, tanti yaṟivum āṟṟalum maṉappāṉmiyum iyalpākavē makaṉukku amintuviṭṭataṉālum, ovvoruvaṉum piḷḷip paruvatti lēyē peṟṟōṉip piṉpaṟṟi oru toḻiliṟ payiṟci peṟṟu viṭṭataṉālum, talimuṟi toṟum maraput toḻiṟṟiṟami vaḷarcciyaṭintu vantataṉālum, makkaḷ toki perukap peruka ellāt toḻilāḷarkkum piḻippu ēṟpaṭṭataṉā lum, perumpālum peṟṟōr toḻiliyē makkaḷ vaḻivaḻi ceytu varuvārāyiṉar.
ūrkkiḻavaṉ (ūraṉ), ūrāḷi, ūrkkuṭumpaṉ, ūrkkā miṇṭaṉ (kavuṇṭaṉ), nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ, nāṭaṉ, nāṭāṉ, nāṭṭāṉ, periyataṉakkāraṉ, ampalakāraṉ, cērvikāraṉ, mūppaṉ, maṉṟāṭi, paṇṇiyāṭi, mutali, talivaṉ, tēvaṉ eṉa iṭattiṟkum kulattiṟkum ēṟpap palapeyar peṟṟirunta ūrttalivaṉ patavi pōṉṟē, aracar patavikaḷum tol varavākat toṭarntu vantaṉa.
iyalpāṉa uṟavaṉpum poruḷutaviyum pātukāppum nōkki, potumakkaḷ perumpālum uṟaviṉarkkuḷḷēyē koḷ vaṉiyuṅ koṭuppaṉiyuñ ceytu vantaṉar. aracarkkō evvaki vilakkum taṭiyum iruntatilli. vēḷir makaḷiri yum maṟakkuṭi makaḷiriyum ayal nāṭṭaracar makaḷiriyum avar maṇantu vantaṉar.
ekkulattārāyiṉum, tuppuravum oḻukkamumē uṇṭāṭṭuṟa viṟkuk kavaṉikkappaṭṭaṉa. kalvi, oḻukkam, tavam, atikāram, celvam, īki, maṟam, āṟṟal eṉpavaṟṟā laṉṟip piṟappāṟ ciṟappilli.
atti, matti, ātaṉ, pūtaṉ, utiyaṉ, titiyaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, paṇṇaṉ, kāṭaṉ, kōṭaṉ, cāttaṉ, kūttaṉ, nākaṉ, pēkaṉ, naṉṉaṉ, poṉṉaṉ, nāṇaṉ, vāṇaṉ, vēntaṉ, cēntaṉ, naḷḷi, kiḷḷi, pāri, kāri, piṭṭaṉ, vaṭṭaṉ, tattaṉ, tittaṉ, mūlaṉ, vēlaṉ, iṟiyaṉ, poṟiyaṉ, mūvaṉ, tēvaṉ, koṟṟaṉ, veṟṟaṉ, marutaṉ, virutaṉ, cēraṉ, kīraṉ, vaṅkaṉ, koṅkaṉ, veḷḷiyaṉ, piḷḷiyaṉ, karuppaṉ, poruppaṉ, pacci, cecci, maliyaṉ, kaliyaṉ, māṟaṉ, vīṟaṉ, nallaṉ, cellaṉ, nampi, tampi, appaṉ, kuppaṉ, vēṭṭaṉ, cēṭṭaṉ mutaliyaṉa vākat taṉittum puṇarntum aṭiyaṭuttum varum peyarkaḷ ettoḻilāruṭiyaṉavāyiṉum, ikkālattu vaḻaṅkum kulappaṭṭamiṉṟi, ēṉi nāṭṭār peyarkaḷ pōṉṟē akkālattu vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
vaṇṇār, aṭuttōr (mayirviṉiñar) mutaliya ūrkkuṭi makkaḷ, paṟampar (tōlviṉiñar) pōṉṟē, orē vakuppārāy ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntaṉar.
mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ pōṉṟē, uṇavuvakiyiṉāl ēṟṟatāḻvillā tiruntatu.
ellāt tirukkōvilkaḷilum tamiḻ oṉṟē vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyāyiruntatu. uvaccar, kurukkaḷ, tirukkaḷ, pulavar, paṇṭāriyār, nampimār, pōṟṟimār, cāttuvār mutaliya tamiḻp pūcāriyarē, civaṉ tirumāl kāḷi eṉṉum mutteyvak kōvilkaḷilum vaḻipāṭu naṭatti vantaṉar. kōviṟ pukavurimi yum vaḻipāṭṭurimiyum ellā vakuppārkkuñ camamāyiruntatu. kōvili yaṭuttirunta tirukkuḷattil kuḷitta piṉ allatu kikāl kaḻuviya piṉ, ellārum kāṇikki yoṭu kōviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar.
tiruviḻākkaḷilum puṉalāṭṭu viḻākkaḷilum ellā vakup pārum kalantē koṇṭāṭiṉar. āṟukuḷaṅkaḷil vakuppiṟ kōriṭamāka variyaṟukkappaṭa villi.
iṭukāṭu ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntatu. piṇattic cuṭuvatu tamiḻar vaḻakkamaṉṟu.
vēḷāḷar, vaṇikar, aracar, antaṇar eṉpatē iyaṟki muṟi yāyiṉum, poruḷilakkaṇa nūlār, ākkavaḻippāṟṟal paṟṟi yum mukkāla aṟivupaṟṟiyum muḻuttūymi paṟṟiyum antaṇari muṉṉum, iṟivaṉ pōl ellāriyum muṟiceytu kāttal paṟṟi aracari iraṇṭāvatum, nāṭṭi vaḷampaṭuttiyum pōrkkālattil aracarkkup poruḷ koṭuttum utavum vaṇikari mūṉṟāvatum, vittu, antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉat talikīḻākac ceyaṟki muṟippaṭuttiṉar.
kumari nāṭṭu nākarikam
 nākarikam eṉpatu tiruntiya paḻakka vaḻakkam. atu mutaṉ mutal nakarattil allatu nakariyil tōṉṟiṉataṉāl nākarikam eṉappaṭṭatu.
o.nō : L. civis = citizen, civil = polite, civil - civilize - civilization = advanced stage in social development.
 L. urbis = city, urbane = courteous, urbanity = polished manners,
akanākarikam puṟanākarikam eṉa nākarikam iruvakippaṭum. aka nākarikamāvatu tiruntiya oḻukkam; puṟanākarikamāvatu tiruntiya ulaka vāḻkki. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu paṇpāṭu eṉṟum, piṉ ṉatu aṭiyiṉṟip potuvāka nākarikam eṉṟum, collap paṭum.
	nākarikam
uṟiyuḷ 
vēntarum kōkkaḷum māperuñcelvarum vatiyum nakaraṅ kaḷum, vēḷirum maṇṭilat talivarum vatiyum nakarkaḷum, kallālum ceṅkallālum cuṇṇāmpuk kāriyiṭṭukkaṭṭic cippi nīṟṟuc cutiyāl tīṟṟiya, māṭa māḷikikaḷum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum koṇṭu, māṭciyāṉa kāṭciyaḷittaṉa.
cippic cutiyiṉ veṇmic ciṟappi,
oḷiyārmuṉ oḷḷiya rātal veḷiyārmuṉ
vāṉcuti vaṇṇaṅ koḷal.
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷālum (714),
putuppiṟi yaṉṉa cuticey māṭattu
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟu aṭiyālum, (378),
varuṅkuṉṟa moṉṟurit tōṉtilli yampala vaṉmalayat
tiruṅkuṉṟa vāṇa riḷaṅkoṭi yēyiṭa reytalemmūrp
paruṅkuṉṟa māḷiki nuṇkaḷa pattoḷi pāyanummūrk
karuṅkuṉṟam veṇṇiṟak kañcukam ēykkum kaṉaṅkuḻiyē.
eṉṉun tirukkōvic ceyyuḷālum (15), aṟiyalām.
māṭaṅkaḷilellām kaṇkavar vaṇṇa vōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunili maṉitoṟum
miyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
evvaki yuyirkaḷum uvamaṅ kāṭṭi
veṇcuti viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya
kaṇkavar ōviyam.
 eṉṟu maṇimēkaliyum (3: 127-131)
māṭakkuc cittiramum eṉṟu naṉṉūṟpāyiramum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
nakarantoṟum vāṉaḷāvum eḻunilik kōpuramum kaṭṭap paṭṭiruntatu. mutaṟkaṇ aracarvatiyum araṇmaṉik kāvaṟ kōpuramāka irunta kaṭṭiṭam, piṟkālattil vāṉaḷāvun tirukkōviṟ kōpuramāka vaḷarntatu.
kō = aracaṉ, puram = uyarnta kaṭṭiṭam.
puriyuyar vākum.  (tol, uri. 4)
kōnakarun talinakarum kōṭṭi matilāluñ cūḻappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. kōṭṭiyuḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ puri eṉṟum, kōpura muḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ puram eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
purital = vaḷital. puri = vaḷinta (cūḻnta) kōṭṭiyuḷḷatu. puri - purici = kōṭṭi matil.
matil, eyil, iñci, cō eṉa matilaraṇ nālvakippaṭ ṭiruntatu. mika uyaramāṉatu matil; muṟṟukiyiṭum pakivar mītu ampeyyum ēvaṟikaḷiyuṭiyatu eyil; cempi yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiyiṟukiyatu iñci; ariya kaṭum poṟikaḷiyuṭiyatu cō.
kōnakarkaḷil aṅkaṇa nīrik kaṇṇiṟ paṭāmaṟ pōkku vataṟku, karantupaṭiyeṉṉum puticālakam iruntatu. atu maṟuku (perunteru) naṭuviṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu yāṉikkūṭṭam mēṟ cellumpaṭi, karuṅkallāl mūṭappaṭṭi runtatu. atiṟ ceṉṟanīr yāṉit tutikki pōṉṟa tūmpiṉ vāyilāy akaḻiyil viḻuntatu.
aracar kuṭumpattuṭaṉ pōrkkālattil tappik koḷvataṟku, araṇmaṉi yiṉ kīḻum kōnakarkaḷiṭiyum, curaṅkap pātikaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
āṭi
pañcu, paṭṭu, mayir ākiya mūvakik karuvipporuḷālum iḻikkappaṭṭa nuṇṇūliṉāl, pūvitaḻ pōṉṟa meṉmi-yum, iḻi pirittaṟiyāc ceṟivum, pāmpuccaṭṭiyum mūṅkiṟ coliyum nīrāviyum naṟumpukiyum otta naṉitava noymmi-yum, kaṇkavar palvaṇṇap pūttoḻilum, uṭiya palvaki āṭikaḷ aṟuvakip paruvattiṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu neyyap paṭṭaṉa.
mayir eṉṟatu, āṭṭumayiriyum oruvaki elimayiriyum.
ūṇ
ulakil mutaṉmutal uṇavi nākarikamāyc camittuṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
uṇṇal tiṉṉal nakkal parukal eṉṉum nālvaki ūṇ viṉiyum oruṅkē cērntatu cāppiṭutal.
uṇavic cōṟum kuḻampum kaṟiyum eṉa mūṉṟākap pakuttu, nellariciyic cōṟākac camittu, kuḻampiyum kaṟiyiyum kaṇṇiṟku aḻakum mūkkiṟku naṟumaṇamum nāviṟku iṉcuvi yum uṇarttuvaṉavum, uṭampi valuppaṭuttuvaṉavum, nōy varātu taṭuttu vāḻnāḷi nīṭṭippaṉavumāṉa palavaki maruntuc carakkukaḷic cērttu ākki, mutaliṟ kuḻampum iṭiyil miḷaku nīrum iṟutiyil tayir allatu mōrum ūṟṟi, iyaṟkiyāṉa melliya vāḻiyiliyil aṟucuviyuṇṭi toṉṟutoṭṭu iṉpamākac cāppiṭṭu varupavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
arici vakiyil, nellarici tali; varakutiṉi cāmi kutiri vāli kāṭik kaṇṇi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ arici iṭi; kañci kaḷi kūḻukku maṭṭum utavum cōḷam kampu kēḻvaraku kaṭi.
nellariciyil cīrakaccampā ciṟumaṇiccampā eṉpavi tali piṟa campā iṭi; maṭṭiyarici kaṭi.
cōḷam kampu kēḻvarakuṇṇum pāṭṭāḷi makkaḷum cuvi yāṉa kuḻampum toṭukaṟiyum virumpuvar.
kuḻampiṟ kutavum payiṟṟu vakikaḷuḷ, tuvari tali; avari mocci pacci iṭi; uḻuntu taṭṭāṉ kal karampi koḷ (kāṇam) iṭi.
(miḷaku) nīr, nīṭṭāṇam (soup), cāṟu, kuḻampu kūṭṭu eṉpaṉa kuḻampu vakiyil muṟiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tiṇṇiyavi.
palvakik kāykaḷum mīṉum iṟicciyum, vāṭṭal, vatakkal, vaṟuval, poriyal, tuvaṭṭal, puraṭṭal, aviyal, oṭiyal eṉap palvēṟu muṟiyil kaṟiyākkappaṭṭaṉa.
kuḻampiṟkum kaṟikkum iṟutiviṉi tāḷippu (ucilippu). ataṟku eṇṇeyum āneyyum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
iṟicci vakiyil, muḻuvuṭumpu, mukkāl kāṭi, ari kōḻi, kāl āṭu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
aṇi
poṉṉum muttum maṇiyum kumarināṭṭil ērāḷamāyk kiṭittaṉa. nuṇṇiya ōviya vēlippāṭuḷḷa aṇikaḷum kalaṅkaḷum taṭṭumuṭṭukkaḷum uruvaṅkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
kittoḻil
ellāt toḻilkaḷiyuñ cevviyākac ceytaṟku, irumpi ṉālum cempiṉālum veṇkalattiṉālum ciṟanta karuvikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. irumpu mutaṉmutal tamiḻakattilēyē kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku, ataṉ tamiḻp peyar āriya moḻi kaḷil vaḻaṅkuvatē cāṉṟām.
E. iron, OE. iren, isen, Ger. eisen, Skt. ayas.
makkaṭ karuttukkaḷ :
ovvoruvarum pāl vakuppu vēṟupāṭiṉṟi aṟam poru ḷiṉpam vīṭu aṭital vēṇṭum. entavūrum contavūr. ellārum ōriṉam. aṟivāṟṟal kuṇañceyalkaḷālaṉṟip piṟappāṟciṟappilli. oruvaṉukkup perumiyum ciṟumiyum taṉṉālēyē varum. iṉpamum tuṉpamum varutaṟku avaravar paḻa viṉiyē kāraṇam; piṟarallar. piṟantaviyellām iṟakkum. ataṟku añcal kūṭātu. atu oru nilimiyiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru nilimikkup pukuvāyilē. ovvoruvarkkum vāḻkkiyil oru kaṭamiyuṇṭu. atu niṟivēṟiyapiṉ iṟappu varum.
uṭaluḻippiṟkum maṉavuḻippiṟkum uyar patavikkum tāḻ patavikkum ēṟṟavāṟu, kuṇaṅkaḷum tiṟamikaḷum piṟappilēyē amintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, periyōrip pukaḻtalum ciṟiyōri ikaḻtalum takkataṉṟu. ovvoruvarum tammip pōṟ piṟarik karututal vēṇṭum. iṟantapiṉ uṭaṉ varuvaṉa avaravar ceyta iruvaki viṉikaḷē.
araciyal
aracar tammi uyirākavum tam kuṭikaḷi uṭampākavum karuti, aimperuṅkuḻu, eṇpērāyam, aivaki uṟutic, cuṟṟam, nālvakip paṭi, nālvakiyaraṇ ākiyavaṟṟit tuṇik koṇṭu, naṭuniliyāka muṟiceytu ceṅkōlāṭci ceytu vantaṉar.
potukkalvi
ikkālattiṟpōl araciyaluṟuppāṉa potukkalvit tiṇik kaḷam akkālattil illiyēṉum, ēṟattāḻa ellārum tamiḻil eḻutap paṭikkat terintavarāyiruntaṉar. pāṭṭāḷiyuṭ paṭap pala toḻilārum pāvalarākavumiruntaṉar.
ceyyuḷ
akkālat tilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷākavēyiruntatu. atu veṇpā, āciriyappā (akavaṟpā), kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum nālvakip pākkaḷāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ceyyuḷ nūliṟkup poruḷ kūṟum uriyum eḷiya ceyyuḷākavēyiruntatu veṇpāviṟkum kalippāviṟkum iṇiyāṉa yāppuvaki vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇaviyalātu.
māttiri, eḻuttu, aci, cīr, aṭi, yāppu, marapu, tūkku, toṭi, nōkku, pā, aḷavu, tiṇi, kikōḷ, kūṟṟu, kēṭpōr, kaḷaṉ, kālam, payaṉ, meyppāṭu, eccam, muṉṉam, poruḷ, tuṟi, māṭṭu, vaṇṇam eṉac ceyyuḷuṟuppukkaḷ irupattāṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
	aṟiviyal
ilakkaṇam
ettuṇic ciṟantaṉavēṉum, ēṉi yuyartaṉic cemmoḻikaḷi lellām, eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉa moḻiyilak kaṇam nālvakippaṭṭatē. tamiḻilakkaṇam maṭṭum, ulakil mutaṉmutal iyaṟṟappaṭṭatēṉum, coṟṟoṭarum ceyyuḷum nūlum kūṟum poruḷukkum ilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭatākum.
akam, puṟam eṉap poruḷ irupāṟpaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ov voṉṟum evvēḻu tiṇikaḷik koṇṭatu. potuvāka akam kātal vāḻkkiyiyum, puṟam pōrviṉiyiyum, kūṟum. ivi yiraṇṭumallāta ellāp poruḷkaḷum, vāki yeṉṉum puṟattiṇikkuḷ aṭakkappaṭum.
kaṇakku
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar kaṇakkil mikat tērcci peṟṟiruntaṉar.
 oṉṟiliruntu mēṟpaṭṭatu mēlvāyilakkam eṉṟum, oṉṟiṟku kīḻppaṭṭatu kīḻvāyilakkam eṉṟum, collappaṭṭaṉa.
kumpam = nūṟu kōṭi
caṅkam (caṅku) = ilakkaṅ kōṭi
tāmari = kōṭā kōṭi
vāraṇam = nūṟu kōṭā kōṭi (kōṭā kōṭi?)
ivaṟṟiṉ matippu vēṟuvakiyākavuñ collappaṭum. kuvaḷi, veḷḷam, āmpal, neytal eṉpaṉavum aṭukkiya kōṭi kaḷik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷākum. kīḻvāyilakkam mukkāl, ari, kāl, arikkāl, mākāṇi (vīcam), mā(1/20), kāṇi (1/80), muntiri (1/320), kīḻmuntiri (muntiriyil 1/320) eṉpaṉa.
ciṟṟilakkattiṟkum pērilakkattiṟkum perukkal vāypāṭirun tatu pōṉṟē, catura vāypāṭum iruntatu. atu kuḻikkaṇakku eṉappaṭṭatu. ciṟṟilakkak kuḻippu ciṟukuḻi yeṉṟum, pērilakkak kuḻippu peruṅkuḻi yeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kaṇiyam
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻak kaṇiyar nuḻi matiyaruṅ kūrṅ kaṇṇarumā yiruntataṉāl, puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟali, mūtiri, kaḻi, koṭiṟu, aravu, koṭunukam, kaṇi, uttaram, ki, aṟuvi, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉi, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, muṟkuḷam, kaṭikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākki, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉṉum irupatteḻu nāṭkaḷiyum; mēḻam, viṭi, āṭavi, alavaṉ, maṭaṅkal, kaṉṉi, tuli, naḷi, cili, cuṟavam, kumpam, mīṉam, eṉṉum paṉṉīrōrikaḷiyum; ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy, aṟivaṉ (putaṉ), viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, kāri (caṉi) eṉṉum ēḻu kōḷkaḷiyum, kaṇṭu, kaṇiya nūli vaḷarttu vantaṉar.
eḻukōḷkaḷiṉ peyarāl eḻunāṭkiḻami (vāram) vakukkap paṭṭatu. atu ulaka muḻutum paravi iṉṟuvari vaḻaṅki varu kiṉṟatu.
kiḻamip peyarkaḷ camaṟkirutattilum, ōrip peyarkaḷ camaṟkirutattilum ilattīṉilum, moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭuḷa.
iṉṉici
mural (curam), paṇ, meṭṭu, tāḷam eṉṉum nāṟkūṟu koṇṭa iṉṉici muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭintiruntatu.
paṇ (eḻumural), paṇṇiyal (aṟumural), tiṟam (aimmural) tiṟat tiṟam (nāṉmural) eṉa nālvakippaṭṭa paṇkaḷ narappaṭi vāl 11,991 ākak kaṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āyappāli, vaṭṭap pāli, caturappāli, mukkōṇappāli (tirikōṇappāli) eṉṉum nālvaki muṟiyil, eḻuperumpālikaḷum avaṟ ṟiṉ kiḷikaḷum tirikkappaṭṭaṉa. attirivu muṟikaḷ, muṟiyē, muḻumural, arimural, kālmural, arikkāl mural ākiya mural nilikaḷit taḻuviyaṉa eṉpar. innuṭpaṅkaḷ iṟṟi icivāṇarkkut teriyāvāṟu, āriyattāl maṟiyuṇṭu pōyiṉa.
tōl tuḷi narampu uṟi (kañcam) eṉṉum nālvaki icik karuvikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu narappuk karuvi. narappuk karuvikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu yāḻ eṉṉum vīṇi. yāḻkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ. ataṉ vaḻiyiṉatē iṟṟi vīṇi. viṇṇeṉal = narampu teṟittal. viṇ - vīṇi. tōliṟ ciṟantatu mattaḷam (periyatu) allatu mataṅkam (mirutaṅkam). tuḷiyiṟ ciṟantatu pullāṅkuḻal.
nāṭakam
iciyoṭu naṭamum naṭippum cērntatu nāṭakam. naṭi + akam = nāṭakam.
naṭam eṉpatu, kati taḻuviyatum taḻuvātatum eṉa iruvakittu. nāṭakam eṉpatu, potuvāka ulaka vaḻakkiṟ kati taḻuvi varum naṭippiyē kuṟikkum. kūttu eṉṉum peyar naṭattiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum potu. naṭam (naṭaṉam) eṉpatu āṭal eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
āṭalkaḷ, nilittaṉavum avvappōtu āṭalāciriyaṉāṟ putituputitāyp payiṟṟappaṭuvaṉavum eṉa irupāṟpaṭum. nāṭaka mellām putituputitāyt tōṉṟuvaṉavē.
āṭalkaḷ, vēttiyal, potuviyal, tēviyal eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. kaṭiyam, marakkāl, kuṭi, tuṭi, alliyam, mal, kuṭam, pēṭu, pāvi, pāṇṭaraṅkam, koṭṭi eṉṉum patiṉōrāṭalum, piṟkālattil teyvaṅkaḷ āṭiṉavākak katikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. kuravi, vari eṉpaṉavum tēviyaliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
naṭak karaṇaṅkaḷ aṟupattu nāṉku. naṭaviṉik ki, oṟṟik ki (piṇṭikki) iṇikki eṉa iruvakippaṭum. oṟṟik ki muppattumūṉṟu; iṇikki patiṉintu.
naṭippiṟkuriya naḷinayam (apinaya) muppattiraṇṭu.
ceyyuṭkum iṉṉicikkum nāṭakattiṟkum uriya cuvikaḷ; naki, aḻuki, iḷivaral, maruṭki, accam, perumitam, vekuḷi, uvaki eṉa eṭṭu.
iṉṉiciyilum nāṭakattilum kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar, āḻntu īṭupaṭṭum ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟum nirampa iṉpuṟṟum vantata ṉāl, iṉṉici nāṭakattiyum tamiḻoṭu cērttu, iyaṟ ṟamiḻ, icittamiḻ, nāṭakattamiḻ eṉa mukkūṟṟatākki muttamiḻ eṉṟaṉar. ittakip puṇarppu vēṟenta nāṭṭilumilli.
maruttuvam
ūti pittuk kōḻi yeṉṉum munnāṭiyiyalpi, teyvat taṉmiyāṉa nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattā laṟintu, ellā nōy kaṭkum maruttuvam aṟuvi eṉṉum iruvakip paṇṭuvam ceyvatu tamiḻ maruttuvam. ataṉ taliciṟanta maruntu irumpu cempu mutaliya poṉṉaṅkaḷit tūya poṉṉākkuvatum, kaḻi neṭuṅkālam (1,00,000 āṇṭu?) vāḻac ceyvatumāṉa muppu (mūvuppu) eṉṉum oruvakik kalaviyuppu. atu ceyyum vaki maṟiyuṇṭu pōyiṟṟu.
mataviyal
kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya cēyōṉ (murukaṉ) vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu civaṉiyamum, mulli nilattiṟkuriya māyōṉ (kariyavaṉ, māl) vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu māliyamum, ākiya peruntēva matam iraṇṭum vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa.
ōkappayiṟciyālum uḷḷattūymiyālum eṇṇattiṇmi yālum iṟippaṟṟālum, nuṇmi, parumi, noymmi, kaṉati, karutiyatu peṟutal, karutiyaviṭañ cellutal karutiya vaṭi veṭuttal, kūṭuviṭṭuk kūṭupāytal ākiya eṇvakip peṟṟi kaḷip peṟṟa peṟṟiyar (cittar), iru peruntēva matattiṟkum potuvāṉa kaṭavuḷ matattik kaṇṭaṉar. kaṭavuḷ matamāvatu, kālam iṭam vaṭivu kuṇam mutaliya ellā vaṟṟiyuṅ kaṭantu iyalpāṉa muṟṟaṟivum muḻuvallamiyuḷḷa orutaṉip param poruḷi, uḷḷattil toḻutal.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar iṅṅaṉam ulakiyalilum mataviyalilum uyarniliyaṭintiruntataṟkuk karaṇiyam, avar neṟṟiyiṟ peṟṟirunta oruvaki aṟivukkaṇ eṉṟum, atu kaṇṇāṟ kāṇātatik kāṇavum kātāṟ kēḷātatik kēṭkavum vallatu eṉṟum, atip piṉṉōr neṭuṅkālam payaṉpaṭuttāmi yāl ati iḻantuviṭṭaṉar eṉṟum, ārāycciyāḷar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ikkarutti, civaperumāṉukku neṟṟik kaṇ uṇṭeṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu ōraḷavu valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
	paṇpāṭu
1. vēḷāḷar paṇpāṭu
putitāka illattiṟku vantavarkkum vaḻic cellum ayalārk kum ciṟanta vuṇavaḷippatum, irappōrkku illiyeṉṉātu īvatum, vēḷāḷar paṇpāṭu.
2. vaṇikar paṇpāṭu
poruḷkaḷi vāṅkumpōtu aḷavik kūṭṭip peṟāmalum, viṟkumpōtu kuṟittuk koṭāmalum, nērmiyāka vaṇikañ ceyvatum; uṟuppilikaṭku ūṭṭuppuriyum, ayalār taṅkac cattirañ cāvaṭiyum, kaṭṭi vippatum; vaḻippōkkarkkuc cōliyoṭu kūṭiya kuḷam veṭṭivippatum; nīr nili yillāk kāṭṭu vaḻikaḷil nelli maraṅkaḷ vaḷarttu vippa tum; vaṇikar paṇpāṭu.
aracar paṇpāṭu
kāṭcika keḷiyāriyum kaṭuñ collarallarāyu miruppatum, naṭuniliyāka muṟiceytu ceṅkōlāṭci celuttuvatum, vaḻakkiḻantavar tammiṭam muṟiyiṭa araṇmaṉi vāyilil ārāyccimaṇi kaṭṭivippatum, vaṟaṭcik kālattil varinīkkuva tum, tirunāḷ perunāṭkaḷil ciṟiñari viṭutali ceyvatum, matampaṟṟiya uṇmiyi makkaḷaṟitaṟkup paṭṭimaṉṟam naṭattu vatum, kūṉar kuṟaḷar mutaliya eccap piṟavikaḷi araṇmaṉik kuṟṟēvaṟ kamarttuvatum, pōr toṭaṅkumuṉ peṇṭir piḷḷikaḷ piṇiyāḷar mutiyōr mutaliyōrip pātukāp pāṉa iṭattiṟku akaṟṟi viṭuvatum paṭikkalamiḻantavaṉum eḷiya paṭikkalamuḷḷavaṉum kīḻē viḻuntavaṉum muṭikulinta vaṉum āṭiyaviḻntavaṉum tōṟṟōṭukiṉṟavaṉum ākiyōr mītu paṭikkalam ēvāmi yum, tōṟṟa aracaṉit tiṟikaṭṭac ceytu avaṉoṭu maṇavuṟavu koḷvatum, pōril mutukiṟ puṇpaṭṭa pōtum vāḻkkiyil māṉaṅkeṭa nērntapōtum uṇṇā nōṉpiruntu uyirtuṟappatum, aracar paṇpāṭām.
pulavar paṇpāṭu
aracar matiyātu tarum paricilip peṟātu pōvatum, vaṟumi yiṟ cemmiyāy oḻukutalum, pērāciyiṉmiyum, aracar tīṅkuceyyiṉ taṭuppatum, avarkku añcātu aṟivuri kūṟuva tum potunalam pēṇuvatum, coṟpoḻivaraṅkiṟkuc cel liṉ iṟutivariyiliruntu kēṭpatum pulavar paṇpāṭām.
akumarināṭṭu ilakkiyam
ilakkiyam (1) eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyam, (2) eḻutappaṭā ilak kiyam eṉa iruvakippaṭum. eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyamum, (1) uri naṭiyilakkiyam (2) ceyyuḷilakkiyam eṉṟum, (1) potu vilakkiyam (2) ciṟappilakkiyam eṉṟum, naṭi paṟṟiyum payaṉ paṭuttum makkaḷ paṟṟiyum ivviruvakippaṭum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻilakkiyam, naṭipaṟṟic ceyyuḷilakkiyamum payaṉpaṭuttuvār paṟṟip potucciṟap pilakkiyamumākum.
potuvilakkiyam
pāṭṭu, uri nūl, vāymoḻi allatu mantiram, pici aṅkatam, mutucol eṉṉum eḻuvaki nilikkaḷattiṟ ceyyuḷilakkiyam tōṉṟiṟṟu. nilikkaḷam yāppu vaki,
avaṟṟuḷ : pāṭṭu eṇvaki vaṉappākap pakukkappaṭṭatu.
eṇvaki vaṉappukkaḷ
 1. ammi
ciṉmeṉ moḻiyāṟ cīrpuṉin tiyāppiṉ
ammi tāṉē aṭinimir piṉṟē.  (tol. cey. 233)
 2. aḻaku
ceyyuḷ moḻiyāṟ cīrpuṉin tiyāppiṉ
avvaki tāṉē aḻakeṉap paṭumē.  (tol.cey. 234)
 3. toṉmi
toṉmi tāṉē
 uriyōṭu puṇarnta paḻimi mēṟṟē  (tol.cey. . 235)
4. tōl
iḻumeṉ moḻiyāṉ viḻumiyatu nuvaliṉum
paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭinimirn toḻukiṉum
tōleṉa moḻi toṉmoḻip pulavar.  (tol.cey. 236)
5. viruntu
viruntē tāṉum
putuvatu kiḷanta yāppiṉ mēṟṟē  (tol.cey. 237)
6. iyipu
ñakāra mutalā ṉakāra vīṟṟup
puḷḷi yiṟuti iyipeṉap paṭumē.  (tol.cey. 238)
7. pulaṉ
 cēri moḻiyāṟ cevvitiṟ kiḷantu
 tērtal vēṇṭātu kuṟittatu tōṉṟiṉ
 pulaṉeṉa moḻipa pulaṉuṇarn tōrē.  (tol.cey. 239)
8. iḻipu
oṟṟoṭu puṇarnta valleḻut taṭakkātu
kuṟaḷaṭi mutalā aintaṭi yoppit
tōṅkiya moḻiyāṉ āṅṅaṉam oḻukiṉ
 iḻipiṉ ilakkaṇam iyinta tākum.  (tol.cey. 240)
ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kūṭa ikkālattilli. cilappatikāramum maṇi mēkaliyum ṉakaramayyīṟṟup pāṭṭukkaḷik koṇṭiruppa tāl, iyipu eṉṉum vaṉappiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. āyiṉ, avi tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭavi yalla. tolkāp piyak kālam ki.mu. 6-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu; cilappatikāra maṇimēkalik kālamō ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa āriyamallāta ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷum moḻic ceytikaḷum, mutaliru kaḻakak kālat tiṟkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟit tolkāppiyar kālattiṟ kuriyaṉavalla.
muntu nūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇip
pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval.
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉārum, eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar, nuṇ ṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟē, eṉamoḻipa otta teṉpa vuṇaru mōrē, celvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē eṉṟum, piṟa vāṟum, cārpu nūlāciriyar muṟiyil tolkāppiyaṉārum, kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
iyipu
vaṉappup pāṭṭukkaḷ ña, ṇ, n, m, ṉ eṉṉum aim melliṉa meykaḷuḷ oṉṟil iṟum. cilappatikāra maṇimēkaliyākiya iraṭṭi vaṉappup pāṭṭukkaḷ ṉ eṉṉum orē meyyil iṟukiṉṟaṉa. oru meykku oruvaṉappu eṉṟu vittuk koḷḷi ṉum, aimmeykkum aivaṉappāvatu iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. oṉṟukūṭa iṉṟilli. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi vaṉappukkaḷum.
viruntu
eṉpatu, avvappōtu putituputitākat tōṉṟum palvakip paṉuvalkaṭṭum potuppeyar. tolkāppiyattil viruntup paṉuvaleṉṟu oṉṟum vitantu kuṟikkappeṟavilli.
kuḻavi maruṅkiṉuṅ kiḻava tākum.
eṉpatu (tol. puṟat. 29) piḷḷittamiḻ eṉṉum paṉuvaliyum,
ūroṭu tōṟṟamum uritteṉa moḻipa.
eṉpatu (tol.cey. 30) ulā allatu ūriṉṉiciyiyum kuṟikkalām.
uri eṉpatu, viḷaṅkāta nūṉ mūlattiṟkup poruḷ kūṟuvatu. atuvuñ ceyyuḷākavē iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu; āyiṉ, alliyara cāṇimāli pavaḷak koṭimāli eṉpaṉa pōla eḷiya naṭiyil amintiruntatu. urinaṭiyil irukkavēṇṭiya uriyum ceyyuḷil iyaṉṟami, akkālattut tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ ceyyuḷ tiṟattic ciṟappak kāṭṭum.
nūl eṉpatu aṟiviyal. ilakkaṇam ōr aṟiviyal. ovvoru kaliyilum teriviyal (Theory) paṟṟiya tellām ilakkaṇam pōṉṟa aṟiviyalē.
vāymoḻi allatu mantiram eṉpatu tirumūlar tirumantiram pōlvatu.
pici yeṉpatu viṭukati
aṅkatam eṉpatu kaṇṭaṉanūl allatu maṟuppunūl. atu etirnūl eṉavumpaṭum.
mutucol eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
pāṭṭuppōṉṟē, ēṉiyaṟuvaki yāppuc ceyyuḷum iṉ ṟilli paṇṇatti yeṉṉum oruvaki yāppum iṉṟilli. eḻuvaki yāppum veṇpā, akavaṟpā (āciriyappā) kalippā, vañcippā, eṉṉum tūya pākkaḷum, maruṭpā, paripāṭal eṉṉum kalavip pākkaḷum ākiya aṟuvakip pākkaḷāl iyaṉṟaṉa.
pākkaṭkuriya vaṇṇaṅkaḷ : pāa vaṇṇam, tāa vaṇṇam, vallici vaṇṇam, mellici vaṇṇam, iyipu vaṇṇam, aḷapeṭi vaṇṇam, neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam, kuṟucīr vaṇṇam, cittira vaṇṇam, nalipu vaṇṇam, akappāṭṭu vaṇṇam, puṟap pāṭṭu vaṇṇam, oḻukuvaṇṇam, orūu vaṇṇam, eṇṇu vaṇṇam, akippuvaṇṇam, tūṅkal vaṇṇam, ēntal vaṇṇam, uruṭṭu vaṇṇam, muṭuku vaṇṇam eṉa irupatu.
vaṇṇam, cittiram eṉpaṉa teṉcoṟkaḷē.
vaḷḷutal = vaḷittal, vaḷitteḻutal. vaḷ-vaṇ-vaṇṇam= eḻutum eḻuttu allatu variyum paṭam, eḻuttiṉ niṟam, niṟam pōṉṟa ceyyuḷoli vaki, vaki.
vaḷ-var-vari - varaṇam = eḻuttu, niṟam, vaki, vakuppu, icippāvaki.
o.nō : tiḷ-tiṇ-tiṇṇi. tiḷ-tir-tiraḷ - tiraḷi - tiraṇi.
cettal = ottal. ce + tiram = cettiram - cittiram = ōviyam.
o.nō : centiram - cintiram = civappu, centūḷ, cennīṟu, cempoṟimuka yāṉi.
	ciṟappilakkiyam
iciyum nāṭakamum
tolkāppiyattiṟku muntiyatu akattiyam. atu muttamiḻilak kaṇam
ilakkiya miṉṟi ilakkaṇa miṉṟē
eḷḷiṉ ṟākil eṇṇeyu miṉṟē
eḷḷiṉiṉ ṟeṇṇe yeṭuppatu pōla
ilakki yattiṉiṉ ṟeṭupaṭum ilakkaṇam.  (akat.)
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampaliṉ.  (naṉ. 141)
eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.
akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇam eṉpatāl, iyaṟṟamiḻilakkiyam pōṉṟē icittamiḻ nāṭakattamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷum avvilak kaṇa nūṟku muṉpirut tiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. akattiyam tolkāppiyattiṟkuc cilapala nūṟṟāṇṭu muntiya tēṉum, iṭikkaḻakat tiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa iciyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.  (tol. eḻut. 33)
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puḷpaṟi
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii
avvaki piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa.  (tol. akat. 18)
 eṉpavaṟṟāl iṉṉiciyilakkiyamum,
nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam.  (tol. akat. 53)
eṉpataṉāl nāṭakavilakkiyamum, kumarināṭṭiliruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
kaṇakku
ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti
alpeyar eṇṇiṉum āyiyal niliyum.  (puḷḷi. 98)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil 	
kuvaḷi, tāmari, caṅkam, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉṉum mā pēreṇkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
caṅkam = ilakkaṅkōṭi
tāmari = kōṭā kōṭi
iraṇṭumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (kuṟṟiya. 76) mā eṉṉum aḷavik kuṟippatāl, kīḻvāyilakkattic cērnta ēṉiyaḷavu kaḷum tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpu vaḻaṅkiṉa veṉṟē koḷḷappaṭum.
kaṇiyam
tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa.  (tol. uyir. 84)
ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ colleṉa varūum.  (tol. kiḷavi. 58)
maṟinta voḻukkattu ōriyum nāḷum.  (tol. kaḷa. 44)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya aṭikaḷ kaṇiyak kuṟippik koṇṭaṉa.
maruttuvam
potiya maliyil vatinta akattiyar oru maruttuva nūlāciriyar eṉṟu collappaṭuvataṉālum, peṟṟiya (citta) maruttuvam toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻa maruttuvamātalālum, oru cila maruntup peyarkaḷ akattiyar peyari aṭiyākap peṟṟiruppataṉālum,
vāyuṟi vāḻttē vayaṅka nāṭiṉ
vēmpuṅ kaṭuvum pōla veñcol
tāṅkuta liṉṟi vaḻinaṉi payakkumeṉ
ṟōmpaṭik kiḷaviyiṉ vāyuṟut taṟṟē.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviluḷḷa (cey. 111) maruttuvak kuṟippiṉālum, kumarināṭṭil maruttuva ilakkiya miruntami uyttuṇarappaṭum.
moḻinūl
centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum moḻip pakuppum iyaṟcol tiricol eṉṉum centamiḻc coṟpakuppum,
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi.
eṉṉum (tol. ec. 4) koṭuntamiḻc col viḷakkamum valittal melittal virittal tokuttal nīṭṭal kuṟukkal eṉṉum coṟṟirivu muṟikaḷum, moḻinūliṉ karuniliyē yāyiṉum, ulakil mutaṉmutaṟ collappaṭṭataṉāl, moḻi nūliṉ toṭakkamēyām.
uḷanūl
eḷḷal iḷami pētimi maṭaṉeṉ
ṟuḷḷap paṭṭa nakināṉ keṉpa.  (tol. meyp. 4)
iḷivē iḻavē acivē vaṟumiyeṉa
viḷivil koḷki aḻuki nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.5)
mūppē piṇiyē varuttam meṉmiyōṭu
yāppuṟa vanta iḷivaral nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.6)
putumi perumi ciṟumi ākkamoṭu
matimi cālā maruṭki nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.7)
aṇaṅkē vilaṅkē kaḷvartam iṟiyeṉap
piṇaṅkal cālā accam nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.8)
kalvi taṟukaṇ icimi koṭiyeṉac
collap paṭṭa perumitam nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.9)
uṟuppaṟi kuṭikōḷ alikoli yeṉṟa
veṟuppa vanta vekuḷi nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.10)
celvam pulaṉē puṇarvuviḷi yāṭṭeṉ
 ṟallal nītta uvaki nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.11)
iviyum piṟa meyppāṭṭiyal nūṟpākkaḷum uḷanūṟ karuvām ēraṇam (logic)
